





|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

aṅguttaranikāyau

catuk‍kanipātapāḷi


1. paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ


1. bhaṇḍagāmavaggau

1. anubuddhasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaj‍jīsu viharati bhaṇḍagāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ  mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? ariyassa, bhikkhavai, sīlassa ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| ariyassa, bhikkhavai, samādhissa ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| ariyāya, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāya ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| ariyāya, bhikkhavai, vimuttiyā ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca| tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyaṃ sīlaṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, ariyau samādhi anubuddhau paṭividdhau, ariyā pañ‍ñā anubuddhā paṭividdhā, ariyā vimutti anubuddhā paṭividdhā, ucchin‍nā bhavataṇhā, khīṇā bhavanaitti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘sīlaṃ  samādhi pañ‍ñā ca, vimutti ca anuttarā|

anubuddhā imai dhammā, gautamaina yasassinā||

‘‘iti buddhau abhiñ‍ñāya, dhammamakkhāsi bhikkhunaṃ|

dukkhassantakarau satthā, cakkhumā parinibbutau’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. papatitasuttaṃ

2. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi asaman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā papatitau’ti vuc‍cati| katamaihi catūhi? ariyaina, bhikkhavai, sīlaina asaman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā papatitau’ti vuc‍cati| ariyaina, bhikkhavai, samādhinā asaman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā papatitau’ti vuc‍cati| ariyāya, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāya asaman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā papatitau’ti vuc‍cati| ariyāya, bhikkhavai, vimuttiyā asaman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā papatitau’ti vuc‍cati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi asaman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā papatitau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā apapatitau’ti appapatitauti (ka॰) vuc‍cati| katamaihi catūhi? ariyaina, bhikkhavai, sīlaina saman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā apapatitau’ti vuc‍cati| ariyaina, bhikkhavai, samādhinā saman‍nāgatau  ‘imasmā dhammavinayā apapatitau’ti vuc‍cati| ariyāya, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā apapatitau’ti vuc‍cati| ariyāya, bhikkhavai, vimuttiyā saman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā apapatitau’ti vuc‍cati| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ‘imasmā dhammavinayā apapatitau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘cutā patanti patitā, giddhā ca punarāgatā|

kataṃ kic‍caṃ rataṃ rammaṃ, sukhainānvāgataṃ sukha’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamakhatasuttaṃ

3. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau avyattau (sī॰ pī॰) asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca  hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi catūhi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā appasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā pasādanīyai ṭhānai appasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau vyattau (sī॰ pī॰), byattau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi catūhi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā appasādanīyai ṭhānai appasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ  attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti|

su॰ ni॰ 663; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.180 ‘‘yau nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati,

taṃ vā nindati yau pasaṃsiyau|

vicināti mukhaina sau kaliṃ,

kalinā taina sukhaṃ na vindati||

su॰ ni॰ 663; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.180 ‘‘appamattau ayaṃ kali,

yau akkhaisu dhanaparājayau|

sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā,

ayamaiva mahantatarau kali|

yau sugataisu manaṃ padausayai||

‘‘sataṃ  sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ,

chattiṃsatī pañ‍ca ca abbudāni|

yamariyagarahī  yamariyaṃ garahīya (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nirayaṃ upaiti,

vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya pāpaka’’nti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyakhatasuttaṃ

4. ‘‘catūsu, bhikkhavai, micchā paṭipaj‍jamānau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaisu catūsu? mātari, bhikkhavai, micchā paṭipaj‍jamānau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| pitari, bhikkhavai, micchā paṭipaj‍jamānau…pai॰… tathāgatai, bhikkhavai, micchā paṭipaj‍jamānau…pai॰… tathāgatasāvakai, bhikkhavai, micchā paṭipaj‍jamānau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca  viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, catūsu micchā paṭipaj‍jamānau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati|

‘‘catūsu, bhikkhavai, sammā paṭipaj‍jamānau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaisu catūsu? mātari, bhikkhavai, sammā paṭipaj‍jamānau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| pitari, bhikkhavai, sammā paṭipaj‍jamānau…pai॰… tathāgatai, bhikkhavai, sammā paṭipaj‍jamānau…pai॰… tathāgatasāvakai, bhikkhavai, sammā paṭipaj‍jamānau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| imaisu khau, bhikkhavai, catūsu sammā paṭipaj‍jamānau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti|

‘‘mātari  pitari cāpi, yau micchā paṭipaj‍jati|

tathāgatai vā sambuddhai, atha vā tassa sāvakai|

bahuñ‍ca  sau pasavati, apuñ‍ñaṃ tādisau narau||

‘‘tāya naṃ adhammacariyāya tāya adhammacariyāya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), mātāpitūsu paṇḍitā|

idhaiva naṃ garahanti, paic‍cāpāyañ‍ca gacchati||

‘‘mātari pitari cāpi, yau sammā paṭipaj‍jati|

tathāgatai vā sambuddhai, atha vā tassa sāvakai|

bahuñ‍ca sau pasavati, puñ‍ñaṃ aitādisau puñ‍ñampi tādisau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) narau||

‘‘tāya naṃ dhammacariyāya, mātāpitūsu paṇḍitā|

idhaiva idha caiva (sī॰) naṃ pasaṃsanti, paic‍ca saggai pamaudatī’’ti saggai ca maudatīti (sī॰)|| catutthaṃ|

5. anusautasuttaṃ

5. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? anusautagāmī puggalau, paṭisautagāmī puggalau, ṭhitattau puggalau, tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau pāragatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, anusautagāmī puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau kāmai ca paṭisaivati, pāpañ‍ca kammaṃ karauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anusautagāmī puggalau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, paṭisautagāmī puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau kāmai ca nappaṭisaivati, pāpañ‍ca kammaṃ na karauti, sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina assumukhaupi rudamānau paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, paṭisautagāmī puggalau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, ṭhitattau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā  aupapātikau hauti, tattha parinibbāyī, anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ṭhitattau puggalau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti|

‘‘yai kaici kāmaisu asañ‍ñatā janā,

avītarāgā idha kāmabhauginau|

punappunaṃ jātijarūpagāmi tai jātijarūpagāhinau (sī॰), jātijarūpagā hi tai (syā॰ kaṃ॰),

taṇhādhipan‍nā anusautagāminau||

‘‘tasmā  hi dhīrau idhupaṭṭhitassatī,

kāmai ca pāpai ca asaivamānau|

sahāpi dukkhaina jahaiyya kāmai,

paṭisautagāmīti tamāhu puggalaṃ||

‘‘yau vai kilaisāni pahāya pañ‍ca,

paripuṇṇasaikhau aparihānadhammau|

caitauvasippattau samāhitindriyau,

sa vai ṭhitattauti narau pavuc‍cati||

‘‘parauparā  yassa samaic‍ca dhammā,

vidhūpitā atthagatā na santi|

sa vai muni sa vaidagū (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vusitabrahmacariyau,

laukantagū pāragatauti vuc‍catī’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. appassutasuttaṃ

6. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? appassutau sutaina anupapan‍nau, appassutau sutaina upapan‍nau, bahussutau sutaina anupapan‍nau, bahussutau sutaina upapan‍nau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau appassutau hauti sutaina anupapan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa appakaṃ sutaṃ hauti – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| sau tassa appakassa sutassa na atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya na dhammamañ‍ñāya (pī॰ ka॰) dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau appassutau hauti sutaina anupapan‍nau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau appassutau hauti sutaina upapan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa appakaṃ sutaṃ hauti – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ  abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| sau tassa appakassa sutassa atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau appassutau hauti sutaina upapan‍nau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau bahussutau hauti sutaina anupapan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa bahukaṃ sutaṃ hauti – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| sau tassa bahukassa sutassa na atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya  na dhammamañ‍ñāya (pī॰) dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau bahussutau hauti sutaina anupapan‍nau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau bahussutau hauti sutaina upapan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa bahukaṃ sutaṃ hauti – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| sau tassa bahukassa sutassa atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau bahussutau hauti sutaina upapan‍nau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti|

‘‘appassutaupi cai hauti, sīlaisu asamāhitau|

ubhayaina naṃ garahanti, sīlatau ca sutaina ca||

‘‘appassutaupi cai hauti, sīlaisu susamāhitau|

sīlatau naṃ pasaṃsanti, tassa sampaj‍jatai sutaṃ||

‘‘bahussutaupi  cai hauti, sīlaisu asamāhitau|

sīlatau naṃ garahanti, nāssa sampaj‍jatai sutaṃ||

‘‘bahussutaupi  cai hauti, sīlaisu susamāhitau|

ubhayaina naṃ pasaṃsanti, sīlatau ca sutaina ca||

‘‘bahussutaṃ  dhammadharaṃ, sappañ‍ñaṃ buddhasāvakaṃ|

naikkhaṃ jambaunadassaiva, kau taṃ ninditumarahati|

daivāpi naṃ pasaṃsanti, brahmunāpi pasaṃsitau’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. saubhanasuttaṃ

7. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā  dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā saṅghaṃ saubhainti| katamai cattārau? bhikkhu, bhikkhavai, viyattau vinītau visāradau bahussutau dhammadharau dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau saṅghaṃ saubhaiti| bhikkhunī, bhikkhavai, viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā saṅghaṃ saubhaiti| upāsakau, bhikkhavai, viyattau vinītau visāradau bahussutau dhammadharau dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau saṅghaṃ saubhaiti| upāsikā, bhikkhavai, viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā saṅghaṃ saubhaiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā dhammadharā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nā saṅghaṃ saubhaintī’’ti|

‘‘yau hauti viyattau vyattau (sī॰ pī॰), byattau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ca visāradau ca,

bahussutau dhammadharau ca hauti|

dhammassa hauti anudhammacārī,

sa tādisau vuc‍cati saṅghasaubhanau saṃghasaubhaṇau (ka॰)||

‘‘bhikkhu ca sīlasampan‍nau, bhikkhunī ca bahussutā|

upāsakau ca yau saddhau, yā ca saddhā upāsikā|

aitai khau saṅghaṃ saubhainti, aitai hi saṅghasaubhanā’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. vaisāraj‍jasuttaṃ

8. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa vaisāraj‍jāni, yaihi vaisāraj‍jaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti| katamāni  cattāri? ‘sammāsambuddhassa tai paṭijānatau imai dhammā anabhisambuddhā’ti tatra vata maṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ sahadhammaina paṭicaudaissatīti nimittamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, na samanupassāmi| aitamahaṃ aitampahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, nimittaṃ asamanupassantau khaimappattau abhayappattau vaisāraj‍jappattau viharāmi|

‘‘‘khīṇāsavassa tai paṭijānatau imai āsavā aparikkhīṇā’ti tatra vata maṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ sahadhammaina paṭicaudaissatīti nimittamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, na samanupassāmi| aitamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nimittaṃ asamanupassantau khaimappattau abhayappattau vaisāraj‍jappattau viharāmi|

‘‘‘yai khau pana tai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti tatra vata maṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ sahadhammaina paṭicaudaissatīti nimittamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, na samanupassāmi| aitamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nimittaṃ asamanupassantau khaimappattau abhayappattau vaisāraj‍jappattau viharāmi|

‘‘‘yassa khau pana tai atthāya dhammau daisitau sau na niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyā’ti tatra vata maṃ samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ sahadhammaina paṭicaudaissatīti nimittamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, na samanupassāmi| aitamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nimittaṃ asamanupassantau khaimappattau abhayappattau vaisāraj‍jappattau viharāmi| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri tathāgatassa vaisāraj‍jāni, yaihi vaisāraj‍jaihi saman‍nāgatau tathāgatau āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ paṭijānāti, parisāsu sīhanādaṃ nadati, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattaitī’’ti|

‘‘yai  kaicimai vādapathā puthussitā,

yaṃ  nissitā samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca|

tathāgataṃ patvā na tai bhavanti,

visāradaṃ vādapathātivattaṃ vādapathābhivattinaṃ (sī॰), vādapathāti vuttaṃ (pī॰ ka॰)||

‘‘yau dhammacak‍kaṃ abhibhuyya kaivalī kaivalaṃ (syā॰), kaivalau (ka॰),

pavattayī sabbabhūtānukampī|

taṃ  tādisaṃ daivamanussasaiṭṭhaṃ,

sattā namassanti bhavassa pāragu’’nti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. taṇhuppādasuttaṃ

9. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, taṇhuppādā yattha bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati| katamai cattārau? cīvarahaitu vā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati; piṇḍapātahaitu vā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati; saināsanahaitu vā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati; itibhavābhavahaitu vā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau taṇhuppādā yattha bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘taṇhā dutiyau purisau, dīghamaddhāna saṃsaraṃ|

itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ, saṃsāraṃ nātivattati||

‘‘aivamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, taṇhaṃ dukkhassa sambhavaṃ|

vītataṇhau anādānau, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti itivu॰ 15, 105|| navamaṃ|

10. yaugasuttaṃ

10. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, yaugā| katamai cattārau? kāmayaugau, bhavayaugau, diṭṭhiyaugau, avij‍jāyaugau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kāmayaugau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kāmānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca atthagamañ‍ca (sī॰ pī॰) assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa  kāmānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ appajānatau nappajānatau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) yau kāmaisu kāmarāgau kāmanandī kāmanandi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) kāmasnaihau kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāhau kāmajjhausānaṃ kāmataṇhā sānusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kāmayaugau| iti kāmayaugau|

‘‘bhavayaugau  ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau bhavānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa bhavānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ appajānatau yau bhavaisu bhavarāgau bhavanandī bhavasnaihau bhavamucchā bhavapipāsā bhavapariḷāhau bhavajjhausānaṃ bhavataṇhā sānusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhavayaugau| iti kāmayaugau bhavayaugau|

‘‘diṭṭhiyaugau ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau diṭṭhīnaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa diṭṭhīnaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ appajānatau yau  diṭṭhīsu diṭṭhirāgau diṭṭhinandī diṭṭhisnaihau diṭṭhimucchā diṭṭhipipāsā diṭṭhipariḷāhau diṭṭhijjhausānaṃ diṭṭhiajjhausānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) diṭṭhitaṇhā sānusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyaugau| iti kāmayaugau bhavayaugau diṭṭhiyaugau|

‘‘avij‍jāyaugau  ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| tassa chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ appajānatau yā chasu phassāyatanaisu avij‍jā añ‍ñāṇaṃ sānusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāyaugau| iti kāmayaugau bhavayaugau diṭṭhiyaugau avij‍jāyaugau, saṃyuttau pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi saṃkilaisikaihi paunaubhavikaihi paunau bbhavikaihi (syā॰ ka॰) sadaraihi dukkhavipākaihi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇikaihi| tasmā ayaugakkhaimīti vuc‍cati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau yaugā|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, visaṃyaugā| katamai cattārau? kāmayaugavisaṃyaugau, bhavayaugavisaṃyaugau, diṭṭhiyaugavisaṃyaugau, avij‍jāyaugavisaṃyaugau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kāmayaugavisaṃyaugau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kāmānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa kāmānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānatau yau kāmaisu kāmarāgau kāmanandī kāmasnaihau kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāhau kāmajjhausānaṃ kāmataṇhā sā nānusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kāmayaugavisaṃyaugau| iti kāmayaugavisaṃyaugau|

‘‘bhavayaugavisaṃyaugau  ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau bhavānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa bhavānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca  yathābhūtaṃ pajānatau yau bhavaisu bhavarāgau bhavanandī bhavasnaihau bhavamucchā bhavapipāsā bhavapariḷāhau bhavajjhausānaṃ bhavataṇhā sā nānusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhavayaugavisaṃyaugau| iti kāmayaugavisaṃyaugau bhavayaugavisaṃyaugau|

‘‘diṭṭhiyaugavisaṃyaugau ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau diṭṭhīnaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa diṭṭhīnaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānatau yau diṭṭhīsu diṭṭhirāgau diṭṭhinandī diṭṭhisnaihau diṭṭhimucchā diṭṭhipipāsā diṭṭhipariḷāhau diṭṭhijjhausānaṃ diṭṭhitaṇhā sā nānusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyaugavisaṃyaugau| iti kāmayaugavisaṃyaugau bhavayaugavisaṃyaugau diṭṭhiyaugavisaṃyaugau|

‘‘avij‍jāyaugavisaṃyaugau ca kathaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānatau yā chasu phassāyatanaisu avij‍jā añ‍ñāṇaṃ sā nānusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāyaugavisaṃyaugau| iti kāmayaugavisaṃyaugau bhavayaugavisaṃyaugau diṭṭhiyaugavisaṃyaugau avij‍jāyaugavisaṃyaugau, visaṃyuttau pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi saṃkilaisikaihi paunaubhavikaihi sadaraihi dukkhavipākaihi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇikaihi| tasmā yaugakkhaimīti vuc‍cati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau visaṃyaugā’’ti|

‘‘kāmayaugaina  saṃyuttā, bhavayaugaina cūbhayaṃ|

diṭṭhiyaugaina saṃyuttā, avij‍jāya purakkhatā||

‘‘sattā gacchanti saṃsāraṃ, jātimaraṇagāminau|

yai ca kāmai pariñ‍ñāya, bhavayaugañ‍ca sabbasau||

‘‘diṭṭhiyaugaṃ  samūhac‍ca, avij‍jañ‍ca virājayaṃ|

sabbayaugavisaṃyuttā, tai vai yaugātigā munī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

bhaṇḍagāmavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

anubuddhaṃ papatitaṃ dvai, khatā anusautapañ‍camaṃ|

appassutau  ca saubhanaṃ, vaisāraj‍jaṃ taṇhāyaugaina tai dasāti||


2. caravaggau

1. carasuttaṃ

11. itivu॰ 110 ‘‘caratau  caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau vā byāpādavitak‍kau vā vihiṃsāvitak‍kau vā| taṃ cai bhikkhu adhivāsaiti, nappajahati na vinaudaiti na byantīkarauti byantikarauti (pī॰), byantiṃ karauti (ka॰) na anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, carampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃbhūtau ‘anātāpī anauttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ kusītau hīnavīriyau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘ṭhitassa  caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau vā byāpādavitak‍kau vā vihiṃsāvitak‍kau vā| taṃ cai bhikkhu adhivāsaiti, nappajahati na vinaudaiti na byantīkarauti na anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, ṭhitaupi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃbhūtau ‘anātāpī anauttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ kusītau  hīnavīriyau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘nisin‍nassa caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau vā byāpādavitak‍kau vā vihiṃsāvitak‍kau vā| taṃ cai bhikkhu adhivāsaiti, nappajahati na vinaudaiti na byantīkarauti na anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, nisin‍naupi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃbhūtau ‘anātāpī anauttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ kusītau hīnavīriyau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sayānassa caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau jāgarassa uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau vā byāpādavitak‍kau vā vihiṃsāvitak‍kau vā| taṃ cai bhikkhu adhivāsaiti, nappajahati na vinaudaiti na byantīkarauti na anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, sayānaupi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jāgarau aivaṃbhūtau ‘anātāpī anauttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ kusītau hīnavīriyau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘caratau caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau vā byāpādavitak‍kau vā vihiṃsāvitak‍kau vā| taṃ cai bhikkhu nādhivāsaiti, pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, carampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃbhūtau ‘ātāpī auttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ āraddhavīriyau pahitattau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘ṭhitassa  caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau vā byāpādavitak‍kau vā vihiṃsāvitak‍kau vā| taṃ cai bhikkhu nādhivāsaiti, pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, ṭhitaupi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃbhūtau ‘ātāpī auttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ āraddhavīriyau pahitattau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘nisin‍nassa caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau vā byāpādavitak‍kau vā vihiṃsāvitak‍kau vā| taṃ cai bhikkhu nādhivāsaiti, pajahati vinaudaiti  byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, nisin‍naupi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃbhūtau ‘ātāpī auttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ āraddhavīriyau pahitattau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sayānassa  caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau jāgarassa uppaj‍jati kāmavitak‍kau vā byāpādavitak‍kau vā vihiṃsāvitak‍kau vā| taṃ cai bhikkhu nādhivāsaiti, pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti  anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, sayānaupi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jāgarau aivaṃbhūtau ‘ātāpī auttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ āraddhavīriyau pahitattau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘caraṃ vā yadi vā tiṭṭhaṃ, nisin‍nau uda vā sayaṃ|

yau vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaiti, pāpakaṃ gaihanissitaṃ||

‘‘kummaggappaṭipan‍nau sau, mauhanaiyyaisu mucchitau|

abhabbau tādisau bhikkhu, phuṭṭhuṃ sambaudhimuttamaṃ||

‘‘yau ca caraṃ vā tiṭṭhaṃ vā, nisin‍nau uda vā sayaṃ|

vitak‍kaṃ samayitvāna, vitak‍kūpasamai ratau|

bhabbau sau tādisau bhikkhu, phuṭṭhuṃ sambaudhimuttama’’nti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sīlasuttaṃ

12. ‘‘sampan‍nasīlā, bhikkhavai, viharatha sampan‍napātimaukkhā, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutā viharatha  ācāragaucarasampan‍nā aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvinau| samādāya sikkhatha sikkhāpadaisu| sampan‍nasīlānaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, viharataṃ sampan‍napātimaukkhānaṃ pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutānaṃ viharataṃ ācāragaucarasampan‍nānaṃ aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvīnaṃ samādāya sikkhataṃ sikkhāpadaisu kimassa uttari karaṇīyaṃ?

‘‘caratau caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau abhijjhābyāpādau  vigatau hauti, thinamiddhaṃ… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ… vicikicchā pahīnā hauti, āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ, carampi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃbhūtau ‘ātāpī auttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ āraddhavīriyau pahitattau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘ṭhitassa caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau abhijjhābyāpādau vigatau hauti, thinamiddhaṃ… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ… vicikicchā pahīnā hauti, āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ  cittaṃ aikaggaṃ, ṭhitaupi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃbhūtau ‘ātāpī auttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ āraddhavīriyau pahitattau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘nisin‍nassa caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau abhijjhābyāpādau vigatau hauti, thinamiddhaṃ… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ… vicikicchā pahīnā hauti, āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ, nisin‍naupi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃbhūtau ‘ātāpī auttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ āraddhavīriyau  pahitattau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sayānassa caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau jāgarassa abhijjhābyāpādau vigatau hauti, thinamiddhaṃ… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ… vicikicchā pahīnā hauti, āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ, sayānaupi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jāgarau aivaṃbhūtau ‘ātāpī auttāpī satataṃ samitaṃ āraddhavīriyau pahitattau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘yataṃ yathā (ka॰) itivu॰ 111 carai yataṃ yathā (ka॰) itivu॰ 111 tiṭṭhai, yataṃ yathā (ka॰) itivu॰ 111 acchai yataṃ yathā (ka॰) itivu॰ 111 sayai|

yataṃ yathā (ka॰) itivu॰ 111 samiñ‍jayai sammiñ‍jayai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhikkhu, yatamainaṃ yatamaiva naṃ (sī॰), yatamaitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), yatamaiva (?) pasārayai||

‘‘uddhaṃ  tiriyaṃ apācīnaṃ, yāvatā jagatau gati|

samavaikkhitā ca dhammānaṃ, khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ||

‘‘caitausamathasāmīciṃ, sikkhamānaṃ sadā sataṃ|

satataṃ pahitattauti, āhu bhikkhuṃ tathāvidha’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. padhānasuttaṃ

13. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, sammappadhānāni| katamāni cattāri? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya  chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ  janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri sammappadhānānī’’ti|

‘‘sammappadhānā māradhaiyyābhibhūtā,

tai asitā jātimaraṇabhayassa pāragū|

tai tusitā jaitvā māraṃ savāhiniṃ savāhanaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) tai anaijā,

sabbaṃ namucibalaṃ upātivattā tai sukhitā’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. saṃvarasuttaṃ

14. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, padhānāni| katamāni cattāri? saṃvarappadhānaṃ, pahānappadhānaṃ, bhāvanāppadhānaṃ, anurakkhaṇāppadhānaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṃvarappadhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya  na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī, yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saṃvarappadhānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pahānappadhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti; uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pahānappadhānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhāvanāppadhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ  bhāvaiti… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhāvanāppadhānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anurakkhaṇāppadhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ bhaddakaṃ samādhinimittaṃ anurakkhati aṭṭhikasañ‍ñaṃ puḷavakasañ‍ñaṃ vinīlakasañ‍ñaṃ vicchiddakasañ‍ñaṃ uddhumātakasañ‍ñaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anurakkhaṇāppadhānaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri padhānānī’’ti|

‘‘saṃvarau ca pahānañ‍ca, bhāvanā anurakkhaṇā|

aitai  padhānā cattārau, daisitādic‍cabandhunā|

yaihi bhikkhu idhātāpī, khayaṃ dukkhassa pāpuṇai’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. pañ‍ñattisuttaṃ

15. ‘‘catassau  imā, bhikkhavai, aggapañ‍ñattiyau| katamā catassau? aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, attabhāvīnaṃ yadidaṃ – rāhu asurindau| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāmabhaugīnaṃ yadidaṃ – rājā mandhātā| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādhipataiyyānaṃ yadidaṃ – mārau pāpimā| sadaivakai, bhikkhavai, laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya tathāgatau aggamakkhāyati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau aggapañ‍ñattiyau’’ti|

‘‘rāhuggaṃ attabhāvīnaṃ, mandhātā kāmabhauginaṃ|

mārau ādhipataiyyānaṃ, iddhiyā yasasā jalaṃ||

‘‘uddhaṃ tiriyaṃ apācīnaṃ, yāvatā jagatau gati|

sadaivakassa laukassa, buddhau aggau pavuc‍catī’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. saukhummasuttaṃ

16. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, saukhummāni| katamāni cattāri? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpasaukhummaina saman‍nāgatau hauti paramaina; taina ca rūpasaukhummaina  añ‍ñaṃ rūpasaukhummaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā na samanupassati; taina ca rūpasaukhummaina añ‍ñaṃ rūpasaukhummaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā na patthaiti| vaidanāsaukhummaina  saman‍nāgatau hauti paramaina; taina ca vaidanāsaukhummaina añ‍ñaṃ vaidanāsaukhummaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā na samanupassati; taina ca vaidanāsaukhummaina añ‍ñaṃ vaidanāsaukhummaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā na patthaiti| sañ‍ñāsaukhummaina saman‍nāgatau hauti paramaina; taina ca sañ‍ñāsaukhummaina añ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāsaukhummaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā na samanupassati; taina ca sañ‍ñāsaukhummaina añ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāsaukhummaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā na patthaiti| saṅkhārasaukhummaina saman‍nāgatau hauti paramaina; taina ca saṅkhārasaukhummaina añ‍ñaṃ saṅkhārasaukhummaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā  paṇītataraṃ vā na samanupassati; taina ca saṅkhārasaukhummaina añ‍ñaṃ saṅkhārasaukhummaṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā na patthaiti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri saukhummānī’’ti|

‘‘rūpasaukhummataṃ  ñatvā, vaidanānañ‍ca sambhavaṃ|

sañ‍ñā yatau samudaiti, atthaṃ gacchati yattha ca|

saṅkhārai paratau ñatvā, dukkhatau nau ca attatau||

‘‘sa vai sammaddasau bhikkhu, santau santipadai ratau|

dhāraiti antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhini’’nti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamaagatisuttaṃ

17. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, agatigamanāni| katamāni cattāri? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri agatigamanānī’’ti|

‘‘chandā dausā bhayā mauhā, yau dhammaṃ ativattati|

nihīyati tassa yasau, kāḷapakkhaiva candimā’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaagatisuttaṃ

18. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, nāgatigamanāni| katamāni cattāri? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri nāgatigamanānī’’ti|

‘‘chandā dausā bhayā mauhā, yau dhammaṃ nātivattati|

āpūrati tassa yasau, suk‍kapakkhaiva candimā’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tatiyaagatisuttaṃ

19. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, agatigamanāni| katamāni cattāri? chandāgatiṃ  gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati  – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri agatigamanāni|

‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, nāgatigamanāni| katamāni cattāri? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri nāgatigamanānī’’ti|

‘‘chandā dausā bhayā mauhā, yau dhammaṃ ativattati|

nihīyati tassa yasau, kāḷapakkhaiva candimā||

‘‘chandā dausā bhayā mauhā, yau dhammaṃ nātivattati|

āpūrati tassa yasau, suk‍kapakkhaiva candimā’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. bhattuddaisakasuttaṃ

20. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ  gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti|

‘‘yai kaici kāmaisu asañ‍ñatā janā,

adhammikā haunti adhammagāravā|

chandā dausā mauhā ca bhayā gāminau chandā ca dausā ca bhayā ca gāminau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī),

parisākasaṭau  parisak‍kasāvau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ca panaisa vuc‍cati||

‘‘aivañhi  vuttaṃ samaṇaina jānatā,

tasmā hi tai sappurisā pasaṃsiyā|

dhammai ṭhitā yai na karaunti pāpakaṃ,

na chandā na dausā na mauhā na bhayā ca gāminau na chandadausā na bhayā ca gāminau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

‘‘parisāya maṇḍau ca panaisa vuc‍cati,

aivañhi vuttaṃ samaṇaina jānatā’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

caravaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

caraṃ sīlaṃ padhānāni, saṃvaraṃ pañ‍ñatti pañ‍camaṃ|

saukhummaṃ tayau agatī, bhattuddaisaina tai dasāti||


3. uruvailavaggau

1. paṭhamauruvailasuttaṃ

21. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.173 āgataṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, samayaṃ uruvailāyaṃ viharāmi naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘dukkhaṃ khau agāravau viharati appatissau| kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ  katvā garukatvā (sī॰ pī॰) upanissāya viharaiyya’’’nti?

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – aparipūrassa khau ahaṃ sīlakkhandhassa pāripūriyā añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| na khau panāhaṃ passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā attanā sīlasampan‍nataraṃ, yamahaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ|

‘‘aparipūrassa khau ahaṃ samādhikkhandhassa pāripūriyā añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| na khau panāhaṃ passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā attanā samādhisampan‍nataraṃ, yamahaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ|

‘‘aparipūrassa khau ahaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhassa pāripūriyā añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| na khau panāhaṃ passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai  sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā attanā pañ‍ñāsampan‍nataraṃ, yamahaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ|

‘‘aparipūrassa khau ahaṃ vimuttikkhandhassa pāripūriyā añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| na khau panāhaṃ passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā attanā vimuttisampan‍nataraṃ, yamahaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ yvāyaṃ yaupāyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dhammau mayā abhisambuddhau tamaiva dhammaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyya’’’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati mama caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya  – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ sammiñ‍jitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaivaṃ – brahmalaukai antarahitau mama puratau pāturahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā dakkhiṇaṃ jāṇumaṇḍalaṃ pathaviyaṃ nihantvā yaināhaṃ tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā  maṃ aitadavauca – ‘aivamaitaṃ bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ sugata! yaipi tai, bhantai, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā taipi bhagavantau dhammaṃyaiva sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya vihariṃsu; yaipi tai, bhantai, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā taipi bhagavantau dhammaṃyaiva sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharissanti; bhagavāpi, bhantai, aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammaṃyaiva sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharatū’’’ti| idamavauca brahmā sahampati| idaṃ vatvā athāparaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘yai ca atītā yai cabbhatītā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) sambuddhā, yai ca buddhā anāgatā|

yau caitarahi sambuddhau, bahūnaṃ bahun‍naṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.173 saukanāsanau||

‘‘sabbai saddhammagarunau, vihaṃsu vihariṃsu (syā॰ kaṃ॰) viharanti ca|

athaupi viharissanti, aisā buddhāna dhammatā||

‘‘tasmā  hi attakāmaina atthakāmaina (sī॰ ka॰), mahattamabhikaṅkhatā|

saddhammau garukātabbau, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti||

‘‘idamavauca, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi| atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmunau ca ajjhaisanaṃ viditvā attanau ca patirūpaṃ yvāyaṃ yaupāyaṃ (sabbattha) dhammau mayā abhisambuddhau tamaiva dhammaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya vihāsiṃ| yatau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, saṅghaupi mahattaina saman‍nāgatau, atha mai  saṅghaipi gāravau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyauruvailasuttaṃ

22. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, samayaṃ uruvailāyaṃ viharāmi naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sambahulā brāhmaṇā jiṇṇā vuddhā mahal‍lakā addhagatā vayauanuppattā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā mayā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau, bhikkhavai, tai brāhmaṇā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘sutaṃ naitaṃ maitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), bhau gautama – na samaṇau gautamau brāhmaṇai jiṇṇai vuddhai mahal‍lakai addhagatai vayauanuppattai abhivādaiti vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiti vā āsanaina vā nimantaitīti| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, tathaiva| na hi bhavaṃ gautamau brāhmaṇai jiṇṇai vuddhai mahal‍lakai addhagatai vayauanuppattai abhivādaiti vā pac‍cuṭṭhaiti vā āsanaina vā nimantaiti| tayidaṃ, bhau gautama, na sampan‍namaivā’’’ti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘nayimai na vata mai (sī॰ pī॰), na cayimai (syā॰ kaṃ॰), na vatimai (?) āyasmantau jānanti thairaṃ vā thairakaraṇai vā dhammai’ti| vuddhau caipi, bhikkhavai, hauti āsītikau vā nāvutikau vā vassasatikau vā jātiyā| sau ca hauti akālavādī abhūtavādī anatthavādī adhammavādī avinayavādī, anidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā akālaina anapadaisaṃ apariyantavatiṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ| atha khau sau ‘bālau thairau’tvaiva taiva (sī॰ pī॰) saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘daharau caipi, bhikkhavai, hauti yuvā susukāḷakaisau bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau paṭhamaina vayasā| sau ca hauti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī nidhānavatiṃ  vācaṃ bhāsitā kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ| atha khau sau ‘paṇḍitau thairau’tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, thairakaraṇā dhammā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, bahussutau hauti  sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ sātthā sabyañ‍janā (sī॰ pī॰) kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇā (sī॰) parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā dhatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau thairakaraṇā dhammā’’ti|

‘‘yau uddhataina cittaina, samphañ‍ca bahu bhāsati|

asamāhitasaṅkappau, asaddhammaratau magau|

ārā sau thāvaraiyyamhā, pāpadiṭṭhi anādarau||

‘‘yau ca sīlaina sampan‍nau, sutavā paṭibhānavā|

sañ‍ñatau dhīrau dhammaisu sañ‍ñatau dhīradhammaisu (sī॰), saṃyuttau thiradhammaisu (syā॰ kaṃ॰), pañ‍ñāyatthaṃ vipassati||

‘‘pāragū  sabbadhammānaṃ, akhilau paṭibhānavā|

pahīnajātimaraṇau, brahmacariyassa kaivalī||

‘‘tamahaṃ vadāmi thairauti, yassa nau santi āsavā|

āsavānaṃ khayā bhikkhu, sau thairauti pavuc‍catī’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. laukasuttaṃ

23. ‘‘laukau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau| laukasmā tathāgatau visaṃyuttau| laukasamudayau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau| laukasamudayau tathāgatassa pahīnau| laukaniraudhau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhau| laukaniraudhau tathāgatassa  sacchikatau| laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina abhisambuddhā| laukaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā tathāgatassa bhāvitā|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ  anuvicaritaṃ manasā, sabbaṃ taṃ tathāgataina abhisambuddhaṃ| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati, yaṃ aitasmiṃ antarai bhāsati lapati niddisati sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘yathāvādī, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| iti yathāvādī tathākārī, yathākārī tathāvādī| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sadaivakai, bhikkhavai, laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya  tathāgatau abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthu dasau vasavattī| tasmā ‘tathāgatau’ti vuc‍cati’’|

‘‘sabbaṃ laukaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, sabbaṃ laukai yathātathaṃ|

sabbaṃ laukaṃ sabbalauka (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) visaṃyuttau, sabbalaukai anūpayau||

‘‘sa vai sabbābhibhū dhīrau, sabbaganthappamaucanau|

phuṭṭha’ssa paramā santi, nibbānaṃ akutaubhayaṃ||

‘‘aisa  khīṇāsavau buddhau, anīghau chin‍nasaṃsayau|

sabbakammakkhayaṃ pattau, vimuttau upadhisaṅkhayai||

‘‘aisa sau bhagavā buddhau, aisa sīhau anuttarau|

sadaivakassa laukassa, brahmacak‍kaṃ pavattayī||

‘‘iti daivā manussā ca, yai buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā|

saṅgamma taṃ namassanti, mahantaṃ vītasāradaṃ||

‘‘dantau  damayataṃ saiṭṭhau, santau samayataṃ isi|

muttau maucayataṃ aggau, tiṇṇau tārayataṃ varau||

‘‘iti haitaṃ namassanti, mahantaṃ vītasāradaṃ|

sadaivakasmiṃ laukasmiṃ, natthi mai natthi tai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paṭipuggalau’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. kāḷakārāmasuttaṃ

24. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sākaitai viharati kāḷakārāmai kauḷikārāmai (ka॰)| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa  samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, tamahaṃ jānāmi|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, tamahaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| taṃ tathāgatassa viditaṃ, taṃ tathāgatau na upaṭṭhāsi|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakassa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā, tamahaṃ na jānāmīti vadaiyyaṃ, taṃ mamassa musā|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… tamahaṃ jānāmi ca na ca jānāmīti vadaiyyaṃ, taṃpassa taṃ pissa (syā॰ kaṃ॰), taṃ mamassa (ka॰) tādisamaiva|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… tamahaṃ naiva jānāmi na na jānāmīti vadaiyyaṃ, taṃ mamassa kali|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau daṭṭhā daṭṭhabbaṃ, diṭṭhaṃ na mañ‍ñati, adiṭṭhaṃ na mañ‍ñati, daṭṭhabbaṃ na mañ‍ñati, daṭṭhāraṃ na mañ‍ñati; sutvā sautabbaṃ, sutaṃ na  mañ‍ñati, asutaṃ na mañ‍ñati, sautabbaṃ na mañ‍ñati, sautāraṃ na mañ‍ñati; mutvā mautabbaṃ, mutaṃ na mañ‍ñati, amutaṃ na mañ‍ñati, mautabbaṃ na mañ‍ñati, mautāraṃ na mañ‍ñati; viñ‍ñatvā viñ‍ñātabbaṃ, viñ‍ñātaṃ na mañ‍ñati, aviñ‍ñātaṃ na mañ‍ñati, viñ‍ñātabbaṃ na mañ‍ñati, viñ‍ñātāraṃ na mañ‍ñati| iti khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu tādīyaiva tādī tādisauva tādī (syā॰ kaṃ॰), tādisai yaiva tādī (pī॰), tādīyaiva tādīyaivaikā (ka॰)| tamhā ca pana tādimhā tāditamhā (sī॰ pī॰) añ‍ñau tādī uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā natthīti vadāmī’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ  kiñ‍ci diṭṭhaṃva sutaṃ mutaṃ vā,

ajjhausitaṃ sac‍camutaṃ paraisaṃ|

na taisu tādī sayasaṃvutaisu,

sac‍caṃ musā vāpi paraṃ dahaiyya||

‘‘aitañ‍ca sal‍laṃ paṭikac‍ca paṭigac‍ca (sī॰ pī॰) disvā,

ajjhausitā yattha pajā visattā|

jānāmi  passāmi tathaiva aitaṃ,

ajjhausitaṃ natthi tathāgatāna’’nti|| catutthaṃ|

5. brahmacariyasuttaṃ

25. ‘‘nayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ vussati janakuhanatthaṃ, na janalapanatthaṃ, na lābhasak‍kārasilaukānisaṃsatthaṃ, na itivādappamaukkhānisaṃsatthaṃ, na ‘iti maṃ janau jānātū’ti| atha khau idaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ vussati saṃvaratthaṃ pahānatthaṃ virāgatthaṃ niraudhattha’’nti|

‘‘saṃvaratthaṃ pahānatthaṃ, brahmacariyaṃ anītihaṃ|

adaisayi sau bhagavā, nibbānaugadhagāminaṃ|

aisa maggau mahantaihi mahattaibhi (ka॰) itivu॰ 35, anuyātau mahaisibhi||

‘‘yai ca taṃ paṭipaj‍janti, yathā buddhaina daisitaṃ|

dukkhassantaṃ karissanti, satthusāsanakārinau’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kuhasuttaṃ

26. itivu॰ 108 itivuttakaipi ‘‘yai  tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū kuhā thaddhā lapā siṅgī un‍naḷā asamāhitā, na mai tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū māmakā| apagatā ca tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmā dhammavinayā, na ca tai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ viruḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti| yai ca khau tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū nik‍kuhā nil‍lapā dhīrā atthaddhā susamāhitā, tai khau mai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū māmakā| anapagatā ca tai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmā dhammavinayā| tai ca imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ viruḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jantī’’ti|

itivu॰ 108 itivuttakaipi ‘‘kuhā thaddhā lapā siṅgī, un‍naḷā asamāhitā|

na tai dhammai virūhanti, sammāsambuddhadaisitai||

‘‘nik‍kuhā nil‍lapā dhīrā, atthaddhā susamāhitā|

tai vai dhammai virūhanti, sammāsambuddhadaisitai’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. santuṭṭhisuttaṃ

27. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, appāni ca caiva (ka॰) sulabhāni ca, tāni ca anavaj‍jāni| katamāni cattāri? paṃsukūlaṃ, bhikkhavai, cīvarānaṃ appañ‍ca sulabhañ‍ca, tañ‍ca anavaj‍jaṃ| piṇḍiyālaupau, bhikkhavai, bhaujanānaṃ appañ‍ca sulabhañ‍ca, tañ‍ca anavaj‍jaṃ| rukkhamūlaṃ, bhikkhavai, saināsanānaṃ appañ‍ca sulabhañ‍ca, tañ‍ca anavaj‍jaṃ| pūtimuttaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhaisaj‍jānaṃ appañ‍ca sulabhañ‍ca, tañ‍ca anavaj‍jaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri appāni ca sulabhāni ca, tāni ca anavaj‍jāni| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu appaina ca tuṭṭhau hauti sulabhaina ca, idamassāhaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ sāmañ‍ñaṅganti sāmañ‍ñanti (ka॰) vadāmī’’ti|

‘‘anavaj‍jaina tuṭṭhassa, appaina sulabhaina ca|

na  saināsanamārabbha, cīvaraṃ pānabhaujanaṃ|

vighātau hauti cittassa, disā nappaṭihañ‍ñati||

‘‘yai cassa dhammā akkhātā, sāmañ‍ñassānulaumikā|

adhiggahitā tuṭṭhassa, appamattassa sikkhatau’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. ariyavaṃsasuttaṃ

28. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, ariyavaṃsā aggañ‍ñā rattañ‍ñā vaṃsañ‍ñā paurāṇā asaṃkiṇṇā asaṃkiṇṇapubbā, na saṃkīyanti na saṃkīyissanti, appaṭikuṭṭhā samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina cīvaraina, itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, na ca cīvarahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati, aladdhā ca cīvaraṃ na paritassati, laddhā ca cīvaraṃ agadhitau agathitau (sī॰ pī॰) amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati; tāya ca pana itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, nau na (dī॰ ni॰ 3.309) paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau patissatau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina, itarītarapiṇḍapātasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, na ca piṇḍapātahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati, aladdhā ca piṇḍapātaṃ na paritassati, laddhā ca piṇḍapātaṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau  paribhuñ‍jati; tāya ca pana itarītarapiṇḍapātasantuṭṭhiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, nau paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau patissatau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina saināsanaina, itarītarasaināsanasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, na ca saināsanahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati, aladdhā ca saināsanaṃ na paritassati, laddhā ca saināsanaṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati; tāya ca pana itarītarasaināsanasantuṭṭhiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, nau paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau patissatau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhāvanārāmau hauti bhāvanāratau, pahānārāmau hauti pahānaratau; tāya ca pana bhāvanārāmatāya bhāvanāratiyā  pahānārāmatāya pahānaratiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti, nau paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau patissatau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau ariyavaṃsā aggañ‍ñā rattañ‍ñā vaṃsañ‍ñā paurāṇā asaṃkiṇṇā asaṃkiṇṇapubbā, na saṃkīyanti na saṃkīyissanti, appaṭikuṭṭhā samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi|

‘‘imaihi ca pana, bhikkhavai, catūhi ariyavaṃsaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu puratthimāya caipi disāya viharati svaiva aratiṃ sahati, na taṃ arati sahati; pacchimāya caipi disāya viharati  svaiva aratiṃ sahati, na taṃ arati sahati; uttarāya caipi disāya viharati svaiva aratiṃ sahati, na taṃ arati sahati; dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya viharati svaiva aratiṃ sahati, na taṃ arati sahati| taṃ kissa haitu? aratiratisahau hi, bhikkhavai, dhīrau’’ti|

‘‘nārati  sahati dhīraṃ vīraṃ (sī॰), nārati dhīraṃ sahati|

dhīrauva aratiṃ sahati, dhīrau hi aratissahau||

‘‘sabbakammavihāyīnaṃ, panuṇṇaṃ paṇun‍naṃ (?) kau nivārayai|

naikkhaṃ jambaunadassaiva, kau taṃ ninditumarahati|

daivāpi naṃ pasaṃsanti, brahmunāpi pasaṃsitau’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhammapadasuttaṃ

29. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, dhammapadāni aggañ‍ñāni rattañ‍ñāni vaṃsañ‍ñāni paurāṇāni asaṃkiṇṇāni asaṃkiṇṇapubbāni, na saṃkīyanti na saṃkīyissanti, appaṭikuṭṭhāni samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| katamāni cattāri? anabhijjhā, bhikkhavai, dhammapadaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ rattañ‍ñaṃ vaṃsañ‍ñaṃ paurāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ, na saṃkīyati na saṃkīyissati, appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi|

‘‘abyāpādau, bhikkhavai, dhammapadaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ rattañ‍ñaṃ vaṃsañ‍ñaṃ paurāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ, na saṃkīyati na saṃkīyissati, appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi|

‘‘sammāsati, bhikkhavai, dhammapadaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ rattañ‍ñaṃ vaṃsañ‍ñaṃ paurāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ, na saṃkīyati na saṃkīyissati, appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi|

‘‘sammāsamādhi, bhikkhavai, dhammapadaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ rattañ‍ñaṃ vaṃsañ‍ñaṃ paurāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ, na saṃkīyati na saṃkīyissati, appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri dhammapadāni aggañ‍ñāni rattañ‍ñāni vaṃsañ‍ñāni paurāṇāni asaṃkiṇṇāni asaṃkiṇṇapubbāni, na saṃkīyanti na saṃkīyissanti, appaṭikuṭṭhāni  samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘anabhijjhālu  viharaiyya, abyāpan‍naina caitasā|

satau aikaggacittassa aikaggacittāyaṃ (ka॰), ajjhattaṃ susamāhitau’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. paribbājakasuttaṃ

30. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā paribbājakā sippinikātīrai sappiniyā tīrai (sī॰ pī॰), sippiniyā tīrai (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sippiniyā nadiyā tīrai (ka॰) paribbājakārāmai paṭivasanti, saiyyathidaṃ an‍nabhārau varadharau sakuludāyī ca paribbājakau añ‍ñai ca abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā paribbājakā| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina sippinikātīraṃ paribbājakārāmau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā tai paribbājakai aitadavauca –

‘‘cattārimāni, paribbājakā, dhammapadāni aggañ‍ñāni rattañ‍ñāni  vaṃsañ‍ñāni paurāṇāni asaṃkiṇṇāni asaṃkiṇṇapubbāni, na saṃkīyanti na saṃkīyissanti, appaṭikuṭṭhāni samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| katamāni cattāri? anabhijjhā, paribbājakā, dhammapadaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ rattañ‍ñaṃ vaṃsañ‍ñaṃ paurāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ, na saṃkīyati na saṃkīyissati, appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| abyāpādau, paribbājakā, dhammapadaṃ…pai॰… sammāsati, paribbājakā, dhammapadaṃ…pai॰… sammāsamādhi, paribbājakā, dhammapadaṃ aggañ‍ñaṃ rattañ‍ñaṃ vaṃsañ‍ñaṃ  paurāṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇaṃ asaṃkiṇṇapubbaṃ, na saṃkīyati na saṃkīyissati, appaṭikuṭṭhaṃ samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| imāni khau, paribbājakā, cattāri dhammapadāni aggañ‍ñāni rattañ‍ñāni vaṃsañ‍ñāni paurāṇāni asaṃkiṇṇāni asaṃkiṇṇapubbāni, na saṃkīyanti na saṃkīyissanti, appaṭikuṭṭhāni samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi|

‘‘yau khau, paribbājakā, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamaitaṃ anabhijjhaṃ dhammapadaṃ pac‍cakkhāya abhijjhāluṃ kāmaisu tibbasārāgaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissāmī’ti, tamahaṃ tattha aivaṃ vadaiyyaṃ – ‘aitu vadatu byāharatu passāmissānubhāva’nti| sau vata, paribbājakā, anabhijjhaṃ dhammapadaṃ pac‍cakkhāya abhijjhāluṃ kāmaisu tibbasārāgaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘yau  khau, paribbājakā, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamaitaṃ abyāpādaṃ dhammapadaṃ pac‍cakkhāya byāpan‍nacittaṃ paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissāmī’ti, tamahaṃ tattha aivaṃ vadaiyyaṃ – ‘aitu vadatu byāharatu passāmissānubhāva’nti| sau vata, paribbājakā, abyāpādaṃ dhammapadaṃ pac‍cakkhāya byāpan‍nacittaṃ paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘yau khau, paribbājakā, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamaitaṃ sammāsatiṃ dhammapadaṃ pac‍cakkhāya muṭṭhassatiṃ asampajānaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissāmī’ti, tamahaṃ tattha aivaṃ vadaiyyaṃ – ‘aitu vadatu byāharatu passāmissānubhāva’nti| sau vata, paribbājakā, sammāsatiṃ dhammapadaṃ pac‍cakkhāya muṭṭhassatiṃ asampajānaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘yau khau, paribbājakā, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamaitaṃ  sammāsamādhiṃ dhammapadaṃ pac‍cakkhāya asamāhitaṃ vibbhantacittaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissāmī’ti, tamahaṃ tattha aivaṃ vadaiyyaṃ – ‘aitu vadatu byāharatu  passāmissānubhāva’nti| sau vata, paribbājakā, sammāsamādhiṃ dhammapadaṃ pac‍cakkhāya asamāhitaṃ vibbhantacittaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘yau khau, paribbājakā, imāni cattāri dhammapadāni garahitabbaṃ paṭik‍kausitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya, tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai cattārau sahadhammikā vādānupātā gārayhā  ṭhānā vādānuvādā gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 3.8) āgacchanti| katamai cattārau? anabhijjhaṃ cai bhavaṃ dhammapadaṃ garahati paṭik‍kausati, yai ca hi yai ca (ma॰ ni॰ 3.142-143) abhijjhālū kāmaisu tibbasārāgā samaṇabrāhmaṇā tai bhautau puj‍jā tai bhautau pāsaṃsā| abyāpādaṃ cai bhavaṃ dhammapadaṃ garahati paṭik‍kausati, yai ca hi byāpan‍nacittā paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappā samaṇabrāhmaṇā tai bhautau puj‍jā tai bhautau pāsaṃsā| sammāsatiṃ cai bhavaṃ dhammapadaṃ garahati paṭik‍kausati, yai ca hi muṭṭhassatī asampajānā samaṇabrāhmaṇā tai bhautau puj‍jā tai bhautau pāsaṃsā| sammāsamādhiṃ cai bhavaṃ dhammapadaṃ garahati paṭik‍kausati, yai ca hi asamāhitā vibbhantacittā samaṇabrāhmaṇā tai bhautau puj‍jā tai bhautau pāsaṃsā|

‘‘yau  khau, paribbājakā, imāni cattāri dhammapadāni garahitabbaṃ paṭik‍kausitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya, tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai imai cattārau sahadhammikā vādānupātā gārayhā ṭhānā āgacchanti| yaipi tai paribbājakā ahaisuṃ uk‍kalā vassabhañ‍ñā vassabhiñ‍ñā (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.62 passitabbaṃ ahaitukavādā akiriyavādā natthikavādā, taipi imāni cattāri dhammapadāni na garahitabbaṃ na paṭik‍kausitabbaṃ amañ‍ñiṃsu| taṃ kissa haitu? nindābyārausanaupārambhabhayā’’ti upavādabhayāti (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.150; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.62 passitabbaṃ|

‘‘abyāpan‍nau sadā satau, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitau|

abhijjhāvinayai sikkhaṃ, appamattauti vuc‍catī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

uruvailavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai uruvailā laukau kāḷakau kauḷikau (ka॰), brahmacariyaina pañ‍camaṃ|

kuhaṃ santuṭṭhi vaṃsau ca, dhammapadaṃ paribbājakaina cāti||


4. cak‍kavaggau

1. cak‍kasuttaṃ

31. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, cak‍kāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatānaṃ daivamanussānaṃ catucak‍kaṃ vattati, yaihi saman‍nāgatā daivamanussā nacirassaiva mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇanti bhaugaisu| katamāni cattāri? patirūpadaisavāsau, sappurisāvassayau sappurisūpassayau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), attasammāpaṇidhi, pubbai ca katapuñ‍ñatā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri cak‍kāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatānaṃ daivamanussānaṃ catucak‍kaṃ vattati, yaihi saman‍nāgatā daivamanussā nacirassaiva mahantattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇanti bhaugaisū’’ti|

‘‘patirūpai  vasai daisai, ariyamittakarau siyā|

sammāpaṇidhisampan‍nau, pubbai puñ‍ñakatau narau|

dhañ‍ñaṃ dhanaṃ yasau kitti, sukhañ‍caitaṃdhivattatī’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. saṅgahasuttaṃ

32. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, saṅgahavatthūni| katamāni cattāri? dānaṃ, paiyyavaj‍jaṃ, atthacariyā, samānattatā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri saṅgahavatthūnī’’ti|

‘‘dānañ‍ca paiyyavaj‍jañ‍ca saṅgahai (aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ) dī॰ ni॰ 3.273 passitabbaṃ, atthacariyā ca yā idha|

samānattatā ca dhammaisu, tattha tattha yathārahaṃ|

aitai khau saṅgahā laukai, rathassāṇīva yāyatau||

‘‘aitai ca saṅgahā nāssu, na mātā puttakāraṇā|

labhaitha mānaṃ pūjaṃ vā, pitā vā puttakāraṇā||

‘‘yasmā  ca saṅgahā saṅgahai (aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ) dī॰ ni॰ 3.273 passitabbaṃ aitai, samavaikkhanti paṇḍitā|

tasmā mahattaṃ pappaunti, pāsaṃsā ca bhavanti tai’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. sīhasuttaṃ

33. ‘‘sīhau, bhikkhavai, migarājā sāyanhasamayaṃ āsayā nikkhamati| āsayā nikkhamitvā vijambhati| vijambhitvā samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaiti| samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaitvā tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadati| tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ naditvā gaucarāya pak‍kamati| yai khau pana tai, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā sīhassa migarañ‍ñau nadatau saddaṃ suṇanti, tai yaibhuyyaina bhayaṃ saṃvaigaṃ santāsaṃ āpaj‍janti| bilaṃ bilāsayā pavisanti, dakaṃ dakāsayā udakaṃ udakāsayā (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.78; paṭi॰ ma॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.37 passitabbaṃ pavisanti, vanaṃ vanāsayā pavisanti, ākāsaṃ pakkhinau bhajanti| yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgā gāmanigamarājadhānīsu daḷhaihi varattaihi bandhanaihi baddhā, taipi tāni bandhanāni sañchinditvā sampadālaitvā bhītā muttakarīsaṃ cajamānā yaina vā taina vā palāyanti| aivaṃ mahiddhikau khau, bhikkhavai, sīhau migarājā tiracchānagatānaṃ pāṇānaṃ, aivaṃ mahaisakkhau aivaṃ mahānubhāvau|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yadā tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā, sau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘iti sak‍kāyau, iti sak‍kāyasamudayau, iti sak‍kāyaniraudhau sak‍kāyassa atthaṅgamau (aṭṭha॰, saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.78) ‘‘sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī’’ti pacchimapadaṃ pana passitabbaṃ, iti sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti| yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā uc‍caisu vimānaisu ciraṭṭhitikā, taipi tathāgatassa dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā yaibhuyyaina bhayaṃ saṃvaigaṃ santāsaṃ āpaj‍janti – ‘anic‍cā vata kira, bhau, mayaṃ samānā nic‍camhāti amañ‍ñimha; addhuvā vata kira, bhau, mayaṃ samānā dhuvamhāti amañ‍ñimha; asassatā vata kira, bhau, mayaṃ samānā sassatamhāti amañ‍ñimha| mayaṃ kira, bhau, anic‍cā addhuvā asassatā sak‍kāyapariyāpan‍nā’ti| aivaṃ mahiddhikau khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sadaivakassa laukassa, aivaṃ mahaisakkhau aivaṃ mahānubhāvau’’ti|

‘‘yadā  buddhau abhiñ‍ñāya, dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattayī|

sadaivakassa laukassa, satthā appaṭipuggalau||

‘‘sak‍kāyañ‍ca  niraudhañ‍ca, sak‍kāyassa ca sambhavaṃ|

ariyañ‍caṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ||

‘‘yaipi dīghāyukā daivā, vaṇṇavantau yasassinau|

bhītā santāsamāpāduṃ, sīhassaivi’taraimigā||

‘‘avītivattā sak‍kāyaṃ, anic‍cā kira bhau mayaṃ|

sutvā arahatau vākyaṃ, vippamuttassa tādinau’’ti saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.78|| tatiyaṃ|

4. aggappasādasuttaṃ

34. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, aggappasādā| katamai cattārau? yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattā apadā vā dvipadā dipadā (sī॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.32; itivu॰ 90 vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā rūpinau vā arūpinau vā sañ‍ñinau vā asañ‍ñinau vā naivasañ‍ñināsañ‍ñinau vā, tathāgatau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| yai, bhikkhavai, buddhai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā| aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hauti|

‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, dhammā saṅkhatā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati| yai, bhikkhavai, ariyai aṭṭhaṅgikai maggai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā| aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hauti|

‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, dhammā saṅkhatā vā asaṅkhatā vā, virāgau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ madanimmadanau pipāsavinayau ālayasamugghātau vaṭṭupacchaidau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| yai, bhikkhavai, virāgai dhammai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā| aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hauti|

‘‘yāvatā, bhikkhavai, saṅghā vā gaṇā vā, tathāgatasāvakasaṅghau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| yai, bhikkhavai, saṅghai pasan‍nā, aggai tai pasan‍nā| aggai khau pana pasan‍nānaṃ aggau vipākau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau aggappasādā’’ti|

‘‘aggatau vai pasan‍nānaṃ, aggaṃ dhammaṃ vijānataṃ|

aggai buddhai pasan‍nānaṃ, dakkhiṇaiyyai anuttarai||

‘‘aggai dhammai pasan‍nānaṃ, virāgūpasamai sukhai|

aggai saṅghai pasan‍nānaṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai||

‘‘aggasmiṃ dānaṃ dadataṃ, aggaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati|

aggaṃ āyu ca vaṇṇau ca, yasau kitti sukhaṃ balaṃ||

‘‘aggassa dātā maidhāvī, aggadhammasamāhitau|

daivabhūtau manussau vā, aggappattau pamaudatī’’ti itivu॰ 90|| catutthaṃ|

5. vassakārasuttaṃ

35. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘catūhi khau mayaṃ, bhau gautama, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ mahāpañ‍ñaṃ mahāpurisaṃ pañ‍ñāpaima| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhau gautama, bahussutau hauti tassa tassaiva sutajātassa tassa tassaiva khau pana bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti – ‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau, ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa  atthau’ti| satimā khau pana hauti cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritā yāni khau pana tāni gahaṭṭhakāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni, tattha dakkhau hauti analasau, tatrupāyāya vīmaṃsāya saman‍nāgatau alaṃ kātuṃ alaṃ saṃvidhātuṃ| imaihi khau mayaṃ, bhau gautama, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ mahāpañ‍ñaṃ mahāpurisaṃ pañ‍ñāpaima| sacai mai sacai pana mai (sī॰ ka॰), sacai mai pana (pī॰), bhau gautama, anumauditabbaṃ anumaudatu mai bhavaṃ gautamau; sacai  pana mai, bhau gautama, paṭik‍kausitabbaṃ paṭik‍kausatu mai bhavaṃ gautamau’’ti|

‘‘naiva khau tyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, anumaudāmi na paṭik‍kausāmi| catūhi khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ mahāpañ‍ñaṃ mahāpurisaṃ pañ‍ñāpaimi| katamaihi catūhi? idha, brāhmaṇa, bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanasukhāya; bahussa janatā ariyai ñāyai patiṭṭhāpitā, yadidaṃ kalyāṇadhammatā kusaladhammatā| sau yaṃ vitak‍kaṃ ākaṅkhati vitak‍kaituṃ taṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaiti, yaṃ vitak‍kaṃ nākaṅkhati vitak‍kaituṃ na taṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaiti; yaṃ saṅkappaṃ ākaṅkhati saṅkappaituṃ taṃ saṅkappaṃ saṅkappaiti, yaṃ saṅkappaṃ nākaṅkhati saṅkappaituṃ na taṃ saṅkappaṃ saṅkappaiti| iti caitauvasippattau hauti vitak‍kapathai| catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai  sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| naiva khau tyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, anumaudāmi na pana paṭik‍kausāmi| imaihi khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ mahāpañ‍ñaṃ mahāpurisaṃ pañ‍ñāpaimī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ bhautā gautamaina| imaihi ca mayaṃ, bhau gautama, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dhāraima; bhavañhi gautamau bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nau bahujanasukhāya; bahu tai bahussa (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) janatā ariyai ñāyai patiṭṭhāpitā, yadidaṃ kalyāṇadhammatā kusaladhammatā| bhavañhi gautamau yaṃ vitak‍kaṃ ākaṅkhati vitak‍kaituṃ taṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaiti, yaṃ vitak‍kaṃ nākaṅkhati vitak‍kaituṃ na taṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaiti, yaṃ saṅkappaṃ ākaṅkhati saṅkappaituṃ taṃ saṅkappaṃ saṅkappaiti, yaṃ saṅkappaṃ nākaṅkhati saṅkappaituṃ na taṃ saṅkappaṃ saṅkappaiti| bhavañhi gautamau caitauvasippattau vitak‍kapathai| bhavañhi gautamau catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| bhavañhi gautamau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti|

‘‘addhā  khau tyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, āsaj‍ja upanīya vācā bhāsitā| api ca, tyāhaṃ byākarissāmi – ‘ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nau bahujanasukhāya; bahu mai bahussa (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) janatā ariyai ñāyai patiṭṭhāpitā, yadidaṃ kalyāṇadhammatā kusaladhammatā| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, yaṃ vitak‍kaṃ ākaṅkhāmi vitak‍kaituṃ taṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaimi, yaṃ vitak‍kaṃ nākaṅkhāmi vitak‍kaituṃ na taṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaimi, yaṃ saṅkappaṃ ākaṅkhāmi saṅkappaituṃ taṃ saṅkappaṃ saṅkappaimi, yaṃ saṅkappaṃ nākaṅkhāmi saṅkappaituṃ na taṃ saṅkappaṃ saṅkappaimi| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, caitauvasippattau vitak‍kapathai| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’’’ti|

‘‘yau vaidi sabbasattānaṃ, mac‍cupāsappamaucanaṃ|

hitaṃ daivamanussānaṃ, ñāyaṃ dhammaṃ pakāsayi|

yaṃ vai disvā ca sutvā ca, pasīdanti bahū janā pasīdati bahuj‍janau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

‘‘maggāmaggassa kusalau, katakic‍cau anāsavau|

buddhau antimasārīrau antimadhāritau (ka॰), ‘‘(mahāpañ‍ñau) ( ) syā॰ pautthakai natthi mahāpurisauti vuc‍catī’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dauṇasuttaṃ

36. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā antarā ca uk‍kaṭṭhaṃ antarā ca saitabyaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti| dauṇaupi sudaṃ brāhmaṇau antarā ca uk‍kaṭṭhaṃ antarā ca saitabyaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti| addasā khau dauṇau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pādaisu cak‍kāni sahassārāni sanaimikāni sanābhikāni sabbākāraparipūrāni; disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! na vatimāni manussabhūtassa padāni bhavissantī’’ti!! atha  khau bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi  pal‍laṅkaṃ  ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| atha khau dauṇau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau padāni anugacchantau addasa bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ pāsādikaṃ pasādanīyaṃ santindriyaṃ santamānasaṃ uttamadamathasamathamanuppattaṃ dantaṃ guttaṃ saṃyatindriyaṃ yatindriyaṃ (mahāva॰ 257) nāgaṃ| disvāna yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘daivau nau bhavaṃ bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, daivau bhavissāmī’’ti| ‘‘gandhabbau nau bhavaṃ bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, gandhabbau bhavissāmī’’ti| ‘‘yakkhau nau bhavaṃ bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yakkhau bhavissāmī’’ti| ‘‘manussau nau bhavaṃ bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, manussau bhavissāmī’’ti|

‘‘‘daivau nau bhavaṃ bhavissatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, daivau bhavissāmī’ti vadaisi| ‘gandhabbau nau bhavaṃ bhavissatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, gandhabbau bhavissāmī’ti vadaisi| ‘yakkhau nau bhavaṃ bhavissatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yakkhau bhavissāmī’ti vadaisi| ‘manussau nau bhavaṃ bhavissatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, manussau bhavissāmī’ti vadaisi| atha kau carahi bhavaṃ bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, āsavānaṃ appahīnattā daivau bhavaiyyaṃ, tai mai āsavā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yaisaṃ khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, āsavānaṃ  appahīnattā gandhabbau bhavaiyyaṃ… yakkhau bhavaiyyaṃ… manussau bhavaiyyaṃ, tai mai āsavā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, uppalaṃ vā padumaṃ vā puṇḍarīkaṃ vā udakai jātaṃ udakai saṃvaḍḍhaṃ udakā ac‍cuggamma tiṭṭhati anupalittaṃ  udakaina; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, laukai jātau laukai saṃvaḍḍhau laukaṃ abhibhuyya viharāmi anupalittau laukaina| buddhauti maṃ, brāhmaṇa, dhāraihī’’ti|

‘‘yaina  daivūpapatyassa, gandhabbau vā vihaṅgamau|

yakkhattaṃ yaina gacchaiyyaṃ, manussattañ‍ca abbajai|

tai mayhaṃ, āsavā khīṇā, viddhastā vinaḷīkatā||

‘‘puṇḍarīkaṃ  yathā vaggu, tauyaina nupalippati nupalimpati (ka॰)|

nupalippāmi nupalimpāmi (ka॰) laukaina, tasmā buddhausmi brāhmaṇā’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. aparihāniyasuttaṃ

37. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau parihānāya nibbānassaiva santikai| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti, indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ; cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā  anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati manindriyaṃ; manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya; yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya| iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati, anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’ti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti, pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya, satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā; rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau parihānāya, nibbānassaiva santikaiti|

‘‘sīlai patiṭṭhitau bhikkhu, indriyaisu ca saṃvutau|

bhaujanamhi ca mattañ‍ñū, jāgariyaṃ anuyuñ‍jati||

‘‘aivaṃ vihārī ātāpī, ahaurattamatanditau|

bhāvayaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ, yaugakkhaimassa pattiyā||

‘‘appamādaratau bhikkhu, pamādai bhayadassi vā bhayadassāvī (ka॰) dha॰ pa॰ 32 dhammapadaipi|

abhabbau parihānāya, nibbānassaiva santikai’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. patilīnasuttaṃ

38. ‘‘panuṇṇapac‍caikasac‍cau  paṇun‍napac‍caikasac‍cau (?), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘samavayasaṭṭhaisanau passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau patilīnau’ti vuc‍cati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu panuṇṇapac‍caikasac‍cau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yāni tāni puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ  puthupac‍caikasac‍cāni, saiyyathidaṃ – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā, antavā laukauti vā, anantavā laukauti vā, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, naiva hauti na  na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā; sabbāni tāni nuṇṇāni haunti panuṇṇāni cattāni vantāni muttāni pahīnāni paṭinissaṭṭhāni| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu panuṇṇapac‍caikasac‍cau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhaisanau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kāmaisanā pahīnā hauti, bhavaisanā pahīnā hauti, brahmacariyaisanā paṭippassaddhā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samavayasaṭṭhaisanau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu patilīnau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau asmimānau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu patilīnau hauti| panuṇṇapac‍caikasac‍cau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘samavayasaṭṭhaisanau passaddhakāyasaṅkhārau patilīnau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti|

itivu॰ 55 itivuttakaipi ‘‘kāmaisanā  bhavaisanā, brahmacariyaisanā saha|

iti sac‍caparāmāsau, diṭṭhiṭṭhānā samussayā||

itivu॰ 55 itivuttakaipi ‘‘sabbarāgavirattassa, taṇhakkhayavimuttinau|

aisanā paṭinissaṭṭhā, diṭṭhiṭṭhānā samūhatā||

‘‘sa vai santau satau bhikkhu, passaddhau aparājitau|

mānābhisamayā buddhau, patilīnauti vuc‍catī’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. uj‍jayasuttaṃ

39. atha  khau uj‍jayau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau uj‍jayau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhavampi nau gautamau yañ‍ñaṃ vaṇṇaitī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ vaṇṇaimi; na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ na vaṇṇaimi| yathārūpai khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñai gāvau hañ‍ñanti, ajaiḷakā hañ‍ñanti, kuk‍kuṭasūkarā hañ‍ñanti, vividhā pāṇā saṅghātaṃ āpaj‍janti; aivarūpaṃ khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sārambhaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ na vaṇṇaimi| taṃ kissa haitu? aivarūpañhi, brāhmaṇa, sārambhaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ na upasaṅkamanti arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā|

‘‘yathārūpai ca khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñai naiva gāvau hañ‍ñanti, na ajaiḷakā hañ‍ñanti, na kuk‍kuṭasūkarā hañ‍ñanti, na vividhā pāṇā saṅghātaṃ āpaj‍janti; aivarūpaṃ khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, nirārambhaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ vaṇṇaimi, yadidaṃ nic‍cadānaṃ anukulayañ‍ñaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? aivarūpañhi, brāhmaṇa, nirārambhaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ upasaṅkamanti arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā’’ti|

‘‘assamaidhaṃ purisamaidhaṃ, sammāpāsaṃ vājapaiyyaṃ niraggaḷaṃ|

mahāyañ‍ñā mahārambhā sammāpāsaṃ vājapaiyyaṃ| niraggaḷaṃ mahārambhā (pī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.120, na tai haunti mahapphalā||

‘‘ajaiḷakā ca gāvau ca, vividhā yattha hañ‍ñarai|

na taṃ sammaggatā yañ‍ñaṃ, upayanti mahaisinau||

‘‘yai  ca yañ‍ñā nirārambhā, yajanti anukulaṃ sadā|

ajaiḷakā ca gāvau ca, vividhā naittha hañ‍ñarai nājaiḷakā ca gāvau ca, vividhā yattha hañ‍ñarai (syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

tañ‍ca sammaggatā yañ‍ñaṃ, upayanti mahaisinau||

‘‘aitaṃ  aivaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) yajaitha maidhāvī, aisau yañ‍ñau mahapphalau|

aitaṃ aivaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) hi yajamānassa, saiyyau hauti na pāpiyau|

yañ‍ñau ca vipulau hauti, pasīdanti ca daivatā’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. udāyīsuttaṃ

40. atha khau udāyī udāyi (sabbattha) brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā  …pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau udāyī brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhavampi nau gautamau yañ‍ñaṃ vaṇṇaitī’’ti? ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ vaṇṇaimi; na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ na vaṇṇaimi| yathārūpai khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñai gāvau hañ‍ñanti, ajaiḷakā hañ‍ñanti, kuk‍kuṭasūkarā hañ‍ñanti, vividhā pāṇā saṅghātaṃ āpaj‍janti; aivarūpaṃ khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sārambhaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ na vaṇṇaimi| taṃ kissa haitu? aivarūpañhi, brāhmaṇa, sārambhaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ na upasaṅkamanti arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā|

‘‘yathārūpai ca khau, brāhmaṇa, yañ‍ñai naiva gāvau hañ‍ñanti, na ajaiḷakā hañ‍ñanti, na kuk‍kuṭasūkarā hañ‍ñanti, na vividhā pāṇā saṅghātaṃ āpaj‍janti; aivarūpaṃ khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, nirārambhaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ vaṇṇaimi, yadidaṃ nic‍cadānaṃ anukulayañ‍ñaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? aivarūpañhi, brāhmaṇa, nirārambhaṃ yañ‍ñaṃ upasaṅkamanti arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā’’ti|

‘‘abhisaṅkhataṃ nirārambhaṃ, yañ‍ñaṃ kālaina kappiyaṃ|

tādisaṃ  upasaṃyanti, sañ‍ñatā brahmacārayau||

‘‘vivaṭacchadā vivattacchadā (sī॰ pī॰), vivaṭṭacchadā (ka॰) yai laukai, vītivattā kulaṃ gatiṃ|

yañ‍ñamaitaṃ pasaṃsanti, buddhā yañ‍ñassa puñ‍ñassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kauvidā||

‘‘yañ‍ñai vā yadi vā saddhai, habyaṃ havyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), huñ‍ñaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) katvā yathārahaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau yajati pasan‍nacittā yajanti (ka॰), sukhaittai brahmacārisu||

‘‘suhutaṃ  suyiṭṭhaṃ suppattaṃ sampattaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), dakkhiṇaiyyaisu yaṃ kataṃ|

yañ‍ñau ca vipulau hauti, pasīdanti ca daivatā||

‘‘aivaṃ aitaṃ (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.37 yajitvā maidhāvī, saddhau muttaina caitasā|

abyābajjhaṃ sukhaṃ laukaṃ, paṇḍitau upapaj‍jatī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

cak‍kavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

cak‍kau  saṅgahau sīhau, pasādau vassakāraina pañ‍camaṃ|

dauṇau aparihāniyau patilīnau, uj‍jayau udāyinā tai dasāti||


5. rauhitassavaggau

1. samādhibhāvanāsuttaṃ

41. ‘‘catassau  imā, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā| katamā catassau? atthi, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya saṃvattati; atthi, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ñāṇadassanappaṭilābhāya saṃvattati; atthi, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satisampajañ‍ñāya saṃvattati; atthi, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ñāṇadassanappaṭilābhāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ālaukasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karauti, divāsañ‍ñaṃ adhiṭṭhāti – yathā divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā  rattiṃ tathā divā| iti vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ñāṇadassanappaṭilābhāya saṃvattati|
