‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satisampajañ‍ñāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau viditā vaidanā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ  gacchanti; viditā sañ‍ñā…pai॰… viditā vitak‍kā uppaj‍janti, viditā upaṭṭhahanti, viditā abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satisampajañ‍ñāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassī viharati – ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau atthagamau (sī॰ pī॰); iti vaidanā, iti vaidanāya samudayau, iti vaidanāya atthaṅgamau; iti sañ‍ñā, iti sañ‍ñāya samudayau, iti sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau; iti saṅkhārā, iti saṅkhārānaṃ samudayau, iti saṅkhārānaṃ atthaṅgamau; iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, samādhibhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā āsavānaṃ khayāya saṃvattati| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau samādhibhāvanā| idañ‍ca pana maitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ pārāyanai puṇṇakapañhai –

‘‘saṅkhāya laukasmiṃ parauparāni,

yassiñ‍jitaṃ natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai|

santau  vidhūmau anīghau nirāsau,

atāri  sau jātijaranti brūmī’’ti su॰ ni॰ 1054; cūḷani॰ puṇṇakamāṇavapucchā 73|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pañhabyākaraṇasuttaṃ

42. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, pañhabyākaraṇāni pañhābyākaraṇāni (ka॰)| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, pañhau aikaṃsabyākaraṇīyau; atthi, bhikkhavai, pañhau vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyau; atthi, bhikkhavai, pañhau paṭipucchābyākaraṇīyau; atthi, bhikkhavai, pañhau ṭhapanīyau| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri pañhabyākaraṇānī’’ti|

‘‘aikaṃsavacanaṃ aikaṃ, vibhaj‍javacanāparaṃ|

tatiyaṃ paṭipucchaiyya, catutthaṃ pana ṭhāpayai||

‘‘yau  ca taisaṃ naisaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) tattha tattha, jānāti anudhammataṃ|

catupañhassa kusalau, āhu bhikkhuṃ tathāvidhaṃ||

‘‘durāsadau duppasahau, gambhīrau duppadhaṃsiyau|

athau atthai anatthai ca, ubhayassa hauti kauvidau ubhayatthassa kauvidau (syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

‘‘anatthaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, atthaṃ gaṇhāti paṇḍitau|

atthābhisamayā dhīrau, paṇḍitauti pavuc‍catī’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamakaudhagarusuttaṃ

43. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? kaudhagaru na saddhammagaru, makkhagaru na saddhammagaru, lābhagaru na saddhammagaru, sak‍kāragaru na saddhammagaru| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? saddhammagaru na kaudhagaru, saddhammagaru na makkhagaru, saddhammagaru na lābhagaru, saddhammagaru na sak‍kāragaru| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti|

‘‘kaudhamakkhagarū bhikkhū, lābhasak‍kāragāravā|

na tai dhammai virūhanti, sammāsambuddhadaisitai||

‘‘yai  ca saddhammagarunau, vihaṃsu viharanti ca|

tai vai dhammai virūhanti, sammāsambuddhadaisitai’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyakaudhagarusuttaṃ

44. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, asaddhammā| katamai cattārau? kaudhagarutā na saddhammagarutā, makkhagarutā na saddhammagarutā, lābhagarutā na saddhammagarutā, sak‍kāragarutā na saddhammagarutā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau asaddhammā|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, saddhammā| katamai cattārau? saddhammagarutā na kaudhagarutā, saddhammagarutā na makkhagarutā, saddhammagarutā na lābhagarutā, saddhammagarutā na sak‍kāragarutā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau saddhammā’’ti|

‘‘kaudhamakkhagaru bhikkhu, lābhasak‍kāragāravau|

sukhaittai pūtibījaṃva, saddhammai na virūhati||

‘‘yai ca saddhammagarunau, vihaṃsu viharanti ca|

tai vai dhammai virūhanti, snaihānvayamivausadhā’’ti snaihamanvāyamivausadhāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|| catutthaṃ|

5. rauhitassasuttaṃ

45. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.107 sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau rauhitassau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau rauhitassau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘yattha nu khau, bhantai, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na  cavati na upapaj‍jati, sak‍kā nu khau sau, bhantai, gamanaina laukassa antau ñātuṃ vā daṭṭhuṃ vā pāpuṇituṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na  jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva subhāsitamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā – ‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmī’’’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbāhaṃ, bhantai, rauhitassau nāma isi ahausiṃ bhaujaputtau iddhimā vaihāsaṅgamau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpau javau ahausi, saiyyathāpi nāma daḷhadhammā daḷhadhammau, a॰ ni॰ 9.38; ma॰ ni॰ 1.161 (sabbattha) ṭīkā ca mauggal‍lānabyākaraṇaṃ ca aulaukaitabbaṃ dhanuggahau sikkhitau katahatthau katūpāsanau lahukaina asanaina appakasiraina tiriyaṃ tālacchāyaṃ atipātaiyya| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpau padavītihārau ahausi, saiyyathāpi nāma puratthimā samuddā pacchimau samuddau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpaina javaina saman‍nāgatassa aivarūpaina ca padavītihāraina aivarūpaṃ icchāgataṃ uppaj‍ji – ‘ahaṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ pāpuṇissāmī’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatraiva asitapītakhāyitasāyitā añ‍ñatra uc‍cārapassāvakammā añ‍ñatra niddākilamathapaṭivinaudanā vassasatāyukau vassasatajīvī vassasataṃ gantvā appatvāva laukassa  antaṃ antarāyaiva kālaṅkatau|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva subhāsitamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā – ‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmī’’’ti|

‘‘‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyya’nti vadāmi| na cāhaṃ, āvusau, appatvāva laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmi| api cāhaṃ, āvusau, imasmiṃyaiva byāmamattai kaḷaivarai kaḷaibarai (sī॰ pī॰) sasañ‍ñimhi samanakai laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñāpaimi laukasamudayañ‍ca laukaniraudhañ‍ca laukaniraudhagāminiñ‍ca paṭipada’’nti|

‘‘gamanaina  na pattabbau, laukassantau kudācanaṃ|

na ca appatvā laukantaṃ, dukkhā atthi pamaucanaṃ||

‘‘tasmā  havai laukavidū sumaidhau,

laukantagū vusitabrahmacariyau|

laukassa antaṃ samitāvi ñatvā,

nāsīsatī nāsiṃsatī (sī॰) laukamimaṃ parañ‍cā’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyarauhitassasuttaṃ

46. atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ rauhitassau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, bhikkhavai, rauhitassau daivaputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘yattha nu khau, bhantai saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.107, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, sak‍kā nu khau sau, bhantai, gamanaina laukassa antau ñātuṃ vā daṭṭhuṃ vā pāpuṇituṃ vā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, rauhitassaṃ daivaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmī’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, rauhitassau daivaputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva subhāsitamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā – yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmi’’’|

‘‘bhūtapubbāhaṃ, bhantai, rauhitassau nāma isi ahausiṃ bhaujaputtau iddhimā vaihāsaṅgamau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpau javau ahausi, saiyyathāpi nāma daḷhadhammā dhanuggahau sikkhitau katahatthau katūpāsanau lahukaina asanaina appakasiraina tiriyaṃ tālacchāyaṃ atipātaiyya| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpau padavītihārau ahausi, saiyyathāpi nāma puratthimā samuddā pacchimau samuddau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpaina javaina saman‍nāgatassa aivarūpaina ca padavītihāraina aivarūpaṃ icchāgataṃ uppaj‍ji – ahaṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ pāpuṇissāmī’’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatraiva asitapītakhāyitasāyitā  añ‍ñatra uc‍cārapassāvakammā  añ‍ñatra niddākilamathapaṭivinaudanā  vassasatāyukau vassasatajīvī vassasataṃ gantvā appatvāva laukassa antaṃ antarāyaiva kālaṅkatau|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāva subhāsitamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā – ‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmī’’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, rauhitassaṃ daivaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ –

‘‘‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ, taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmī’ti| na cāhaṃ, āvusau, appatvāva laukassa antaṃ dukkhassantakiriyaṃ vadāmi| api cāhaṃ, āvusau, imasmiṃyaiva byāmamattai kaḷaivarai sasañ‍ñimhi samanakai laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñāpaimi laukasamudayañ‍ca laukaniraudhañ‍ca laukaniraudhagāminiñ‍ca paṭipada’’nti|

‘‘gamanaina na pattabbau, laukassantau kudācanaṃ|

na ca appatvā laukantaṃ, dukkhā atthi pamaucanaṃ||

‘‘tasmā havai laukavidū sumaidhau,

laukantagū vusitabrahmacariyau|

laukassa antaṃ samitāvi ñatvā,

nāsīsatī laukamimaṃ parañ‍cā’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. suvidūrasuttaṃ

47. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, suvidūravidūrāni| katamāni cattāri? nabhañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pathavī ca; idaṃ paṭhamaṃ suvidūravidūrai| aurimañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, tīraṃ samuddassa pārimañ‍ca; idaṃ dutiyaṃ suvidūravidūrai| yatau ca, bhikkhavai, vairaucanau abbhudaiti  yattha ca atthamaiti atthaṅgamaiti (syā॰), vaiti (ka॰); idaṃ tatiyaṃ suvidūravidūrai| satañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammau asatañ‍ca dhammau; idaṃ catutthaṃ suvidūravidūrai| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri suvidūravidūrānī’’ti|

jā॰ 2.21.414, 448 ‘‘nabhañ‍ca  dūrai pathavī ca dūrai,

pāraṃ samuddassa tadāhu dūrai|

yatau ca vairaucanau abbhudaiti,

pabhaṅkarau yattha ca atthamaiti|

tatau havai dūrataraṃ vadanti,

satañ‍ca dhammaṃ asatañ‍ca dhammaṃ||

‘‘abyāyikau hauti sataṃ samāgamau,

yāvāpi yāvampi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tiṭṭhaiyya tathaiva hauti|

khippañhi vaiti asataṃ samāgamau,

tasmā sataṃ dhammau asabbhi ārakā’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. visākhasuttaṃ

48. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā visākhau pañ‍cālaputtau pañ‍cāliputtau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti  samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti, pauriyā vācāya vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā pariyāpan‍nāya anissitāya| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina upaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘kau nu khau, bhikkhavai, upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti pauriyā vācāya vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā pariyāpan‍nāya anissitāyā’’ti? ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, visākhau pañ‍cālaputtau upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti pauriyā vācāya vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā pariyāpan‍nāya anissitāyā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ visākhaṃ pañ‍cālaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu sādhu, visākha! sādhu  khau tvaṃ, visākha, bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi pauriyā vācāya vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā pariyāpan‍nāya anissitāyāti|

‘‘nābhāsamānaṃ jānanti, missaṃ bālaihi paṇḍitaṃ|

bhāsamānañ‍ca jānanti, daisaintaṃ amataṃ padaṃ||

‘‘bhāsayai jautayai dhammaṃ, paggaṇhai isinaṃ dhajaṃ|

subhāsitadhajā isayau, dhammau hi isinaṃ dhajau’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vipal‍lāsasuttaṃ

49. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsā cittavipal‍lāsā diṭṭhivipal‍lāsā| katamai cattārau? anic‍cai, bhikkhavai, nic‍canti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau; dukkhai, bhikkhavai, sukhanti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau; anattani, bhikkhavai, attāti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau; asubhai, bhikkhavai, subhanti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsā cittavipal‍lāsā diṭṭhivipal‍lāsā|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsā nacittavipal‍lāsā nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsā| katamai cattārau? anic‍cai, bhikkhavai, anic‍canti nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau nacittavipal‍lāsau nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsau; dukkhai, bhikkhavai, dukkhanti nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau nacittavipal‍lāsau nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsau; anattani, bhikkhavai, anattāti nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau nacittavipal‍lāsau nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsau; asubhai, bhikkhavai, asubhanti nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau nacittavipal‍lāsau nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau nasañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsā nacittavipal‍lāsā nadiṭṭhivipal‍lāsā’’ti|

‘‘anic‍cai  nic‍casañ‍ñinau, dukkhai ca sukhasañ‍ñinau|

anattani ca attāti, asubhai subhasañ‍ñinau|

micchādiṭṭhihatā sattā, khittacittā visañ‍ñinau||

‘‘tai yaugayuttā mārassa, ayaugakkhaiminau janā|

sattā gacchanti saṃsāraṃ, jātimaraṇagāminau||

‘‘yadā ca buddhā laukasmiṃ, uppaj‍janti pabhaṅkarā|

tai  imaṃ dhammaṃ taimaṃ dhammaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) pakāsainti, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ||

‘‘taisaṃ sutvāna sappañ‍ñā, sacittaṃ pac‍caladdhā tai|

anic‍caṃ anic‍catau dakkhuṃ, dukkhamaddakkhu dukkhatau||

‘‘anattani anattāti, asubhaṃ asubhataddasuṃ|

sammādiṭṭhisamādānā, sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ upac‍cagu’’nti paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.236|| navamaṃ|

10. upak‍kilaisasuttaṃ

50. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai cūḷava॰ 447, candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti| katamai cattārau? abbhā, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisā, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti|

‘‘mahikā, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisā, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti|

‘‘dhūmau rajau, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti|

‘‘rāhu, bhikkhavai, asurindau candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattāraumai samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti| katamai cattārau? santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā suraṃ pivanti mairayaṃ, surāmairayapānā appaṭiviratā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanti, maithunasmā dhammā appaṭiviratā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā jātarūparajataṃ sādiyanti, jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā micchājīvaina jīvanti, micchājīvā appaṭiviratā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti na bhāsanti na viraucantī’’ti|

‘‘rāgadausaparik‍kiṭṭhā, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

avij‍jānivutā pausā, piyarūpābhinandinau||

‘‘suraṃ  pivanti mairayaṃ, paṭisaivanti maithunaṃ|

rajataṃ  jātarūpañ‍ca, sādiyanti aviddasū|

micchājīvaina jīvanti, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā||

‘‘aitai  upak‍kilaisā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā (sī॰ pī॰), aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

na tapanti na bhāsanti, asuddhā sarajā magā||

‘‘andhakāraina aunaddhā, taṇhādāsā sanaittikā|

vaḍḍhainti kaṭasiṃ ghauraṃ, ādiyanti punabbhava’’nti|| dasamaṃ|

rauhitassavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

samādhipañhā dvai kaudhā, rauhitassāparai duvai|

suvidūravisākhavipal‍lāsā, upak‍kilaisaina tai dasāti||

paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



2. dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(6) 1. puñ‍ñābhisandavaggau

1. paṭhamapuñ‍ñābhisandasuttaṃ

51. sāvatthinidānaṃ| cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā sauvaggikā sukhavipākā saggasaṃvattanikā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? yassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jamānau appamāṇaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, appamāṇau tassa puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘yassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jamānau appamāṇaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, appamāṇau tassa puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘yassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saināsanaṃ paribhuñ‍jamānau appamāṇaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, appamāṇau tassa puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘yassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍jamānau appamāṇaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, appamāṇau tassa puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau  sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā sauvaggikā sukhavipākā saggasaṃvattanikā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘imaihi ca pana, bhikkhavai, catūhi puñ‍ñābhisandaihi kusalābhisandaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gahaituṃ gaṇaituṃ (ka॰) – ‘aittakau  puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau  iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattatī’ti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau asaṅkhaiyyau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhyaṃ saṅkhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) gacchati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai na sukaraṃ udakassa pamāṇaṃ gahaituṃ – ‘aittakāni udakāḷhakānīti vā, aittakāni udakāḷhakasatānīti vā, aittakāni udakāḷhakasahassānīti vā, aittakāni udakāḷhakasatasahassānīti vā’, atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāudakakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imaihi catūhi puñ‍ñābhisandaihi kusalābhisandaihi saman‍nāgatassa ariyasāvakassa na sukaraṃ puñ‍ñassa pamāṇaṃ gahaituṃ – ‘aittakau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattatī’ti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāpuñ‍ñakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

‘‘mahaudadhiṃ aparimitaṃ mahāsaraṃ,

bahubhairavaṃ ratanavarānamālayaṃ ratanagaṇānamālayaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

naj‍jau yathā naragaṇasaṅghasaivitā macchagaṇasaṃghasaivitā (syā॰ kaṃ॰),

puthū  savantī upayanti sāgaraṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ naraṃ an‍nadapānavatthadaṃ an‍napānavatthaṃ (ka॰),

saiyyānisaj‍jattharaṇassa dāyakaṃ|

puñ‍ñassa dhārā upayanti paṇḍitaṃ,

naj‍jau yathā vārivahāva sāgara’’nti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyapuñ‍ñābhisandasuttaṃ

52. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā sauvaggikā sukhavipākā saggasaṃvattanikā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau  buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā, aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau  saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi  bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau sauvaggikau sukhavipākau saggasaṃvattanikau iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā sauvaggikā sukhavipākā saggasaṃvattanikā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattantī’’ti|

a॰ ni॰ 5.47 ‘‘yassa  saddhā tathāgatai, acalā suppatiṭṭhitā|

sīlañ‍ca yassa kalyāṇaṃ, ariyakantaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ||

‘‘saṅghai pasādau yassatthi, ujubhūtañ‍ca dassanaṃ|

adaliddauti taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

‘‘tasmā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamasaṃvāsasuttaṃ

53. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā antarā ca madhuraṃ antarā ca vairañ‍jaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti| sambahulāpi khau gahapatī ca gahapatāniyau ca antarā ca madhuraṃ antarā ca vairañ‍jaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| atha khau bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai ( ) (pañ‍ñattai āsanai) (pī॰ ka॰) nisīdi| addasaṃsu  khau gahapatī ca gahapatāniyau ca bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ| disvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai gahapatī ca gahapatāniyau ca bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘cattāraumai, gahapatayau, saṃvāsā| katamai cattārau? chavau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati, chavau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati, daivau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati, daivau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, chavau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati? idha, gahapatayau, sāmikau hauti pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī dussīlau pāpadhammau macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ; bhariyāpissa hauti pāṇātipātinī adin‍nādāyinī kāmaisumicchācārinī musāvādinī surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyinī dussīlā pāpadhammā macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati ak‍kausikaparibhāsikā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, chavau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, chavau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati? idha, gahapatayau, sāmikau hauti pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī dussīlau pāpadhammau macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ; bhariyā khvassa hauti pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā musāvādā paṭiviratā surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā  vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati anak‍kausikaparibhāsikā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, chavau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, daivau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati? idha, gahapatayau, sāmikau hauti pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau musāvādā paṭiviratau surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati anak‍kausakaparibhāsakau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ; bhariyā khvassa hauti pāṇātipātinī…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyinī dussīlā pāpadhammā macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati ak‍kausikaparibhāsikā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, daivau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, daivau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati? idha, gahapatayau, sāmikau hauti pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau…pai॰… sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati anak‍kausakaparibhāsakau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ; bhariyāpissa hauti pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati anak‍kausikaparibhāsikā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ| aivaṃ  khau, gahapatayau, daivau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati| imai  khau, gahapatayau, cattārau saṃvāsā’’ti|

‘‘ubhau ca haunti dussīlā, kadariyā paribhāsakā|

tai  haunti jānipatayau, chavā saṃvāsamāgatā||

‘‘sāmikau  hauti dussīlau, kadariyau paribhāsakau|

bhariyā sīlavatī hauti, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharā|

sāpi daivī saṃvasati, chavaina patinā saha||

‘‘sāmikau sīlavā hauti, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharau|

bhariyā hauti dussīlā, kadariyā paribhāsikā|

sāpi chavā saṃvasati, daivaina patinā saha||

‘‘ubhau saddhā vadañ‍ñū ca, sañ‍ñatā dhammajīvinau|

tai haunti jānipatayau, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyaṃvadā||

‘‘atthāsaṃ pacurā haunti, phāsukaṃ phāsattaṃ (sī॰), vāsatthaṃ (pī॰) upajāyati|

amittā dummanā haunti, ubhin‍naṃ samasīlinaṃ||

‘‘idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, samasīlabbatā ubhau|

nandinau daivalaukasmiṃ, maudanti kāmakāminau’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyasaṃvāsasuttaṃ

54. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, saṃvāsā| katamai cattārau? chavau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati, chavau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati, daivau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati, daivau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, chavau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati| idha, bhikkhavai, sāmikau hauti pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī musāvādī pisuṇavācau pharusavācau samphappalāpī abhijjhālu byāpan‍nacittau micchādiṭṭhikau dussīlau pāpadhammau macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau  samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ; bhariyāpissa hauti pāṇātipātinī adin‍nādāyinī kāmaisumicchācārinī musāvādinī pisuṇavācā pharusavācā samphappalāpinī abhijjhālunī byāpan‍nacittā micchādiṭṭhikā dussīlā pāpadhammā macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati  ak‍kausikaparibhāsikā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, chavau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, chavau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati? idha, bhikkhavai, sāmikau hauti pāṇātipātī…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhikau dussīlau pāpadhammau macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ; bhariyā khvassa hauti pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā musāvādā paṭiviratā pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratā pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratā samphappalāpā paṭiviratā anabhijjhālunī abyāpan‍nacittā sammādiṭṭhikā sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati anak‍kausikaparibhāsikā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, chavau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daivau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati? idha, bhikkhavai, sāmikau hauti pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau musāvādā paṭiviratau pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau samphappalāpā paṭiviratau anabhijjhālu abyāpan‍nacittau sammādiṭṭhikau sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati anak‍kausakaparibhāsakau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ; bhariyā  khvassa hauti pāṇātipātinī…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhikā dussīlā pāpadhammā macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati ak‍kausikaparibhāsikā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, daivau chavāya saddhiṃ saṃvasati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daivau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati? idha, bhikkhavai, sāmikau hauti pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhikau sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati anak‍kausakaparibhāsakau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ; bhariyāpissa hauti pāṇātipātā  paṭiviratā…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhikā sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati anak‍kausikaparibhāsikā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, daivau daiviyā saddhiṃ saṃvasati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau saṃvāsā’’ti|

‘‘ubhau ca haunti dussīlā, kadariyā paribhāsakā|

tai  haunti jānipatayau, chavā saṃvāsamāgatā||

‘‘sāmikau  hauti dussīlau, kadariyau paribhāsakau|

bhariyā sīlavatī hauti, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharā|

sāpi daivī saṃvasati, chavaina patinā saha||

‘‘sāmikau sīlavā hauti, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharau|

bhariyā hauti dussīlā, kadariyā paribhāsikā|

sāpi chavā saṃvasati, daivaina patinā saha||

‘‘ubhau saddhā vadañ‍ñū ca, sañ‍ñatā dhammajīvinau|

tai haunti jānipatayau, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyaṃvadā||

‘‘atthāsaṃ pacurā haunti, phāsukaṃ upajāyati|

amittā  dummanā haunti, ubhin‍naṃ samasīlinaṃ||

‘‘idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, samasīlabbatā ubhau|

nandinau daivalaukasmiṃ, maudanti kāmakāminau’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamasamajīvīsuttaṃ

55. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai suṃsumāragirai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhaisakaḷāvanai bhaisakalāvanai (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) migadāyai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina nakulapitunau gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau nakulapitā ca gahapati nakulamātā ca gahapatānī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau nakulapitā gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘yatau mai, bhantai, nakulamātā gahapatānī daharassaiva daharā ānītā, nābhijānāmi nakulamātaraṃ gahapatāniṃ manasāpi aticaritā, kutau pana kāyaina! icchaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhai caiva dhammai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passituṃ abhisamparāyañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passitu’’nti| nakulamātāpi khau gahapatānī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yatauhaṃ, bhantai, nakulapitunau gahapatissa daharassaiva daharā ānītā, nābhijānāmi nakulapitaraṃ gahapatiṃ manasāpi aticaritā, kutau pana kāyaina! icchaiyyāma mayaṃ, bhantai, diṭṭhai caiva dhammai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passituṃ abhisamparāyañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passitu’’nti|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyyuṃ  cai, gahapatayau, ubhau jānipatayau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passituṃ abhisamparāyañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passituṃ ubhauva ubhau ca (sī॰ pī॰) assu  samasaddhā samasīlā samacāgā samapañ‍ñā, tai diṭṭhai caiva dhammai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passanti abhisamparāyañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passantī’’ti passissantīti (ka॰)|

‘‘ubhau saddhā vadañ‍ñū ca, sañ‍ñatā dhammajīvinau|

tai haunti jānipatayau, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyaṃvadā||

‘‘atthāsaṃ pacurā haunti, phāsukaṃ upajāyati|

amittā dummanā haunti, ubhin‍naṃ samasīlinaṃ||

‘‘idha  dhammaṃ caritvāna, samasīlabbatā ubhau|

nandinau daivalaukasmiṃ, maudanti kāmakāminau’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasamajīvīsuttaṃ

56. ‘‘ākaṅkhaiyyuṃ cai, bhikkhavai, ubhau jānipatayau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passituṃ abhisamparāyañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passituṃ ubhauva assu samasaddhā samasīlā samacāgā samapañ‍ñā, tai diṭṭhai caiva dhammai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passanti abhisamparāyañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ passantī’’ti|

‘‘ubhau saddhā vadañ‍ñū ca, sañ‍ñatā dhammajīvinau|

tai haunti jānipatayau, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyaṃvadā||

‘‘atthāsaṃ pacurā haunti, phāsukaṃ upajāyati|

amittā dummanā haunti, ubhin‍naṃ samasīlinaṃ||

‘‘idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, samasīlabbatā ubhau|

nandinau daivalaukasmiṃ, maudanti kāmakāminau’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. suppavāsāsuttaṃ

57. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kauliyaisu viharati paj‍janikaṃ saj‍janailaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), paj‍janailaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nāma kauliyānaṃ nigamau| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya  yaina suppavāsāya kauliyadhītuyā nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau suppavāsā kauliyadhītā  bhagavantaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau suppavāsā kauliyadhītā bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau suppavāsaṃ kauliyadhītaraṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhaujanaṃ, suppavāsai, daintī ariyasāvikā paṭiggāhakānaṃ cattāri ṭhānāni daiti| katamāni cattāri? āyuṃ daiti, vaṇṇaṃ daiti, sukhaṃ daiti, balaṃ daiti| āyuṃ khau pana datvā āyussa bhāginī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā| vaṇṇaṃ datvā vaṇṇassa bhāginī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā| sukhaṃ datvā sukhassa bhāginī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā| balaṃ datvā balassa bhāginī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā| bhaujanaṃ, suppavāsai, daintī ariyasāvikā paṭiggāhakānaṃ imāni cattāri ṭhānāni daitī’’ti|

‘‘susaṅkhataṃ bhaujanaṃ yā dadāti,

suciṃ paṇītaṃ supaṇītaṃ (ka॰) rasasā upaitaṃ|

sā dakkhiṇā uj‍jugataisu din‍nā,

caraṇūpapan‍naisu mahaggataisu|

puñ‍ñaina puñ‍ñaṃ saṃsandamānā,

mahapphalā  laukavidūna vaṇṇitā||

‘‘aitādisaṃ yañ‍ñamanussarantā,

yai vaidajātā vicaranti laukai|

vinaiyya macchairamalaṃ samūlaṃ,

aninditā saggamupainti ṭhāna’’nti|| sattamaṃ|

8. sudattasuttaṃ

58. atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhaujanaṃ, gahapati, dadamānau ariyasāvakau paṭiggāhakānaṃ cattāri ṭhānāni daiti| katamāni cattāri? āyuṃ  daiti, vaṇṇaṃ daiti, sukhaṃ daiti, balaṃ daiti| āyuṃ khau pana datvā āyussa bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā| vaṇṇaṃ datvā… sukhaṃ datvā… balaṃ  datvā balassa bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā| bhaujanaṃ, gahapati, dadamānau ariyasāvakau paṭiggāhakānaṃ imāni cattāri ṭhānāni daitī’’ti|

mahāva॰ 282 ‘‘yau sañ‍ñatānaṃ paradattabhaujinaṃ,

kālaina sak‍kac‍ca dadāti bhaujanaṃ|

cattāri ṭhānāni anuppavaicchati,

āyuñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca sukhaṃ balañ‍ca||

‘‘sau āyudāyī vaṇṇadāyī sau āyudāyī baladāyī (sī॰ pī॰), āyudāyī baladāyī (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sukhaṃ balaṃ dadau sukhaṃ vaṇṇaṃ dadau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sukhabaladadau (ka॰) narau|

dīghāyu yasavā hauti, yattha yatthūpapaj‍jatī’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. bhaujanasuttaṃ

59. ‘‘bhaujanaṃ, bhikkhavai, dadamānau dāyakau paṭiggāhakānaṃ cattāri ṭhānāni daiti| katamāni cattāri? āyuṃ daiti, vaṇṇaṃ daiti, sukhaṃ daiti, balaṃ daiti| āyuṃ khau pana datvā āyussa bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā| vaṇṇaṃ datvā… sukhaṃ datvā… balaṃ datvā balassa bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā| bhaujanaṃ, bhikkhavai, dadamānau dāyakau paṭiggāhakānaṃ imāni cattāri ṭhānāni daitī’’ti|

mahāva॰ 282 ‘‘yau sañ‍ñatānaṃ paradattabhaujinaṃ,

kālaina sak‍kac‍ca dadāti bhaujanaṃ|

cattāri ṭhānāni anuppavaicchati,

āyuñ‍ca vaṇṇañ‍ca sukhaṃ balañ‍ca||

‘‘sau āyudāyī vaṇṇadāyī, sukhaṃ balaṃ dadau narau|

dīghāyu yasavā hauti, yattha yatthūpapaj‍jatī’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. gihisāmīcisuttaṃ

60. atha  khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘catūhi khau, gahapati, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau gihisāmīcipaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti yasaupaṭilābhiniṃ saggasaṃvattanikaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti cīvaraina, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti piṇḍapātaina, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti saināsanaina, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| imaihi khau, gahapati, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau gihisāmīcipaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti yasaupaṭilābhiniṃ saggasaṃvattanika’’nti|

‘‘gihisāmīcipaṭipadaṃ, paṭipaj‍janti paṇḍitā|

sammaggatai sīlavantai, cīvaraina upaṭṭhitā||

piṇḍapātasayanaina, gilānappac‍cayaina ca|

taisaṃ divā ca rattau ca, sadā puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati|

saggañ‍ca kamatiṭṭhānaṃ saggañ‍ca sappatiṭṭhānaṃ (ka॰), kammaṃ katvāna bhaddaka’’nti|| dasamaṃ|

puñ‍ñābhisandavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai puñ‍ñābhisandā dvai ca, saṃvāsā samajīvinau|

suppavāsā sudattau ca, bhaujanaṃ gihisāmicīti||


(7) 2. pattakammavaggau

1. pattakammasuttaṃ

61. atha  khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘cattāraumai, gahapati, dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? bhaugā mai uppaj‍jantu sahadhammaināti, ayaṃ paṭhamau dhammau iṭṭhau  kantau manāpau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘bhaugai laddhā sahadhammaina yasau mai āgacchatu saha ñātīhi saha upajjhāyaihīti, ayaṃ dutiyau dhammau iṭṭhau kantau manāpau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘bhaugai laddhā sahadhammaina yasaṃ laddhā saha ñātīhi saha upajjhāyaihi ciraṃ jīvāmi dīghamāyuṃ pālaimīti, ayaṃ tatiyau dhammau iṭṭhau kantau manāpau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘bhaugai laddhā sahadhammaina yasaṃ laddhā saha ñātīhi saha upajjhāyaihi ciraṃ jīvitvā dīghamāyuṃ pālaitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jāmīti, ayaṃ catutthau dhammau iṭṭhau kantau manāpau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| imai khau, gahapati, cattārau dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dul‍labhā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, gahapati, catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ dul‍labhānaṃ laukasmiṃ cattārau dhammā paṭilābhāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, cāgasampadā, pañ‍ñāsampadā|

‘‘katamā  ca, gahapati, saddhāsampadā? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau saddhau hauti, saddahati tathāgatassa baudhiṃ – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi, satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, saddhāsampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, gahapati, sīlasampadā? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, sīlasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, gahapati, cāgasampadā? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau vigatamalamacchairaina  caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vausaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, cāgasampadā|

‘‘katamā  ca, gahapati, pañ‍ñāsampadā? abhijjhāvisamalaubhābhibhūtaina, gahapati, caitasā viharantau akic‍caṃ karauti, kic‍caṃ aparādhaiti| akic‍caṃ karauntau kic‍caṃ aparādhaintau yasā ca sukhā ca dhaṃsati| byāpādābhibhūtaina, gahapati, caitasā viharantau akic‍caṃ karauti, kic‍caṃ aparādhaiti| akic‍caṃ karauntau kic‍caṃ aparādhaintau yasā ca sukhā ca dhaṃsati| thinamiddhābhibhūtaina, gahapati, caitasā viharantau akic‍caṃ karauti kic‍caṃ aparādhaiti| akic‍caṃ karauntau kic‍caṃ aparādhaintau yasā ca sukhā ca dhaṃsati| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cābhibhūtaina, gahapati, caitasā viharantau akic‍caṃ karauti, kic‍caṃ aparādhaiti| akic‍caṃ karauntau kic‍caṃ aparādhaintau yasā ca sukhā ca dhaṃsati| vicikicchābhibhūtaina, gahapati, caitasā viharantau akic‍caṃ karauti, kic‍caṃ aparādhaiti| akic‍caṃ karauntau kic‍caṃ aparādhaintau yasā ca sukhā ca dhaṃsati|

‘‘sa khau sau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau abhijjhāvisamalaubhau cittassa upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā abhijjhāvisamalaubhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati| byāpādau cittassa upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā byāpādaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati| thinamiddhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā thinamiddhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ cittassa  upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati| vicikicchā cittassa upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā vicikicchaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati|

‘‘yatau ca khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa  abhijjhāvisamalaubhau cittassa upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā abhijjhāvisamalaubhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti| byāpādau cittassa upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā byāpādau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti| thinamiddhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā thinamiddhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti| vicikicchā cittassa upak‍kilaisauti, iti viditvā vicikicchā cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, ariyasāvakau mahāpañ‍ñau puthupañ‍ñau āpātadasau āpāthadasau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hāsapañ‍ñau (ka॰)| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, pañ‍ñāsampadā| imaisaṃ khau, gahapati, catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ dul‍labhānaṃ laukasmiṃ imai cattārau dhammā paṭilābhāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘sa khau sau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi cattāri pattakammāni kattā hauti| katamāni cattāri? idha gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati| mātāpitarau sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati| puttadāradāsakammakarapaurisai sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati| mittāmac‍cai sukhaiti pīṇaiti sammā sukhaṃ pariharati| idamassa paṭhamaṃ ṭhānagataṃ hauti pattagataṃ āyatanasau paribhuttaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi yā tā haunti āpadā aggitau vā udakatau vā rājatau vā cauratau vā appiyatau vā dāyādatau a॰ ni॰ 5.41, tathārūpāsu āpadāsu pariyaudhāya  saṃvattati| sautthiṃ  attānaṃ karauti| idamassa dutiyaṃ ṭhānagataṃ hauti pattagataṃ āyatanasau paribhuttaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi pañ‍cabaliṃ kattā hauti – ñātibaliṃ, atithibaliṃ, pubbapaitabaliṃ, rājabaliṃ, daivatābaliṃ| idamassa tatiyaṃ ṭhānagataṃ hauti pattagataṃ āyatanasau paribhuttaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā madappamādā paṭiviratā khantisaurac‍cai niviṭṭhā aikamattānaṃ damainti, aikamattānaṃ samainti, aikamattānaṃ parinibbāpainti, tathārūpaisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu uddhaggikaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ patiṭṭhāpaiti sauvaggikaṃ sukhavipākaṃ saggasaṃvattanikaṃ| idamassa catutthaṃ ṭhānagataṃ hauti pattagataṃ āyatanasau paribhuttaṃ|

‘‘sa khau sau, gahapati, ariyasāvakau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi imāni cattāri pattakammāni kattā hauti| yassa kassaci, gahapati, añ‍ñatra imaihi catūhi pattakammaihi bhaugā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, imai vuc‍canti, gahapati, bhaugā aṭṭhānagatā apattagatā anāyatanasau paribhuttā| yassa kassaci, gahapati, imaihi catūhi pattakammaihi bhaugā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, imai vuc‍canti, gahapati, bhaugā ṭhānagatā pattagatā āyatanasau paribhuttā’’ti|

‘‘bhuttā bhaugā bhatā bhac‍cā gatā bhūtā (ka॰) bhaṭā bhac‍cā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), vitiṇṇā āpadāsu mai|

uddhaggā dakkhiṇā din‍nā, athau pañ‍cabalī katā|

upaṭṭhitā  sīlavantau, sañ‍ñatā brahmacārayau||

‘‘yadatthaṃ bhaugaṃ icchaiyya, paṇḍitau gharamāvasaṃ|

sau  mai atthau anuppattau, kataṃ ananutāpiyaṃ||

‘‘aitaṃ  aivaṃ (ka॰) anussaraṃ mac‍cau, ariyadhammai ṭhitau narau|

idhaiva naṃ pasaṃsanti, paic‍ca saggai pamaudatī’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ānaṇyasuttaṃ

62. atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘cattārimāni, gahapati, sukhāni adhigamanīyāni gihinā kāmabhauginā kālaina kālaṃ samayaina samayaṃ upādāya| katamāni cattāri? atthisukhaṃ, bhaugasukhaṃ, ānaṇyasukhaṃ aṇaṇasukhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), anavaj‍jasukhaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, gahapati, atthisukhaṃ? idha, gahapati, kulaputtassa bhaugā haunti uṭṭhānavīriyādhigatā bāhābalaparicitā saidāvakkhittā dhammikā dhammaladdhā| sau ‘bhaugā mai atthi uṭṭhānavīriyādhigatā bāhābalaparicitā saidāvakkhittā dhammikā dhammaladdhā’ti adhigacchati sukhaṃ, adhigacchati saumanassaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, atthisukhaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, gahapati, bhaugasukhaṃ? idha, gahapati, kulaputtau uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi paribhuñ‍jati puñ‍ñāni ca karauti| sau ‘uṭṭhānavīriyādhigataihi bhaugaihi bāhābalaparicitaihi saidāvakkhittaihi dhammikaihi dhammaladdhaihi paribhuñ‍jāmi puñ‍ñāni ca karaumī’ti adhigacchati sukhaṃ, adhigacchati saumanassaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, bhaugasukhaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, gahapati, ānaṇyasukhaṃ? idha, gahapati, kulaputtau na kassaci kiñ‍ci dhāraiti appaṃ vā bahuṃ vā| sau ‘na kassaci kiñ‍ci dhāraimi kiñ‍ci vā daiti (ka॰) appaṃ vā bahuṃ vā’ti adhigacchati sukhaṃ, adhigacchati saumanassaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, ānaṇyasukhaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, gahapati, anavaj‍jasukhaṃ? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau anavaj‍jaina kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, anavaj‍jaina vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, anavaj‍jaina manaukammaina  saman‍nāgatau hauti| sau ‘anavaj‍jainamhi  kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau, anavaj‍jaina vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau, anavaj‍jaina manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau’ti adhigacchati sukhaṃ, adhigacchati saumanassaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, gahapati, anavaj‍jasukhaṃ| imāni khau, gahapati, cattāri sukhāni adhigamanīyāni gihinā kāmabhauginā kālaina kālaṃ samayaina samayaṃ upādāyā’’ti|

‘‘ānaṇyasukhaṃ ñatvāna, athau atthisukhaṃ paraṃ|

bhuñ‍jaṃ bhaugasukhaṃ mac‍cau, tatau pañ‍ñā vipassati||

‘‘vipassamānau jānāti, ubhau bhaugai sumaidhasau|

anavaj‍jasukhassaitaṃ, kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasi’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. brahmasuttaṃ

63. ‘‘sabrahmakāni, bhikkhavai itivu॰ 106, tāni kulāni yaisaṃ puttānaṃ mātāpitarau ajjhāgārai pūjitā haunti| sapubbācariyakāni, bhikkhavai, tāni kulāni, yaisaṃ puttānaṃ mātāpitarau ajjhāgārai pūjitā haunti| sapubbadaivatāni sapubbadaivāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhikkhavai, tāni kulāni yaisaṃ puttānaṃ mātāpitarau ajjhāgārai pūjitā haunti| sāhunaiyyakāni, bhikkhavai, tāni kulāni yaisaṃ puttānaṃ mātāpitarau  ajjhāgārai pūjitā haunti|

‘‘brahmāti, bhikkhavai, mātāpitūnaṃ mātāpitun‍naṃ (sī॰ pī॰) aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| pubbācariyāti, bhikkhavai, mātāpitūnaṃ aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| pubbadaivatāti pubbadaivāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhikkhavai, mātāpitūnaṃ aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| āhunaiyyāti, bhikkhavai, mātāpitūnaṃ aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? bahukārā, bhikkhavai, mātāpitarau, puttānaṃ āpādakā pausakā imassa laukassa dassaitārau’’ti|

‘‘brahmāti mātāpitarau, pubbācariyāti vuc‍carai|

āhunaiyyā ca puttānaṃ, pajāya anukampakā||

‘‘tasmā  hi nai namassaiyya, sak‍karaiyya ca paṇḍitau|

an‍naina atha pānaina, vatthaina sayanaina ca|

ucchādanaina nhāpanaina, pādānaṃ dhauvanaina ca||

‘‘tāya naṃ pāricariyāya, mātāpitūsu paṇḍitā|

idhaiva naṃ pasaṃsanti, paic‍ca saggai pamaudatī’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. nirayasuttaṃ

64. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai’’ti|

‘‘pāṇātipātau adin‍nādānaṃ, musāvādau ca vuc‍cati|

paradāragamanañ‍cāpi, nappasaṃsanti paṇḍitā’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. rūpasuttaṃ

65. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai  cattārau? rūpappamāṇau rūpappasan‍nau, ghausappamāṇau ghausappasan‍nau, lūkhappamāṇau lūkhappasan‍nau, dhammappamāṇau dhammappasan‍nau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti|

‘‘yai ca rūpai pamāṇiṃsu yai ca rūpaina pāmiṃsu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), yai ca ghausaina anvagū|

chandarāgavasūpaitā, nābhijānanti tai janā na tai jānanti taṃ janā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca  na jānāti, bahiddhā ca na passati|

samantāvaraṇau bālau, sa vai ghausaina vuyhati||

‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca na jānāti, bahiddhā ca vipassati|

bahiddhā phaladassāvī, saupi ghausaina vuyhati||

‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca pajānāti, bahiddhā ca vipassati|

vinīvaraṇadassāvī, na sau ghausaina vuyhatī’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sarāgasuttaṃ

66. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? sarāgau, sadausau, samauhau, samānau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti|

‘‘sārattā  rajanīyaisu, piyarūpābhinandinau|

mauhaina āvutā adhamā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sattā, baddhā bandhā (ka॰) vaḍḍhainti bandhanaṃ||

‘‘rāgajaṃ dausajañ‍cāpi, mauhajaṃ cāpaviddasū|

karauntākusalaṃ kammaṃ dhammaṃ (ka॰), savighātaṃ dukhudrayaṃ||

‘‘avij‍jānivutā pausā, andhabhūtā acakkhukā|

yathā dhammā tathā santā, na tassaivanti nassaivanti (sī॰) mañ‍ñarai’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ahirājasuttaṃ

67. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ahinā daṭṭhau kālaṅkatau hauti| atha khau  sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ahinā daṭṭhau kālaṅkatau’’ti|

‘‘na hi nūna na ha nūna (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina phari| sacai hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina pharaiyya, na hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ahinā daṭṭhau kālaṅkaraiyya|

‘‘katamāni cattāri? virūpakkhaṃ ahirājakulaṃ, airāpathaṃ ahirājakulaṃ, chabyāputtaṃ ahirājakulaṃ, kaṇhāgautamakaṃ ahirājakulaṃ| na hi nūna sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imāni cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina phari| sacai hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imāni cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina pharaiyya, na hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ahinā daṭṭhau kālaṅkaraiyya|

‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imāni cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina pharituṃ attaguttiyā attarakkhāya attaparittāyā’’ti|

cūḷava॰ 251; jā॰ 1.2.105 passitabbaṃ ‘‘virūpakkhaihi mai maittaṃ, maittaṃ airāpathaihi mai|

chabyāputtaihi mai maittaṃ, maittaṃ kaṇhāgautamakaihi ca||

‘‘apādakaihi mai maittaṃ, maittaṃ dvipādakaihi dipādakaihi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mai|

catuppadaihi  mai maittaṃ, maittaṃ bahuppadaihi mai||

‘‘mā  maṃ apādakau hiṃsi, mā maṃ hiṃsi dvipādakau dipādakau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

mā maṃ catuppadau hiṃsi, mā maṃ hiṃsi bahuppadau||

‘‘sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā, sabbai bhūtā ca kaivalā|

sabbai bhadrāni passantu, mā kañ‍ci kiñ‍ci (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) pāpamāgamā||

‘‘appamāṇau  buddhau, appamāṇau dhammau|

appamāṇau saṅghau, pamāṇavantāni sarīsapāni siriṃsapāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

‘‘ahivicchikā satapadī, uṇṇanābhī sarabū mūsikā|

katā mai rakkhā katā mai parittā kataṃ mai parittaṃ (?), paṭik‍kamantu bhūtāni|

sauhaṃ namau bhagavatau, namau sattan‍naṃ sammāsambuddhāna’’nti|| sattamaṃ|

8. daivadattasuttaṃ

68. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai acirapak‍kantai daivadattai| tatra khau bhagavā daivadattaṃ ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘attavadhāya, bhikkhavai cūḷava॰ 252; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.184, daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi| parābhavāya, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kadalī attavadhāya phalaṃ daiti, parābhavāya phalaṃ daiti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vaiḷu attavadhāya phalaṃ daiti, parābhavāya phalaṃ daiti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, naḷau attavadhāya phalaṃ daiti, parābhavāya phalaṃ daiti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa  lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, assatarī attavadhāya gabbhaṃ gaṇhāti, parābhavāya gabbhaṃ gaṇhāti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādī’’ti|

‘‘phalaṃ  vai kadaliṃ hanti, phalaṃ vaiḷuṃ phalaṃ naḷaṃ|

sak‍kārau kāpurisaṃ hanti, gabbhau assatariṃ yathā’’ti cūḷava॰ 335; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.183; 2.184; naitti॰ 90|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. padhānasuttaṃ

69. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, padhānāni| katamāni cattāri? saṃvarappadhānaṃ, pahānappadhānaṃ, bhāvanāppadhānaṃ, anurakkhaṇāppadhānaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṃvarappadhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya  chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saṃvarappadhānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pahānappadhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pahānappadhānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhāvanāppadhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhāvanāppadhānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anurakkhaṇāppadhānaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| idaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anurakkhaṇāppadhānaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri padhānānī’’ti|

‘‘saṃvarau ca pahānañ‍ca, bhāvanā anurakkhaṇā|

aitai padhānā cattārau, daisitādic‍cabandhunā|

yau hi yaihi (?) bhikkhu idhātāpī, khayaṃ dukkhassa pāpuṇai’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. adhammikasuttaṃ

70. ‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai rājānau adhammikā haunti, rājāyuttāpi tasmiṃ samayai adhammikā haunti| rājāyuttaisu adhammikaisu brāhmaṇagahapatikāpi tasmiṃ samayai adhammikā haunti| brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu adhammikaisu naigamajānapadāpi tasmiṃ samayai adhammikā haunti| naigamajānapadaisu adhammikaisu visamaṃ candimasūriyā parivattanti| visamaṃ candimasūriyaisu parivattantaisu visamaṃ nakkhattāni tārakarūpāni parivattanti| visamaṃ nakkhattaisu tārakarūpaisu parivattantaisu visamaṃ rattindivā rattidivā (ka॰) parivattanti| visamaṃ rattindivaisu parivattantaisu visamaṃ māsaddhamāsā parivattanti| visamaṃ māsaddhamāsaisu parivattantaisu visamaṃ utusaṃvaccharā parivattanti| visamaṃ utusaṃvaccharaisu parivattantaisu visamaṃ vātā vāyanti visamā apañ‍jasā| visamaṃ vātaisu vāyantaisu visamaisu apañ‍jasaisu daivatā parikupitā bhavanti| daivatāsu parikupitāsu daivau na sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchati| daivai na sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchantai visamapākāni visamapākīni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), visamaṃ pākāni (ka॰) sassāni bhavanti| visamapākāni, bhikkhavai, sassāni manussā paribhuñ‍jantā  appāyukā haunti dubbaṇṇā ca bavhābādhā bahvābādhā (ka॰) ca|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai rājānau dhammikā haunti, rājāyuttāpi tasmiṃ samayai dhammikā haunti| rājāyuttaisu dhammikaisu brāhmaṇagahapatikāpi tasmiṃ samayai dhammikā haunti| brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu dhammikaisu naigamajānapadāpi tasmiṃ samayai dhammikā haunti| naigamajānapadaisu dhammikaisu samaṃ candimasūriyā parivattanti| samaṃ candimasūriyaisu parivattantaisu samaṃ nakkhattāni tārakarūpāni parivattanti| samaṃ nakkhattaisu tārakarūpaisu parivattantaisu samaṃ rattindivā parivattanti| samaṃ rattindivaisu parivattantaisu samaṃ māsaddhamāsā parivattanti| samaṃ māsaddhamāsaisu parivattantaisu samaṃ utusaṃvaccharā parivattanti| samaṃ utusaṃvaccharaisu parivattantaisu samaṃ vātā vāyanti samā pañ‍jasā| samaṃ vātaisu vāyantaisu samaisu pañ‍jasaisu daivatā aparikupitā bhavanti| daivatāsu aparikupitāsu daivau sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchati| daivai sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchantai samapākāni sassāni bhavanti| samapākāni, bhikkhavai, sassāni manussā paribhuñ‍jantā dīghāyukā ca haunti vaṇṇavantau ca balavantau ca appābādhā cā’’ti|

‘‘gun‍naṃ  cai taramānānaṃ, jimhaṃ gacchati puṅgavau|

sabbā tā jimhaṃ gacchanti, naittai jimhaṃ gatai sati||

‘‘aivamaivaṃ manussaisu, yau hauti saiṭṭhasammatau|

sau cai adhammaṃ carati, pagaiva itarā pajā|

sabbaṃ  raṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti adhammikau||

‘‘gun‍naṃ cai taramānānaṃ, ujuṃ gacchati puṅgavau|

sabbā tā ujuṃ gacchanti, naittai ujuṃ gatai sati||

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  manussaisu, yau hauti saiṭṭhasammatau|

sau sacai sau caiva (sī॰ pī॰), sau cai (syā॰) dhammaṃ carati, pagaiva itarā pajā|

sabbaṃ  raṭṭhaṃ sukhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti dhammikau’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

pattakammavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

pattakammaṃ ānaṇyakau anaṇakau (sī॰ pī॰), anaṇyakau (ka॰), sabrahmanirayā rūpaina pañ‍camaṃ|

sarāgaahirājā daivadattau, padhānaṃ adhammikaina cāti||


(8) 3. apaṇṇakavaggau

1. padhānasuttaṃ

71. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu apaṇṇakappaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāya| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu apaṇṇakappaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sammādiṭṭhisuttaṃ

72. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu apaṇṇakappaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāya| katamaihi  catūhi? naikkhammavitak‍kaina, abyāpādavitak‍kaina, avihiṃsāvitak‍kaina, sammādiṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu  apaṇṇakappaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sappurisasuttaṃ

73. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau asappurisau vaiditabbau| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, asappurisau yau hauti parassa avaṇṇau taṃ apuṭṭhaupi pātu karauti, kau pana vādau puṭṭhassa! puṭṭhau khau pana pañhābhinītau ahāpaitvā alambitvā paripūraṃ vitthāraina parassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau yau hauti parassa vaṇṇau taṃ puṭṭhaupi na pātu karauti, kau pana vādau apuṭṭhassa! puṭṭhau khau pana pañhābhinītau hāpaitvā lambitvā aparipūraṃ avitthāraina parassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau yau hauti attanau avaṇṇau taṃ puṭṭhaupi na pātu karauti, kau pana vādau apuṭṭhassa! puṭṭhau khau pana pañhābhinītau hāpaitvā lambitvā aparipūraṃ avitthāraina attanau avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau yau hauti attanau vaṇṇau taṃ apuṭṭhaupi pātu karauti, kau pana vādau puṭṭhassa! puṭṭhau khau pana pañhābhinītau ahāpaitvā alambitvā paripūraṃ vitthāraina attanau  vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, asappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau asappurisau vaiditabbau|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sappurisau vaiditabbau| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, sappurisau yau hauti parassa avaṇṇau taṃ puṭṭhaupi na pātu karauti, kau pana vādau apuṭṭhassa! puṭṭhau khau pana pañhābhinītau hāpaitvā lambitvā aparipūraṃ  avitthāraina parassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sappurisau yau hauti parassa vaṇṇau taṃ apuṭṭhaupi pātu karauti, kau pana vādau puṭṭhassa! puṭṭhau khau pana pañhābhinītau ahāpaitvā alambitvā paripūraṃ vitthāraina parassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sappurisau yau hauti attanau avaṇṇau taṃ apuṭṭhaupi pātu karauti, kau pana vādau puṭṭhassa! puṭṭhau khau pana pañhābhinītau ahāpaitvā alambitvā paripūraṃ vitthāraina attanau avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sappurisau yau hauti attanau vaṇṇau taṃ puṭṭhaupi na pātu karauti, kau pana vādau apuṭṭhassa! puṭṭhau khau pana pañhābhinītau hāpaitvā lambitvā aparipūraṃ avitthāraina attanau vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti| vaiditabbamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau sappurisau vaiditabbau|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vadhukā yañ‍ñadaiva rattiṃ vā divaṃ vā ānītā hauti, tāvadaivassā tibbaṃ hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sassuyāpi sasuraipi sāmikaipi antamasau dāsakammakarapaurisaisu| sā aparaina samayaina saṃvāsamanvāya vissāsamanvāya sassumpi sasurampi sāmikampi aivamāha – ‘apaitha, kiṃ pana tumhai jānāthā’ti! aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu yañ‍ñadaiva rattiṃ vā divaṃ vā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, tāvadaivassa tibbaṃ hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti bhikkhūsu bhikkhunīsu upāsakaisu upāsikāsu antamasau ārāmikasamaṇuddaisaisu| sau aparaina samayaina saṃvāsamanvāya vissāsamanvāya ācariyampi upajjhāyampi aivamāha – ‘apaitha, kiṃ pana tumhai jānāthā’ti! tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘adhunāgatavadhukāsamaina caitasā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamaaggasuttaṃ

74. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, aggāni| katamāni cattāri? sīlaggaṃ, samādhiggaṃ samādhaggaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ), pañ‍ñāggaṃ, vimuttaggaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri aggānī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyaaggasuttaṃ

75. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, aggāni| katamāni cattāri? rūpaggaṃ, vaidanāggaṃ, sañ‍ñāggaṃ, bhavaggaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri aggānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kusinārasuttaṃ

76. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kusinārāyaṃ viharati upavattanai mal‍lānaṃ sālavanai antaraina yamakasālānaṃ parinibbānasamayai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū  āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai dī॰ ni॰ 2.217, aikabhikkhussapi kaṅkhā vā vimati vā buddhai vā dhammai vā saṅghai vā maggai vā paṭipadāya vā, pucchatha, bhikkhavai, mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha – ‘sammukhībhūtau nau satthā ahausi, nāsakkhimha bhagavantaṃ sammukhā paṭipucchitu’’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai tai bhikkhū tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| dutiyampi khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, aikabhikkhussapi kaṅkhā vā vimati vā buddhai vā dhammai vā saṅghai vā maggai vā paṭipadāya vā, pucchatha, bhikkhavai, mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha – ‘sammukhībhūtau nau satthā ahausi, nāsakkhimha bhagavantaṃ sammukhā paṭipucchitu’’’nti| dutiyampi khau tai bhikkhū tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| tatiyampi khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, aikabhikkhussapi kaṅkhā vā vimati vā buddhai vā dhammai vā saṅghai vā maggai vā paṭipadāya vā, pucchatha, bhikkhavai, mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha – ‘sammukhībhūtau nau satthā ahausi, nāsakkhimha bhagavantaṃ sammukhā paṭipucchitu’’’nti| tatiyampi khau tai bhikkhū tuṇhī ahaisuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, satthugāravainapi na pucchaiyyātha, sahāyakaupi, bhikkhavai, sahāyakassa āraucaitū’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai tai bhikkhū tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha  khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! aivaṃ pasan‍nau ahaṃ, bhantai! natthi imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai aikabhikkhussapi kaṅkhā vā vimati vā buddhai vā dhammai vā saṅghai vā maggai vā paṭipadāya vā’’ti|

‘‘pasādā  khau tvaṃ, ānanda, vadaisi| ñāṇamaiva haittha, ānanda, tathāgatassa – ‘natthi imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai aikabhikkhussapi kaṅkhā vā vimati vā buddhai vā dhammai vā saṅghai vā maggai  vā paṭipadāya vā’| imaisañhi, ānanda, pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ yau pacchimakau bhikkhu sau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. acintaiyyasuttaṃ

77. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, acintaiyyāni, na cintaitabbāni; yāni cintaintau ummādassa vighātassa bhāgī assa| katamāni cattāri? buddhānaṃ, bhikkhavai, buddhavisayau acintaiyyau, na cintaitabbau; yaṃ cintaintau ummādassa vighātassa bhāgī assa| jhāyissa, bhikkhavai, jhānavisayau acintaiyyau, na cintaitabbau; yaṃ cintaintau ummādassa vighātassa bhāgī assa| kammavipākau, bhikkhavai, acintaiyyau, na cintaitabbau; yaṃ cintaintau ummādassa vighātassa bhāgī assa| laukacintā, bhikkhavai, acintaiyyā, na cintaitabbā; yaṃ cintaintau ummādassa vighātassa bhāgī assa| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri acintaiyyāni, na cintaitabbāni; yāni cintaintau ummādassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dakkhiṇasuttaṃ

78. ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā visuddhiyau| katamā catassau? atthi, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau visujjhati, nau paṭiggāhakatau; atthi, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā paṭiggāhakatau visujjhati, nau dāyakatau; atthi, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā naiva dāyakatau visujjhati, nau paṭiggāhakatau; atthi, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau caiva visujjhati paṭiggāhakatau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau visujjhati, nau paṭiggāhakatau? idha, bhikkhavai, dāyakau hauti sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau; paṭiggāhakā haunti dussīlā pāpadhammā  paṭiggāhakau hauti dussīlau pāpadhammau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.381 aulaukaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau visujjhati, nau paṭiggāhakatau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā paṭiggāhakatau visujjhati, nau dāyakatau? idha, bhikkhavai, dāyakau hauti dussīlau pāpadhammau; paṭiggāhakā haunti sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā paṭiggāhakatau visujjhati, nau dāyakatau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā naiva dāyakatau visujjhati, nau paṭiggāhakatau? idha, bhikkhavai, dāyakau hauti dussīlau pāpadhammau; paṭiggāhakāpi haunti dussīlā pāpadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā naiva dāyakatau visujjhati, nau paṭiggāhakatau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau caiva visujjhati paṭiggāhakatau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, dāyakau hauti sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau; paṭiggāhakāpi haunti sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dakkhiṇā dāyakatau caiva visujjhati paṭiggāhakatau ca| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau dakkhiṇā visuddhiyau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vaṇij‍jasuttaṃ

79. atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisāva vaṇij‍jā payuttā chaidagāminī hauti? kau pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisāva vaṇij‍jā payuttā na yathādhippāyā yathādhippāyaṃ (sī॰) hauti? kau nu khau, bhantai haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisāva vaṇij‍jā payuttā yathādhippāyā yathādhippāyaṃ (sī॰) hauti? kau pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina  midhaikac‍cassa tādisāva vaṇij‍jā payuttā parādhippāyā hautī’’ti?

‘‘idha, sāriputta, aikac‍cau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā pavāraiti – ‘vadatu, bhantai, pac‍cayainā’ti| sau yaina  pavāraiti taṃ na daiti| sau cai tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgacchati, sau yañ‍ñadaiva vaṇij‍jaṃ payaujaiti, sāssa hauti chaidagāminī|

‘‘idha  pana, sāriputta, aikac‍cau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā pavāraiti – ‘vadatu, bhantai, pac‍cayainā’ti| sau yaina pavāraiti taṃ na yathādhippāyaṃ daiti| sau cai tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgacchati, sau yañ‍ñadaiva vaṇij‍jaṃ payaujaiti, sāssa na hauti yathādhippāyā yathādhippāyaṃ (sī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘idha pana, sāriputta, aikac‍cau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā pavāraiti – ‘vadatu, bhantai, pac‍cayainā’ti| sau yaina pavāraiti taṃ yathādhippāyaṃ daiti| sau cai tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgacchati, sau yañ‍ñadaiva vaṇij‍jaṃ payaujaiti, sāssa hauti yathādhippāyā yathādhippāyaṃ (sī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘idha, sāriputta, aikac‍cau samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā pavāraiti – ‘vadatu, bhantai, pac‍cayainā’ti| sau yaina pavāraiti taṃ parādhippāyaṃ daiti| sau cai tatau cutau itthattaṃ āgacchati, sau yañ‍ñadaiva vaṇij‍jaṃ payaujaiti, sāssa hauti parādhippāyā parādhippāyaṃ (ka॰)|

‘‘ayaṃ khau, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisāva vaṇij‍jā payuttā chaidagāminī hauti| ayaṃ pana, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisāva vaṇij‍jā payuttā na yathādhippāyā hauti| ayaṃ khau pana, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisāva vaṇij‍jā payuttā yathādhippāyā hauti| ayaṃ pana, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cassa tādisāva vaṇij‍jā  payuttā parādhippāyā hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. kambaujasuttaṃ

80. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina mātugāmau naiva sabhāyaṃ nisīdati, na kammantaṃ payaujaiti, na kambaujaṃ gacchatī’’ti? ‘‘kaudhanau, ānanda, mātugāmau; issukī, ānanda, mātugāmau; maccharī, ānanda, mātugāmau; duppañ‍ñau, ānanda, mātugāmau – ayaṃ khau, ānanda, haitu  ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina mātugāmau naiva sabhāyaṃ nisīdati, na kammantaṃ payaujaiti, na kambaujaṃ gacchatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

apaṇṇakavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

padhānaṃ diṭṭhisappurisa, vadhukā dvai ca haunti aggāni|

kusināraacintaiyyā, dakkhiṇā ca vaṇij‍jā kambaujanti||


(9) 4. macalavaggau

1. pāṇātipātasuttaṃ

81. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. musāvādasuttaṃ

82. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? musāvādī hauti, pisuṇavācau hauti, pharusavācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā  paṭiviratau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. avaṇṇārahasuttaṃ

83. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā appasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaṃ  upadaṃsaiti, ananuvic‍ca apariyaugāhaitvā pasādanīyai ṭhānai appasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā appasādanīyai ṭhānai appasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti anuvic‍ca pariyaugāhaitvā pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaṃ upadaṃsaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. kaudhagarusuttaṃ

84. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? kaudhagaru hauti na saddhammagaru, makkhagaru hauti na saddhammagaru, lābhagaru hauti na saddhammagaru, sak‍kāragaru hauti na saddhammagaru – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? saddhammagaru hauti na kaudhagaru, saddhammagaru hauti na makkhagaru, saddhammagaru hauti na lābhagaru, saddhammagaru hauti na sak‍kāragaru – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. tamautamasuttaṃ

85. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? tamau tamaparāyaṇau … parāyanau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pu॰ pa॰ 168; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.132, tamau jautiparāyaṇau, jauti tamaparāyaṇau, jauti jautiparāyaṇau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau tamau hauti tamaparāyaṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau nīcai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – caṇḍālakulai vā vainakulai vā naisādakulai vā rathakārakulai vā puk‍kusakulai vā daliddai appan‍napānabhaujanai kasiravuttikai, yattha kasiraina ghāsacchādau labbhati| sau ca hauti dubbaṇṇau duddasikau aukauṭimakau bavhābādhau kāṇau vā kuṇī vā khañ‍jau vā pakkhahatau vā, na lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau tamau hauti tamaparāyaṇau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau tamau hauti jautiparāyaṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau nīcai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – caṇḍālakulai vā vainakulai vā naisādakulai vā rathakārakulai vā puk‍kusakulai vā daliddai appan‍napānabhaujanai kasiravuttikai, yattha kasiraina ghāsacchādau labbhati; sau ca hauti dubbaṇṇau duddasikau aukauṭimakau bavhābādhau kāṇau vā kuṇī vā khañ‍jau vā pakkhahatau vā na lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya  sucaritaṃ caritvā, manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau tamau hauti jautiparāyaṇau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau jauti hauti tamaparāyaṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau uc‍cai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – khattiyamahāsālakulai vā brāhmaṇamahāsālakulai vā gahapatimahāsālakulai  vā aḍḍhai mahaddhanai mahābhaugai pahūtajātarūparajatai pahūtavittūpakaraṇai pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñai; sau ca hauti abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau, lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau jauti hauti tamaparāyaṇau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau jauti hauti jautiparāyaṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau uc‍cai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti – khattiyamahāsālakulai vā brāhmaṇamahāsālakulai vā gahapatimahāsālakulai vā aḍḍhai mahaddhanai mahābhaugai pahūtajātarūparajatai pahūtavittūpakaraṇai pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñai; sau ca hauti abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatau, lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina sucaritaṃ caritvā, vācāya sucaritaṃ caritvā, manasā sucaritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau jauti hauti jautiparāyaṇau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. auṇatauṇatasuttaṃ

86. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? auṇatauṇatau, auṇatuṇṇatau, uṇṇatauṇatau, uṇṇatuṇṇatau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti pu॰ pa॰ 169| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. puttasuttaṃ

87. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? samaṇamacalau, samaṇapuṇḍarīkau, samaṇapadumau, samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇamacalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saikhau hauti pāṭipadau paṭipadau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2 saikhasuttavaṇṇanā aulaukaitabbā; anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānau viharati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa jaiṭṭhau puttau ābhisaikau anabhisittau macalappattau; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saikhau hauti pāṭipadau, anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānau viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇamacalau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapuṇḍarīkau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ  caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, nau ca khau aṭṭha vimaukkhai kāyaina phusitvā viharati phassitvā (sī॰ pī॰)| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapuṇḍarīkau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapadumau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, aṭṭha ca vimaukkhai kāyaina phusitvā viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapadumau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yācitauva bahulaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ saināsanaṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau| yaihi khau pana sabrahmacārīhi saddhiṃ viharati, tyassa tyāssa (sī॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.104 manāpainaiva bahulaṃ kāyakammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ  amanāpaina; manāpainaiva bahulaṃ vacīkammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpainaiva bahulaṃ manaukammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpaṃyaiva bahulaṃ upahāraṃ upaharanti, appaṃ amanāpaṃ| yāni khau pana tāni vaidayitāni pittasamuṭṭhānāni vā saimhasamuṭṭhānāni vā vātasamuṭṭhānāni vā san‍nipātikāni vā utupariṇāmajāni vā visamaparihārajāni vā aupak‍kamikāni vā kammavipākajāni vā, tāni panassa na bahudaiva uppaj‍janti| appābādhau hauti| catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ  diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ  pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai  sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau hauti|

‘‘yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālauti, mamaiva taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālauti| ahañhi, bhikkhavai, yācitauva bahulaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jāmi, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jāmi, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ saināsanaṃ paribhuñ‍jāmi, appaṃ ayācitau; yācitauva bahulaṃ gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍jāmi, appaṃ ayācitau| yaihi khau pana bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ viharāmi tai mai manāpainaiva bahulaṃ kāyakammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpainaiva bahulaṃ vacīkammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpainaiva bahulaṃ manaukammaina samudācaranti, appaṃ amanāpaina; manāpaṃyaiva bahulaṃ upahāraṃ upaharanti, appaṃ amanāpaṃ| yāni khau pana tāni vaidayitāni pittasamuṭṭhānāni vā saimhasamuṭṭhānāni vā vātasamuṭṭhānāni vā san‍nipātikāni vā utupariṇāmajāni vā visamaparihārajāni vā aupak‍kamikāni vā kammavipākajāni vā, tāni mai na bahudaiva uppaj‍janti| appābādhauhamasmi| catun‍naṃ khau panasmi jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā  upasampaj‍ja viharāmi|

‘‘yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālauti, mamaiva taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya samaṇaisu  samaṇasukhumālauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. saṃyaujanasuttaṃ

88. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? samaṇamacalau, samaṇapuṇḍarīkau, samaṇapadumau, samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau pu॰ pa॰ 190 (thaukaṃ visadisaṃ) samaṇamacalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ  parikkhayā  sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇamacalau hauti|
