‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapuṇḍarīkau hauti? idha bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā, rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapuṇḍarīkau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapadumau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapadumau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau hauti| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sammādiṭṭhisuttaṃ

89. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? samaṇamacalau, samaṇapuṇḍarīkau, samaṇapadumau, samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇamacalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvācau hauti, sammākammantau  hauti, sammāājīvau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati sammāsatī (sī॰ ka॰) hauti, sammāsamādhi sammāsamādhī (sī॰ ka॰) hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇamacalau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapuṇḍarīkau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvācau hauti, sammākammantau hauti, sammāājīvau  hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti, sammāñāṇī hauti, sammāvimutti sammāvimuttī (sī॰ ka॰) hauti, nau ca khau aṭṭha vimaukkhai kāyaina phusitvā viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapuṇḍarīkau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapadumau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… sammāvimutti hauti, aṭṭha ca vimaukkhai kāyaina phusitvā viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapadumau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yācitauva bahulaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau…pai॰… yañhi taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālauti, mamaiva taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. khandhasuttaṃ

90. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? samaṇamacalau, samaṇapuṇḍarīkau, samaṇapadumau, samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇamacalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saikhau hauti appattamānasau, anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānau viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇamacalau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapuṇḍarīkau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassī viharati – ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā…pai॰… iti sañ‍ñā…pai॰… iti saṅkhārā…pai॰… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti; nau ca khau aṭṭha vimaukkhai kāyaina phusitvā viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapuṇḍarīkau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapadumau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassī viharati – ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā…pai॰… iti sañ‍ñā…pai॰… iti saṅkhārā…pai॰… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti; aṭṭha ca vimaukkhai kāyaina phusitvā viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇapadumau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yācitauva bahulaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jati, appaṃ ayācitau…pai॰… mamaiva taṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā vadamānau vadaiyya samaṇaisu samaṇasukhumālauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| dasamaṃ|

macalavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

pāṇātipātau ca musā, avaṇṇakaudhatamauṇatā|

puttau saṃyaujanañ‍caiva, diṭṭhi khandhaina tai dasāti||


(10) 5. asuravaggau

1. asurasuttaṃ

91. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? asurau asuraparivārau, asurau daivaparivārau, daivau asuraparivārau, daivau daivaparivārau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau asurau hauti asuraparivārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dussīlau hauti pāpadhammau, parisāpissa hauti dussīlā pāpadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau asurau hauti asuraparivārau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau asurau hauti  daivaparivārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dussīlau hauti pāpadhammau, parisā ca khvassa hauti sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau asurau hauti daivaparivārau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau daivau hauti asuraparivārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlavā hauti kalyāṇadhammau, parisā ca khvassa hauti dussīlā pāpadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau daivau hauti asuraparivārau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau daivau hauti daivaparivārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlavā hauti kalyāṇadhammau, parisāpissa hauti sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau daivau hauti, daivaparivārau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamasamādhisuttaṃ

92. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau lābhī hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau lābhī hauti adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, na lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na caiva lābhī hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa na ca lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyasamādhisuttaṃ

93. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau lābhī hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau lābhī hauti adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, na lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na caiva lābhī hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa na ca lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau lābhī hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa  na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, taina, bhikkhavai, puggalaina ajjhattaṃ caitausamathai patiṭṭhāya adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya yaugau karaṇīyau| sau aparaina samayaina lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya na lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, taina, bhikkhavai, puggalaina adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya patiṭṭhāya ajjhattaṃ caitausamathai yaugau  karaṇīyau| sau aparaina samayaina lābhī caiva hauti adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau na caiva lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa na ca lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, taina, bhikkhavai, puggalaina taisaṃyaiva kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ādittacailau vā ādittasīsau vā tassaiva tassa tassaiva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) cailassa vā sīsassa vā nibbāpanāya adhimattaṃ chandañ‍ca vāyāmañ‍ca ussāhañ‍ca ussauḷhiñ‍ca appaṭivāniñ‍ca satiñ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, taina puggalaina taisaṃyaiva kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya adhimattau chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca  sati  ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca karaṇīyaṃ| sau aparaina samayaina lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, taina, bhikkhavai, puggalaina taisuyaiva kusalaisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya uttari uttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) āsavānaṃ khayāya yaugau karaṇīyau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. tatiyasamādhisuttaṃ

94. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau lābhī hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya| idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau lābhī hauti adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, na lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na caiva lābhī hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa na ca lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau lābhī  ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, taina, bhikkhavai, puggalaina yvāyaṃ puggalau lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘kathaṃ nu khau, āvusau, saṅkhārā daṭṭhabbā? kathaṃ saṅkhārā sammasitabbā? kathaṃ saṅkhārā vipassitabbā’ passitabbā (ka॰) ti? tassa sau yathādiṭṭhaṃ yathāviditaṃ byākarauti – ‘aivaṃ khau, āvusau, saṅkhārā daṭṭhabbā, aivaṃ saṅkhārā sammasitabbā, aivaṃ saṅkhārā vipassitabbā’ti| sau aparaina samayaina lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya na lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa, taina, bhikkhavai, puggalaina yvāyaṃ puggalau lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘kathaṃ nu khau, āvusau, cittaṃ saṇṭhapaitabbaṃ? kathaṃ cittaṃ san‍nisādaitabbaṃ? kathaṃ cittaṃ aikaudi kātabbaṃ? kathaṃ cittaṃ samādahātabba’nti? tassa sau yathādiṭṭhaṃ yathāviditaṃ byākarauti – ‘aivaṃ khau, āvusau, cittaṃ saṇṭhapaitabbaṃ, aivaṃ cittaṃ san‍nisādaitabbaṃ, aivaṃ cittaṃ aikaudi kātabbaṃ aikaudi kattabbaṃ (pī॰), aivaṃ cittaṃ samādahātabba’nti| sau aparaina samayai lābhī caiva hauti adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau na caiva lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa na ca lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, taina, bhikkhavai, puggalaina yvāyaṃ puggalau lābhī caiva ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī  ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya sau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘kathaṃ nu khau, āvusau, cittaṃ saṇṭhapaitabbaṃ? kathaṃ cittaṃ san‍nisādaitabbaṃ? kathaṃ cittaṃ aikaudi kātabbaṃ? kathaṃ cittaṃ samādahātabbaṃ? kathaṃ saṅkhārā daṭṭhabbā? kathaṃ saṅkhārā sammasitabbā? kathaṃ saṅkhārā vipassitabbā’ti? tassa sau yathādiṭṭhaṃ yathāviditaṃ byākarauti – ‘aivaṃ khau, āvusau, cittaṃ saṇṭhapaitabbaṃ, aivaṃ cittaṃ san‍nisādaitabbaṃ, aivaṃ cittaṃ aikaudi kātabbaṃ, aivaṃ cittaṃ samādahātabbaṃ, aivaṃ saṅkhārā daṭṭhabbā, aivaṃ saṅkhārā sammasitabbā, aivaṃ saṅkhārā vipassitabbā’ti| sau aparaina samayaina  lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau lābhī caiva hauti ajjhattaṃ caitausamathassa lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, taina, bhikkhavai, puggalaina taisu caiva kusalaisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya uttari āsavānaṃ khayāya yaugau karaṇīyau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. chavālātasuttaṃ

95. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, parahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau attahitāya, attahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, attahitāya caiva attahitāya ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paṭipan‍nau parahitāya ca|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, chavālātaṃ ubhatau padittaṃ ādittaṃ (ka॰), majjhai gūthagataṃ, naiva gāmai kaṭṭhatthaṃ pharati na arañ‍ñai ( ) (kaṭṭhatthaṃ pharati) katthaci; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi yvāyaṃ puggalau naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau parahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau attahitāya, ayaṃ imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, ayaṃ imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau parahitāya ca, ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca pāmaukkhau maukkhau (pī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.662-663; a॰ ni॰ 5.181 ca uttamau ca pavarau ca|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau, sappimaṇḍau sappimhā sappimaṇḍau (ka॰) tattha tatra (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.662-662) aggamakkhāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau parahitāya ca, ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca pāmaukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. rāgavinayasuttaṃ

96. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? attahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, parahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau attahitāya, naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau parahitāya ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti nau parahitāya? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau  attanā rāgavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ rāgavinayāya samādapaiti; attanā dausavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ dausavinayāya samādapaiti; attanā  mauhavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ mauhavinayāya samādapaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau parahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau attahitāya? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau attanā na rāgavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, paraṃ rāgavinayāya samādapaiti; attanā na dausavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, paraṃ dausavinayāya samādapaiti; attanā na mauhavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, paraṃ mauhavinayāya samādapaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau parahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau attahitāya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau attanā na rāgavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ rāgavinayāya samādapaiti; attanā na dausavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ dausavinayāya samādapaiti; attanā na mauhavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau paraṃ mauhavinayāya samādapaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau attanā ca rāgavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca rāgavinayāya samādapaiti; attanā ca dausavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca dausavinayāya samādapaiti; attanā ca  mauhavinayāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca mauhavinayāya  samādapaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. khippanisantisuttaṃ

97. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? attahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, parahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau attahitāya, naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau parahitāya ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau khippanisantī ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhārakajātikau dhāraṇajātikau (ka॰) hauti, dhātānañ‍ca dhatānañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhī hauti atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya, dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti; nau ca kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā, nau ca sandassakau hauti samādapakau samādāpakau (?) samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau parahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau attahitāya? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na haiva khau khippanisantī hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhārakajātikau hauti, nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhī hauti, nau ca atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau  hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā, sandassakau ca hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau parahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau attahitāya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na haiva khau khippanisantī hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau ca sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ dhārakajātikau hauti, nau ca dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhī hauti, nau ca atthamañ‍ñāya  dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti; nau ca kalyāṇavācau hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā, nau ca sandassakau hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau khippanisantī ca hauti kusalaisu dhammaisu, sutānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ dhārakajātikau hauti, dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhī hauti atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya, dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti; kalyāṇavācau ca hauti kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau pauriyā vācāya saman‍nāgatau vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā, sandassakau ca hauti samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīnaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau hauti  parahitāya ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. attahitasuttaṃ

98. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? attahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, parahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau attahitāya, naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau parahitāya ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sikkhāpadasuttaṃ

99. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? attahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau  parahitāya, parahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau attahitāya, naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau nau parahitāya, attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau parahitāya ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau attanā pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, nau paraṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, nau paraṃ adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, nau paraṃ kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, nau paraṃ musāvādā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti, nau paraṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau parahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau attahitāya? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau attanā pāṇātipātā appaṭiviratau hauti, paraṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā adin‍nādānā appaṭiviratau hauti, paraṃ adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā kāmaisumicchācārā appaṭiviratau hauti, paraṃ kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā musāvādā appaṭiviratau hauti, paraṃ musāvādā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā appaṭiviratau hauti, paraṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau parahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau attahitāya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti nau parahitāya? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau attanā pāṇātipātā appaṭiviratau hauti, nau paraṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā appaṭiviratau hauti, nau paraṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, nau parahitāya|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā  vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau attahitāya caiva paṭipan‍nau hauti parahitāya ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. pautaliyasuttaṃ

100. atha  khau pautaliyau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau pautaliyaṃ paribbājakaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘cattāraumai, pautaliya, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, pautaliya, aikac‍cau puggalau pu॰ pa॰ 165 avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, nau ca khau vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| idha pana, pautaliya, aikac‍cau puggalau vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, nau ca khau avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| idha pana, pautaliya, aikac‍cau puggalau naiva avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, nau ca vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| idha pana, pautaliya, aikac‍cau puggalau avaṇṇārahassa ca avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, vaṇṇārahassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| imai khau, pautaliya, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, pautaliya, catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ katamau tai puggalau khamati abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘cattāraumai, bhau gautama, puggalā  santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhau gautama, aikac‍cau puggalau avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, nau ca khau vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| idha pana, bhau gautama, aikac‍cau puggalau vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, nau ca khau avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| idha pana, bhau gautama, aikac‍cau puggalau naiva avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, nau ca vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| idha  pana, bhau gautama, aikac‍cau puggalau avaṇṇārahassa ca avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, vaṇṇārahassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina| imai khau, bhau gautama, cattārau puggalā santau  saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ, bhau gautama, catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ yvāyaṃ puggalau naiva avaṇṇārahassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, nau  ca vaṇṇārahassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina; ayaṃ mai puggalau khamati imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? abhik‍kantā abhik‍kantatarā (ka॰) haisā, bhau gautama, yadidaṃ upaikkhā’’ti|

‘‘cattāraumai, pautaliya, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau…pai॰… imai khau, pautaliya, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, pautaliya, catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ yvāyaṃ puggalau avaṇṇārahassa ca avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, vaṇṇārahassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā hauti  bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina; ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? abhik‍kantā haisā, pautaliya, yadidaṃ tattha tattha kālañ‍ñutā’’ti|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhau gautama, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau…pai॰… imai khau, bhau gautama, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ, bhau gautama, catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ yvāyaṃ puggalau avaṇṇārahassa ca avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, vaṇṇārahassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitā bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina; ayaṃ mai puggalau khamati imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? abhik‍kantā haisā, bhau gautama, yadidaṃ tattha tattha  kālañ‍ñutā|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti, aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| dasamaṃ|

asuravaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

asurau tayau samādhī, chavālātaina pañ‍camaṃ|

rāgau nisanti attahitaṃ, sikkhā pautaliyaina cāti||

dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



3. tatiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(11) 1. valāhakavaggau

1. paṭhamavalāhakasuttaṃ

101. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, valāhakā| katamai cattārau? gaj‍jitā nau vassitā, vassitā nau gaj‍jitā, naiva gaj‍jitā nau vassitā, gaj‍jitā ca vassitā ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau valāhakā| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau valāhakūpamā pu॰ pa॰ 157 puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? gaj‍jitā nau vassitā, vassitā nau gaj‍jitā, naiva gaj‍jitā nau vassitā, gaj‍jitā ca vassitā ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau gaj‍jitā hauti nau vassitā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau bhāsitā hauti, nau kattā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau gaj‍jitā hauti, nau vassitā| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, valāhakau gaj‍jitā, nau vassitā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau vassitā hauti, nau gaj‍jitā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau kattā hauti, nau bhāsitā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau vassitā hauti, nau gaj‍jitā| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, valāhakau vassitā, nau gaj‍jitā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naiva gaj‍jitā hauti, nau vassitā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau naiva bhāsitā hauti, nau kattā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau naiva gaj‍jitā hauti, nau vassitā| saiyyathāpi  sau, bhikkhavai, valāhakau naiva gaj‍jitā naiva gaj‍jitā hauti (ka॰), nau vassitā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau gaj‍jitā ca hauti vassitā ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau bhāsitā ca hauti kattā ca| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau gaj‍jitā ca hauti vassitā ca| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, valāhakau gaj‍jitā ca gaj‍jitā ca hauti (ka॰) vassitā ca; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau valāhakūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyavalāhakasuttaṃ

102. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, valāhakā| katamai cattārau? gaj‍jitā nau vassitā, vassitā nau gaj‍jitā, naiva gaj‍jitā nau vassitā gaj‍jitā ca vassitā ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau valāhakā| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau valāhakūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? gaj‍jitā nau vassitā, vassitā nau gaj‍jitā, naiva gaj‍jitā nau vassitā, gaj‍jitā ca vassitā ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau gaj‍jitā hauti, nau vassitā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti  yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau gaj‍jitā hauti, nau vassitā| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, valāhakau gaj‍jitā, nau vassitā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau vassitā hauti, nau gaj‍jitā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau  dhammaṃ na pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau vassitā hauti, nau gaj‍jitā| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, valāhakau vassitā, nau gaj‍jitā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naiva gaj‍jitā hauti, nau vassitā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau naiva dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau naiva gaj‍jitā hauti, nau vassitā| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, valāhakau naiva gaj‍jitā, nau vassitā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau gaj‍jitā ca hauti vassitā ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau gaj‍jitā ca hauti vassitā ca| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, valāhakau gaj‍jitā ca vassitā ca; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau valāhakūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kumbhasuttaṃ

103. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, kumbhā| katamai cattārau? tucchau pihitau, pūrau vivaṭau, tucchau vivaṭau, pūrau pihitau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau kumbhā| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau kumbhūpamā pu॰ pa॰ 160 puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? tucchau pihitau, pūrau vivaṭau, tucchau vivaṭau, pūrau pihitau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau tucchau hauti pihitau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau tucchau hauti pihitau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, kumbhau tucchau pihitau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau pūrau hauti vivaṭau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa na pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau pūrau hauti vivaṭau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, kumbhau pūrau vivaṭau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau tucchau hauti vivaṭau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa na pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau tucchau hauti vivaṭau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, kumbhau tucchau vivaṭau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau pūrau hauti pihitau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰…  ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau pūrau hauti pihitau| saiyyathāpi  sau, bhikkhavai, kumbhau pūrau pihitau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau kumbhūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. udakarahadasuttaṃ

104. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, udakarahadā| katamai cattārau? uttānau gambhīraubhāsau, gambhīrau uttānaubhāsau, uttānau uttānaubhāsau, gambhīrau gambhīraubhāsau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau udakarahadā| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau udakarahadūpamā pu॰ pa॰ 161 puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? uttānau gambhīraubhāsau, gambhīrau uttānaubhāsau, uttānau uttānaubhāsau, gambhīrau gambhīraubhāsau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau uttānau hauti gambhīraubhāsau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau uttānau hauti gambhīraubhāsau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, udakarahadau uttānau gambhīraubhāsau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau gambhīrau hauti uttānaubhāsau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa na pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau gambhīrau hauti uttānaubhāsau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, udakarahadau gambhīrau uttānaubhāsau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau uttānau hauti uttānaubhāsau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa na pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau uttānau hauti uttānaubhāsau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, udakarahadau uttānau uttānaubhāsau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau gambhīrau hauti gambhīraubhāsau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau gambhīrau hauti gambhīraubhāsau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, udakarahadau gambhīrau gambhīraubhāsau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ  vadāmi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau udakarahadūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. ambasuttaṃ

105. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, ambāni| katamāni cattāri? āmaṃ pak‍kavaṇṇi pak‍kavaṇṇī (pu॰ pa॰ 159), pak‍kaṃ āmavaṇṇi āmavaṇṇī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), āmaṃ āmavaṇṇi, pak‍kaṃ pak‍kavaṇṇi – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri ambāni| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau ambūpamā pak‍kavaṇṇī (pu॰ pa॰ 159) puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? āmau pak‍kavaṇṇī, pak‍kau āmavaṇṇī, āmau āmavaṇṇī, pak‍kau pak‍kavaṇṇī|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau āmau hauti pak‍kavaṇṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau āmau hauti  pak‍kavaṇṇī| saiyyathāpi taṃ, bhikkhavai, ambaṃ āmaṃ pak‍kavaṇṇi; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau pak‍kau hauti āmavaṇṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa na pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau pak‍kau hauti āmavaṇṇī| saiyyathāpi taṃ, bhikkhavai, ambaṃ pak‍kaṃ āmavaṇṇi; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau āmau hauti āmavaṇṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa na pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau āmau hauti āmavaṇṇī| saiyyathāpi taṃ, bhikkhavai, ambaṃ āmaṃ āmavaṇṇi; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau pak‍kau hauti pak‍kavaṇṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa pāsādikaṃ hauti abhik‍kantaṃ paṭik‍kantaṃ ālaukitaṃ vilaukitaṃ samiñ‍jitaṃ pasāritaṃ saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇaṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau pak‍kau hauti pak‍kavaṇṇī| saiyyathāpi taṃ, bhikkhavai, ambaṃ pak‍kaṃ pak‍kavaṇṇi; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau ambūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā  laukasmi’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaambasuttaṃ

(chaṭṭhaṃ uttānatthamaivāti aṭṭhakathāyaṃ dassitaṃ, pāḷipautthakaisu pana katthacipi na dissati|) ( ) ‘‘chaṭṭhaṃ uk‍kānatthamaivā’’ti aṭṭhakathāyaṃ dassitaṃ, pāḷipautthakaisu pana katthacipi na dissati| ‘‘… āmaṃ pak‍kaubhāsaṃ, pak‍kaṃ āmaubhāsa’’ntiādinā pāṭhau bhavaiyya

7. mūsikasuttaṃ

107. ‘‘catassau  imā, bhikkhavai, mūsikā| katamā catassau? gādhaṃ kattā nau vasitā, vasitā nau gādhaṃ kattā, naiva gādhaṃ kattā nau vasitā, gādhaṃ kattā ca vasitā ca – imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau mūsikā| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau mūsikūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? gādhaṃ kattā nau vasitā, vasitā nau gādhaṃ kattā, naiva gādhaṃ kattā nau vasitā, gādhaṃ kattā ca vasitā ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau gādhaṃ kattā hauti nau vasitā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau gādhaṃ kattā hauti, nau vasitā| saiyyathāpi sā, bhikkhavai, mūsikā gādhaṃ kattā, nau vasitā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau vasitā hauti, nau gādhaṃ kattā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dhammaṃ na pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau vasitā hauti, nau gādhaṃ kattā| saiyyathāpi sā, bhikkhavai, mūsikā vasitā hauti, nau gādhaṃ kattā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ, puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naiva gādhaṃ kattā hauti nau vasitā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dhammaṃ na pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau naiva gādhaṃ kattā hauti, nau vasitā| saiyyathāpi sā, bhikkhavai, mūsikā naiva gādhaṃ kattā hauti, nau vasitā; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau gādhaṃ kattā ca hauti vasitā ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau  dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau gādhaṃ kattā ca hauti vasitā ca| saiyyathāpi sā, bhikkhavai, mūsikā gādhaṃ kattā ca hauti vasitā ca; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau mūsikūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. balībaddasuttaṃ

108. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, balībaddā balivaddā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), balibaddhā (ka॰) pu॰ pa॰ 162| katamai cattārau? sagavacaṇḍau  nau paragavacaṇḍau, paragavacaṇḍau nau sagavacaṇḍau, sagavacaṇḍau ca paragavacaṇḍau ca, naiva sagavacaṇḍau nau paragavacaṇḍau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau balībaddā| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau balībaddūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? sagavacaṇḍau nau paragavacaṇḍau, paragavacaṇḍau nau sagavacaṇḍau, sagavacaṇḍau ca paragavacaṇḍau ca, naiva sagavacaṇḍau nau paragavacaṇḍau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau sagavacaṇḍau hauti, nau paragavacaṇḍau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sakaparisaṃ ubbaijaitā hauti, nau paraparisaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau sagavacaṇḍau hauti, nau paragavacaṇḍau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, balībaddau sagavacaṇḍau, nau paragavacaṇḍau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau paragavacaṇḍau hauti, nau sagavacaṇḍau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau paraparisaṃ ubbaijaitā hauti, nau sakaparisaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau paragavacaṇḍau  hauti, nau sagavacaṇḍau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, balībaddau paragavacaṇḍau, nau sagavacaṇḍau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau sagavacaṇḍau ca hauti paragavacaṇḍau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sakaparisaṃ ubbaijaitā hauti paraparisañ‍ca| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau sagavacaṇḍau ca hauti paragavacaṇḍau ca| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, balībaddau sagavacaṇḍau ca paragavacaṇḍau ca; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naiva sagavacaṇḍau hauti nau paragavacaṇḍau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau naiva sakaparisaṃ ubbaijaitā hauti, nau paraparisañ‍ca| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau naiva sagavacaṇḍau hauti, nau paragavacaṇḍau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, balībaddau naiva sagavacaṇḍau, nau paragavacaṇḍau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau balībaddūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. rukkhasuttaṃ

109. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, rukkhā| katamai cattārau? phaiggu  phaigguparivārau, phaiggu sāraparivārau, sārau phaigguparivārau, sārau sāraparivārau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau rukkhā| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau rukkhūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai  cattārau? phaiggu phaigguparivārau, phaiggu sāraparivārau, sārau phaigguparivārau, sārau sāraparivārau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau phaiggu hauti phaigguparivārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dussīlau hauti pāpadhammau; parisāpissa hauti dussīlā pāpadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau phaiggu hauti phaigguparivārau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, rukkhau phaiggu phaigguparivārau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau phaiggu hauti sāraparivārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dussīlau hauti pāpadhammau; parisā ca khvassa hauti sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau phaiggu hauti sāraparivārau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, rukkhau phaiggu sāraparivārau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau sārau hauti phaigguparivārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlavā hauti kalyāṇadhammau; parisā ca khvassa hauti dussīlā pāpadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau sārau hauti phaigguparivārau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sārau phaigguparivārau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau sārau hauti sāraparivārau? idha bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlavā hauti kalyāṇadhammau; parisāpissa hauti sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau sārau hauti sāraparivārau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, rukkhau sārau sāraparivārau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau rukkhūpamā puggalā santau  saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti pu॰ pa॰ 170| navamaṃ|

10. āsīvisasuttaṃ

110. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, āsīvisā āsivisā (ka॰) pu॰ pa॰ 163| katamai cattārau? āgatavisau na ghauravisau, ghauravisau na āgatavisau, āgatavisau ca ghauravisau ca, naivāgatavisau na ghauravisau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau āsīvisā| aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau āsīvisūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? āgatavisau na ghauravisau, ghauravisau na āgatavisau, āgatavisau ca ghauravisau ca, naivāgatavisau na ghauravisau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau āgatavisau hauti, na ghauravisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau abhiṇhaṃ kujjhati| sau ca khvassa kaudhau kaupau (ka॰) pu॰ pa॰ 163 na dīgharattaṃ anusaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau āgatavisau hauti, na ghauravisau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, āsīvisau āgatavisau, na ghauravisau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ghauravisau hauti, na āgatavisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na haiva khau abhiṇhaṃ kujjhati| sau ca khvassa kaudhau dīgharattaṃ anusaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau ghauravisau hauti, na āgatavisau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, āsīvisau ghauravisau, na āgatavisau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau āgatavisau ca hauti ghauravisau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau abhiṇhaṃ kujjhati| sau ca khvassa kaudhau dīgharattaṃ anusaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau āgatavisau ca hauti ghauravisau  ca| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, āsīvisau āgatavisau ca ghauravisau ca; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivāgatavisau hauti na ghauravisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na haiva khau abhiṇhaṃ kujjhati| sau ca khvassa kaudhau na dīgharattaṃ anusaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivāgatavisau hauti, na ghauravisau| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, āsīvisau naivāgatavisau na ghauravisau; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau āsīvisūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| dasamaṃ|

valāhakavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai valāhā kumbha-udaka, rahadā dvai haunti ambāni|

mūsikā balībaddā rukkhā, āsīvisaina tai dasāti||


(12) 2. kaisivaggau

1. kaisisuttaṃ

111. atha  khau kaisi assadammasārathi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kaisiṃ assadammasārathiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘tvaṃ khausi, kaisi, pañ‍ñātau assadammasārathīti sañ‍ñātau assadammasārathi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, kaisi, assadammaṃ vinaisī’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, assadammaṃ saṇhainapi vinaimi, pharusainapi vinaimi, saṇhapharusainapi vinaimī’’ti| ‘‘sacai tai, kaisi, assadammau saṇhaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, pharusaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, saṇhapharusaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, kinti naṃ karausī’’ti? ‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, assadammau saṇhaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, pharusaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, saṇhapharusaina vinayaṃ na upaiti; hanāmi naṃ, bhantai| taṃ kissa haitu? mā mai ācariyakulassa avaṇṇau ahausī’’ti|

‘‘bhagavā pana, bhantai, anuttarau purisadammasārathi| kathaṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavā purisadammaṃ vinaitī’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ khau, kaisi, purisadammaṃ saṇhainapi vinaimi, pharusainapi vinaimi, saṇhapharusainapi vinaimi| tatridaṃ, kaisi, saṇhasmiṃ – iti kāyasucaritaṃ iti kāyasucaritassa vipākau, iti vacīsucaritaṃ iti vacīsucaritassa vipākau, iti manausucaritaṃ iti manausucaritassa vipākau, iti daivā, iti manussāti| tatridaṃ, kaisi, pharusasmiṃ – iti kāyaduc‍caritaṃ iti kāyaduc‍caritassa vipākau, iti vacīduc‍caritaṃ iti vacīduc‍caritassa vipākau, iti manauduc‍caritaṃ iti manauduc‍caritassa vipākau, iti nirayau, iti tiracchānayauni, iti paittivisayau’’ti|

‘‘tatridaṃ, kaisi, saṇhapharusasmiṃ – iti kāyasucaritaṃ iti kāyasucaritassa vipākau, iti kāyaduc‍caritaṃ iti kāyaduc‍caritassa vipākau, iti vacīsucaritaṃ iti vacīsucaritassa vipākau, iti vacīduc‍caritaṃ iti vacīduc‍caritassa vipākau, iti manausucaritaṃ iti manausucaritassa  vipākau, iti manauduc‍caritaṃ iti manauduc‍caritassa vipākau, iti daivā, iti manussā, iti nirayau, iti tiracchānayauni, iti paittivisayau’’ti|

‘‘sacai  tai, bhantai, purisadammau saṇhaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, pharusaina  vinayaṃ na upaiti, saṇhapharusaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, kinti naṃ bhagavā karautī’’ti? ‘‘sacai mai, kaisi, purisadammau saṇhaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, pharusaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, saṇhapharusaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, hanāmi naṃ, kaisī’’ti| ‘‘na khau, bhantai, bhagavatau pāṇātipātau kappati| atha ca pana bhagavā aivamāha – ‘hanāmi, naṃ kaisī’’’ti! ‘‘sac‍caṃ, kaisi! na tathāgatassa pāṇātipātau kappati| api ca yau purisadammau saṇhaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, pharusaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, saṇhapharusaina vinayaṃ na upaiti, na taṃ tathāgatau vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñati, nāpi viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti| vadhau haisau, kaisi, ariyassa vinayai – yaṃ na tathāgatau vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñati, nāpi viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñantī’’ti|

‘‘sau hi nūna, bhantai, suhatau hauti – yaṃ na tathāgatau vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñati, nāpi viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī vattabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñantīti| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. javasuttaṃ

112. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi catūhi? aj‍javaina, javaina, khantiyā, saurac‍caina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti, rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi catūhi? aj‍javaina, javaina, khantiyā, saurac‍caina – imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu  āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. pataudasuttaṃ

113. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, bhadrā assājānīyā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau bhadrau assājānīyau pataudacchāyaṃ disvā saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘kiṃ nu khau maṃ aj‍ja assadammasārathi kāraṇaṃ kāraissati, kimassāhaṃ kathamassāhaṃ (ka॰) paṭikaraumī’ti! aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau assājānīyau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau bhadrau assājānīyau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau assājānīyau na haiva khau pataudacchāyaṃ disvā saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati, api ca khau laumavaidhaviddhau saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘kiṃ nu khau maṃ aj‍ja assadammasārathi kāraṇaṃ kāraissati, kimassāhaṃ paṭikaraumī’ti! aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau  assājānīyau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau bhadrau assājānīyau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau assājānīyau na haiva khau pataudacchāyaṃ disvā saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati nāpi laumavaidhaviddhau saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati, api ca khau cammavaidhaviddhau saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘kiṃ nu khau maṃ aj‍ja assadammasārathi kāraṇaṃ kāraissati, kimassāhaṃ paṭikaraumī’ti! aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau assājānīyau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau bhadrau assājānīyau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau assājānīyau na haiva khau pataudacchāyaṃ disvā saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati nāpi laumavaidhaviddhau saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati nāpi cammavaidhaviddhau saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati, api ca khau aṭṭhivaidhaviddhau saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘kiṃ  nu khau maṃ aj‍ja assadammasārathi kāraṇaṃ kāraissati, kimassāhaṃ paṭikaraumī’ti! aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau assājānīyau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau bhadrau assājānīyau santau saṃvij‍jamānau  laukasmiṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau bhadrā assājānīyā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattāraumai bhadrā purisājānīyā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau bhadrau purisājānīyau suṇāti – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma gāmai vā nigamai vā itthī vā purisau vā dukkhitau vā kālaṅkatau kālakatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā’ti| sau taina saṃvij‍jati, saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati| saṃviggau yaunisau padahati| pahitattau kāyaina  caiva paramasac‍caṃ paramatthasac‍caṃ (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.183 passitabbaṃ sacchikarauti, pañ‍ñāya ca ativijjha passati| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, bhadrau assājānīyau pataudacchāyaṃ disvā saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ bhadraṃ purisājānīyaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau purisājānīyau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau bhadrau purisājānīyau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau purisājānīyau na haiva khau suṇāti – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma gāmai vā nigamai vā itthī vā purisau vā dukkhitau vā kālaṅkatau vā’ti, api ca khau sāmaṃ passati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā dukkhitaṃ vā kālaṅkataṃ vā| sau taina saṃvij‍jati, saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati| saṃviggau yaunisau padahati| pahitattau kāyaina caiva paramasac‍caṃ sacchikarauti, pañ‍ñāya ca ativijjha passati| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, bhadrau assājānīyau laumavaidhaviddhau saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ bhadraṃ purisājānīyaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau purisājānīyau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau bhadrau purisājānīyau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau purisājānīyau na haiva khau suṇāti – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma gāmai vā nigamai vā itthī vā purisau vā dukkhitau vā kālaṅkatau vā’ti, nāpi sāmaṃ passati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā dukkhitaṃ vā kālaṅkataṃ vā, api ca khvassa ñāti vā sālauhitau vā dukkhitau vā hauti kālaṅkatau vā| sau taina saṃvij‍jati, saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati| saṃviggau  yaunisau padahati| pahitattau kāyaina  caiva paramasac‍caṃ  sacchikarauti, pañ‍ñāya ca ativijjha passati| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, bhadrau assājānīyau cammavaidhaviddhau saṃvij‍jati  saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ bhadraṃ purisājānīyaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau purisājānīyau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau bhadrau purisājānīyau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau purisājānīyau na haiva khau suṇāti – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma gāmai vā nigamai vā itthī vā purisau vā dukkhitau vā kālaṅkatau vā’ti, nāpi sāmaṃ passati itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā dukkhitaṃ vā kālaṅkataṃ vā, nāpissa ñāti vā sālauhitau vā dukkhitau vā hauti kālaṅkatau vā, api ca khau sāmañ‍ñaiva phuṭṭhau hauti sārīrikāhi vaidanāhi dukkhāhi tibbāhi tippāhi (sī॰ pī॰) kharāhi kaṭukāhi asātāhi amanāpāhi pāṇaharāhi| sau taina saṃvij‍jati, saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati| saṃviggau yaunisau padahati| pahitattau kāyaina caiva paramasac‍caṃ sacchikarauti, pañ‍ñāya ca ativijjha passati| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, bhadrau assājānīyau aṭṭhivaidhaviddhau saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati; tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ bhadraṃ purisājānīyaṃ vadāmi| aivarūpaupi, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhadrau purisājānīyau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau bhadrau purisājānīyau santau saṃvij‍jamānau laukasmiṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau bhadrā purisājānīyā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. nāgasuttaṃ

114. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau rājārahau  hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau sautā ca hauti, hantā ca, khantā ca, gantā ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau sautā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau yamainaṃ hatthidammasārathi kāraṇaṃ kāraiti – yadi vā katapubbaṃ yadi vā akatapubbaṃ – taṃ aṭṭhiṃ katvā aṭṭhikatvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.140 manasi katvā sabbacaitasā sabbaṃ caitasau (sabbattha) saman‍nāharitvā  auhitasautau suṇāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau sautā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau hantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau  hatthimpi hanati, hatthāruhampi hanati, assampi hanati, assāruhampi hanati, rathampi hanati, rathikampi hanati, pattikampi hanati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau hantā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khantā hauti? idha bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau saṅgāmagatau khamau hauti sattippahārānaṃ asippahārānaṃ usuppahārānaṃ pharasuppahārānaṃ ‘‘pharasuppahārāna’’nti idaṃ padaṃ syāmapautthakai natthi| ma॰ ni॰ 3.217 passitabbaṃ bhairipaṇavasaṅkhatiṇavanin‍nādasaddānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau khantā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau gantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau yamainaṃ hatthidammasārathi disaṃ paisaiti – yadi vā gatapubbaṃ yadi vā agatapubbaṃ – taṃ khippamaiva gantā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgau gantā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau nāgau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti  …pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautā ca hauti, hantā ca, khantā ca, gantā ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai daisiyamānai aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti (hanati) ( ) natthi sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu| a॰ ni॰ 4.164 paṭipadāvaggai catutthasuttai pana ‘‘samaitī’’ti padaṃ sabbatthapi dissati byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti hanati  byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hantā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khamau hauti sītassa  uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ  sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu khantā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gantā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāyaṃ disā agatapubbā iminā dīghaina addhunā yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ, taṃ khippañ‍ñaiva gantā  hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gantā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. ṭhānasuttaṃ

115. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, ṭhānāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaṃ amanāpaṃ kātuṃ; tañ‍ca kayiramānaṃ anatthāya saṃvattati| atthi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaṃ amanāpaṃ kātuṃ; tañ‍ca kayiramānaṃ atthāya saṃvattati| atthi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaṃ manāpaṃ kātuṃ; tañ‍ca kayiramānaṃ anatthāya saṃvattati| atthi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaṃ manāpaṃ kātuṃ; tañ‍ca kayiramānaṃ atthāya saṃvattati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ yadidaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ṭhānaṃ amanāpaṃ kātuṃ; tañ‍ca kayiramānaṃ anatthāya saṃvattati – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaṃ ubhayainaiva na kattabbaṃ mañ‍ñati pañ‍ñāyati (?)| yampidaṃ yadidaṃ (ka॰) ṭhānaṃ amanāpaṃ kātuṃ; imināpi naṃ taṃ (sī॰ pī॰) syāmapautthakai natthi na kattabbaṃ mañ‍ñati| yampidaṃ ṭhānaṃ kayiramānaṃ anatthāya saṃvattati; imināpi naṃ taṃ (pī॰) sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu natthi na kattabbaṃ mañ‍ñati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaṃ ubhayainaiva na kattabbaṃ mañ‍ñati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ ṭhānaṃ amanāpaṃ kātuṃ; tañ‍ca kayiramānaṃ atthāya saṃvattati – imasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, ṭhānai bālau ca paṇḍitau ca vaiditabbau purisathāmai  purisavīriyai purisaparak‍kamai| na, bhikkhavai, bālau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘kiñ‍cāpi khau idaṃ ṭhānaṃ amanāpaṃ kātuṃ; atha carahidaṃ  ṭhānaṃ kayiramānaṃ atthāya saṃvattatī’ti| sau taṃ ṭhānaṃ na karauti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ akayiramānaṃ anatthāya saṃvattati| paṇḍitau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti  paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘kiñ‍cāpi khau idaṃ ṭhānaṃ amanāpaṃ kātuṃ; atha  carahidaṃ ṭhānaṃ kayiramānaṃ atthāya saṃvattatī’ti| sau taṃ ṭhānaṃ karauti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ kayiramānaṃ atthāya saṃvattati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ yadidaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ṭhānaṃ manāpaṃ kātuṃ; tañ‍ca kayiramānaṃ anatthāya saṃvattati – imasmimpi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānai bālau ca paṇḍitau ca vaiditabbau purisathāmai purisavīriyai purisaparak‍kamai| na, bhikkhavai, bālau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘kiñ‍cāpi khau idaṃ ṭhānaṃ manāpaṃ kātuṃ; atha carahidaṃ ṭhānaṃ kayiramānaṃ anatthāya saṃvattatī’ti| sau taṃ ṭhānaṃ karauti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ kayiramānaṃ anatthāya saṃvattati| paṇḍitau ca khau, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘kiñ‍cāpi khau idaṃ ṭhānaṃ manāpaṃ kātuṃ; atha carahidaṃ ṭhānaṃ kayiramānaṃ anatthāya saṃvattatī’ti| sau taṃ ṭhānaṃ na karauti| tassa taṃ ṭhānaṃ akayiramānaṃ atthāya saṃvattati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ ṭhānaṃ manāpaṃ kātuṃ, tañ‍ca kayiramānaṃ atthāya saṃvattati – idaṃ, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaṃ ubhayainaiva kattabbaṃ mañ‍ñati| yampidaṃ ṭhānaṃ manāpaṃ kātuṃ, imināpi naṃ kattabbaṃ mañ‍ñati; yampidaṃ ṭhānaṃ kayiramānaṃ atthāya saṃvattati, imināpi naṃ kattabbaṃ mañ‍ñati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaṃ ubhayainaiva kattabbaṃ mañ‍ñati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri ṭhānānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. appamādasuttaṃ

116. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaihi appamādau karaṇīyau| katamaihi catūhi? kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, bhikkhavai, pajahatha, kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaitha; tattha ca mā pamādattha| vacīduc‍caritaṃ, bhikkhavai, pajahatha, vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaitha; tattha ca mā pamādattha| manauduc‍caritaṃ, bhikkhavai, pajahatha, manausucaritaṃ bhāvaitha; tattha ca mā pamādattha| micchādiṭṭhiṃ, bhikkhavai, pajahatha, sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaitha; tattha ca mā pamādattha|

‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvitaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ  pahīnaṃ hauti vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvitaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti manausucaritaṃ bhāvitaṃ, micchādiṭṭhi pahīnā hauti sammādiṭṭhi bhāvitā, sau na bhāyati samparāyikassa maraṇassā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ārakkhasuttaṃ

117. ‘‘catūsu, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaisu attarūpaina appamādau sati caitasau ārakkhau karaṇīyau| katamaisu catūsu? ‘mā mai rajanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ raj‍jī’ti attarūpaina appamādau sati caitasau ārakkhau karaṇīyau; ‘mā mai dausanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ dussī’ti attarūpaina appamādau sati caitasau ārakkhau karaṇīyau; ‘mā mai mauhanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ muyhī’ti attarūpaina appamādau sati caitasau ārakkhau karaṇīyau; ‘mā mai madanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ maj‍jī’ti attarūpaina appamādau sati caitasau ārakkhau karaṇīyau|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau rajanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ na raj‍jati vītarāgattā, dausanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ na dussati vītadausattā, mauhanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ na muyhati vītamauhattā, madanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ na maj‍jati vītamadattā, sau na chambhati na kampati na vaidhati na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati, na ca pana samaṇavacanahaitupi gacchatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. saṃvaijanīyasuttaṃ

118. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyāni saṃvaijanīyāni ṭhānāni| katamāni cattāri? ‘idha tathāgatau jātau’ti, bhikkhavai, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyaṃ saṃvaijanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ| ‘idha tathāgatau anuttaraṃ  sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti, bhikkhavai, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyaṃ saṃvaijanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ| ‘idha tathāgatau anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaisī’ti, bhikkhavai, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyaṃ saṃvaijanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ| ‘idha tathāgatau anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbutau’ti, bhikkhavai, saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyaṃ saṃvaijanīyaṃ ṭhānaṃ| imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri saddhassa kulaputtassa dassanīyāni saṃvaijanīyāni ṭhānānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamabhayasuttaṃ

119. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, bhayāni| katamāni cattāri? jātibhayaṃ, jarābhayaṃ, byādhibhayaṃ, maraṇabhayaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri bhayānī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyabhayasuttaṃ

120. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, bhayāni| katamāni cattāri? aggibhayaṃ, udakabhayaṃ, rājabhayaṃ, caurabhayaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri bhayānī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

kaisivaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kaisi javau pataudau ca, nāgau ṭhānaina pañ‍camaṃ|

appamādau ca ārakkhau, saṃvaijanīyañ‍ca dvai bhayāti||


(13) 3. bhayavaggau

1. attānuvādasuttaṃ

121. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, bhayāni| katamāni cattāri? attānuvādabhayaṃ, parānuvādabhayaṃ, daṇḍabhayaṃ, duggatibhayaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, attānuvādabhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahañ‍caiva ahañ‍cai (?) khau  pana kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caraiyyaṃ, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caraiyyaṃ, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caraiyyaṃ, kiñ‍ca taṃ yaṃ maṃ kiñ‍ca taṃ maṃ (sī॰), kiñ‍ca maṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), kiñ‍ca taṃ kammaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) attā sīlatau na upavadaiyyā’ti! sau attānuvādabhayassa bhītau kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya  kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, vacīduc‍caritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, manauduc‍caritaṃ pahāya manausucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, attānuvādabhayaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, parānuvādabhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahañ‍caiva khau pana kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caraiyyaṃ, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caraiyyaṃ, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caraiyyaṃ, kiñ‍ca taṃ yaṃ maṃ parai sīlatau na upavadaiyyu’nti! sau parānuvādabhayassa bhītau kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, vacīduc‍caritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, manauduc‍caritaṃ pahāya manausucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, parānuvādabhayaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daṇḍabhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau passati cauraṃ āgucāriṃ, rājānau gahaitvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kāraintai, kasāhipi tāḷaintai, vaittaihipi tāḷaintai, addhadaṇḍakaihipi tāḷaintai, hatthampi chindantai, pādampi chindantai, hatthapādampi chindantai, kaṇṇampi chindantai, nāsampi chindantai, kaṇṇanāsampi chindantai, bilaṅgathālikampi karauntai, saṅkhamuṇḍikampi karauntai, rāhumukhampi karauntai, jautimālikampi karauntai, hatthapaj‍jautikampi karauntai, airakavattikampi  karauntai, cīrakavāsikampi karauntai, aiṇaiyyakampi karauntai, balisamaṃsikampi karauntai, kahāpaṇakampi karauntai, khārāpatacchikampi  karauntai, palighaparivattikampi karauntai, palālapīṭhakampi karauntai, tattainapi tailaina ausiñ‍cantai, sunakhaihipi khādāpaintai, jīvantampi sūlai uttāsaintai, asināpi sīsaṃ chindantai|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yathārūpānaṃ khau pāpakānaṃ kammānaṃ haitu cauraṃ āgucāriṃ rājānau gahaitvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kārainti, kasāhipi tāḷainti…pai॰… asināpi sīsaṃ chindanti, ahañ‍caiva khau pana aivarūpaṃ pāpakammaṃ karaiyyaṃ, mampi rājānau gahaitvā aivarūpā vividhā kammakāraṇā kāraiyyuṃ, kasāhipi tāḷaiyyuṃ, vaittaihipi tāḷaiyyuṃ, addhadaṇḍakaihipi tāḷaiyyuṃ, hatthampi chindaiyyuṃ, pādampi chindaiyyuṃ, hatthapādampi chindaiyyuṃ, kaṇṇampi chindaiyyuṃ, nāsampi chindaiyyuṃ, kaṇṇanāsampi chindaiyyuṃ, bilaṅgathālikampi karaiyyuṃ, saṅkhamuṇḍikampi karaiyyuṃ; rāhumukhampi karaiyyuṃ, jautimālikampi karaiyyuṃ, hatthapaj‍jautikampi karaiyyuṃ, airakavattikampi karaiyyuṃ, cīrakavāsikampi karaiyyuṃ, aiṇaiyyakampi karaiyyuṃ, balisamaṃsikampi karaiyyuṃ, kahāpaṇakampi  karaiyyuṃ, khārāpatacchikampi karaiyyuṃ, palighaparivattikampi karaiyyuṃ, palālapīṭhakampi karaiyyuṃ, tattainapi tailaina ausiñ‍caiyyuṃ, sunakhaihipi khādāpaiyyuṃ, jīvantampi sūlai uttāsaiyyuṃ, asināpi sīsaṃ chindaiyyu’nti| sau daṇḍabhayassa bhītau na paraisaṃ pābhataṃ vilumpantau carati| kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya…pai॰… suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, daṇḍabhayaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, duggatibhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘kāyaduc‍caritassa khau pāpakau vipākau abhisamparāyaṃ, vacīduc‍caritassa pāpakau vipākau abhisamparāyaṃ, manauduc‍caritassa pāpakau vipākau abhisamparāyaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caraiyyaṃ, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caraiyyaṃ, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caraiyyaṃ, kiñ‍ca taṃ yāhaṃ na kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti! sau duggatibhayassa bhītau kāyaduc‍caritaṃ pahāya kāyasucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, vacīduc‍caritaṃ pahāya vacīsucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, manauduc‍caritaṃ pahāya manausucaritaṃ bhāvaiti, suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, duggatibhayaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri bhayānī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ūmibhayasuttaṃ

122. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, bhayāni udakaurauhantassa udakaurauhantai (ma॰ ni॰ 2.161) pāṭikaṅkhitabbāni| katamāni cattāri? ūmibhayaṃ, kumbhīlabhayaṃ, āvaṭṭabhayaṃ, susukābhayaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri bhayāni udakaurauhantassa pāṭikaṅkhitabbāni| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri bhayāni idhaikac‍cassa kulaputtassa imasmiṃ dhammavinayai agārasmā saddhā agārasmā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) anagāriyaṃ pabbajitassa pabbajatau (sī॰) pāṭikaṅkhitabbāni| katamāni cattāri? ūmibhayaṃ, kumbhīlabhayaṃ, āvaṭṭabhayaṃ, susukābhayaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, ūmibhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau  dukkhaparaitau; appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti! tamainaṃ tathā pabbajitaṃ samānaṃ sabrahmacārinau auvadanti anusāsanti – ‘aivaṃ tai abhik‍kamitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai paṭik‍kamitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai ālaukaitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai vilaukaitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai samiñ‍jitabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai pasāritabbaṃ, aivaṃ tai saṅghāṭipattacīvaraṃ dhāraitabba’nti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘mayaṃ khau pubbai agāriyabhūtā samānā añ‍ñai auvadāmapi anusāsāmapi| imai panamhākaṃ puttamattā mañ‍ñai nattamattā mañ‍ñai auvaditabbaṃ anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñantī’ti| sau kupitau anattamanau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ūmibhayassa bhītau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattau| ūmibhayanti khau, bhikkhavai, kaudhūpāyāsassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ūmibhayaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kumbhīlabhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi  jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau; appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti! tamainaṃ tathā pabbajitaṃ samānaṃ sabrahmacārinau auvadanti anusāsanti – ‘idaṃ tai khāditabbaṃ, idaṃ tai na khāditabbaṃ, idaṃ tai bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, idaṃ tai na bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, idaṃ tai sāyitabbaṃ, idaṃ tai na sāyitabbaṃ, idaṃ tai pātabbaṃ, idaṃ tai na pātabbaṃ, kappiyaṃ tai khāditabbaṃ, akappiyaṃ tai na khāditabbaṃ, kappiyaṃ tai bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, akappiyaṃ tai na bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, kappiyaṃ tai sāyitabbaṃ, akappiyaṃ tai na sāyitabbaṃ, kappiyaṃ tai pātabbaṃ, akappiyaṃ tai na pātabbaṃ, kālai tai khāditabbaṃ, vikālai tai na khāditabbaṃ, kālai tai bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, vikālai tai na bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, kālai tai sāyitabbaṃ, vikālai tai na sāyitabbaṃ, kālai tai pātabbaṃ, vikālai tai na pātabba’nti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘mayaṃ khau pubbai agāriyabhūtā samānā yaṃ icchāma taṃ khādāma, yaṃ na icchāma na taṃ khādāma; yaṃ icchāma taṃ bhuñ‍jāma, yaṃ na icchāma na taṃ bhuñ‍jāma; yaṃ icchāma taṃ sāyāma, yaṃ na icchāma na taṃ sāyāma; yaṃ icchāma taṃ pivāma, yaṃ na icchāma na taṃ pivāma; kappiyampi khādāma akappiyampi khādāma kappiyampi bhuñ‍jāma akappiyampi bhuñ‍jāma kappiyampi sāyāma akappiyampi sāyāma kappiyampi pivāma akappiyampi pivāma, kālaipi khādāma vikālaipi khādāma kālaipi bhuñ‍jāma vikālaipi bhuñ‍jāma kālaipi  sāyāma  vikālaipi sāyāma kālaipi pivāma  vikālaipi pivāma; yampi nau saddhā gahapatikā divā vikālai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā dainti, tatrapimai tatthapimai (ma॰ ni॰ 2.163) mukhāvaraṇaṃ mañ‍ñai karauntī’ti| sau kupitau anattamanau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kumbhīlabhayassa bhītau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattau| kumbhīlabhayanti khau, bhikkhavai, audarikattassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kumbhīlabhayaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, āvaṭṭabhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi, dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau; appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti! sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya arakkhitaina cittaina anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tattha passati gahapatiṃ vā gahapatiputtaṃ vā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitaṃ samaṅgībhūtaṃ paricārayamānaṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘mayaṃ khau pubbai agāriyabhūtā samānā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricārimhā; saṃvij‍janti khau pana mai kulai bhaugā| sak‍kā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jituṃ puñ‍ñāni ca kātuṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jaiyyaṃ puñ‍ñāni ca karaiyya’nti! sau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āvaṭṭabhayassa bhītau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattau| āvaṭṭabhayanti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, āvaṭṭabhayaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, susukābhayaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau; appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti! sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ  vā piṇḍāya pavisati  arakkhitainaiva kāyaina arakkhitāya vācāya arakkhitaina cittaina anupaṭṭhitāya satiyā asaṃvutaihi indriyaihi| sau tattha passati mātugāmaṃ dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā| tassa mātugāmaṃ disvā dun‍nivatthaṃ vā duppārutaṃ vā rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| sau rāgānuddhaṃsitaina cittaina sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu susukābhayassa bhītau sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattau| susukābhayanti khau, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, susukābhayaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri bhayāni idhaikac‍cassa kulaputtassa imasmiṃ dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitassa pāṭikaṅkhitabbānī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. paṭhamanānākaraṇasuttaṃ

123. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai  cattārau? kathā॰ 671 ādayau idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tadassādaiti, taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| tattha ṭhitau tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| brahmakāyikānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ kappau āyuppamāṇaṃ| tattha puthuj‍janau yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā nirayampi gacchati tiracchānayaunimpi gacchati paittivisayampi gacchati| bhagavatau pana sāvakau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ  daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā tasmiṃyaiva bhavai parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janaina, yadidaṃ gatiyā upapattiyā sati yadidaṃ cutiyā upapattiyā cāti (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.117 passitabbaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tadassādaiti, taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| tattha ṭhitau tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī  aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau ābhassarānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ābhassarānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ dvai kappā āyuppamāṇaṃ| tattha puthuj‍janau yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā nirayampi gacchati tiracchānayaunimpi gacchati paittivisayampi gacchati| bhagavatau pana  sāvakau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā tasmiṃyaiva bhavai parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janaina, yadidaṃ gatiyā upapattiyā sati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tadassādaiti, taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| tattha ṭhitau tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau, kālaṃ kurumānau subhakiṇhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| subhakiṇhānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ cattārau kappā āyuppamāṇaṃ| tattha puthuj‍janau yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā nirayampi gacchati tiracchānayaunimpi gacchati paittivisayampi gacchati| bhagavatau pana sāvakau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā tasmiṃyaiva bhavai parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janaina, yadidaṃ gatiyā upapattiyā sati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva  saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tadassādaiti, taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ  āpaj‍jati| tattha ṭhitau  tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau vaihapphalānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| vaihapphalānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ pañ‍ca kappasatāni āyuppamāṇaṃ| tattha puthuj‍janau yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā nirayampi gacchati tiracchānayaunimpi gacchati  paittivisayampi gacchati| bhagavatau pana sāvakau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā tasmiṃyaiva bhavai parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janaina, yadidaṃ gatiyā upapattiyā sati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyanānākaraṇasuttaṃ

124. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā suddhāvāsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, upapatti asādhāraṇā puthuj‍janaihi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā suddhāvāsānaṃ  daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, upapatti asādhāraṇā puthuj‍janaihi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamamaittāsuttaṃ
