125. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ  disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ  maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau tadassādaiti, taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| tattha ṭhitau tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| brahmakāyikānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ kappau āyuppamāṇaṃ| tattha puthuj‍janau yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā nirayampi gacchati tiracchānayaunimpi gacchati paittivisayampi gacchati| bhagavatau pana sāvakau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā tasmiṃyaiva bhavai parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janaina, yadidaṃ gatiyā upapattiyā sati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰…  upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau tadassādaiti, taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati| tattha ṭhitau tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau ābhassarānaṃ syāmapautthakai pana karuṇādayau tayau vihārā ābhassarādīhi tīhi visuṃ visuṃ yaujaitvā paripuṇṇamaiva dassitaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ābhassarānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ dvai kappā āyuppamāṇaṃ…pai॰… subhakiṇhānaṃ syāmapautthakai pana karuṇādayau tayau vihārā ābhassarādīhi tīhi visuṃ visuṃ yaujaitvā paripuṇṇamaiva dassitaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| subhakiṇhānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ cattārau kappā āyuppamāṇaṃ…pai॰… vaihapphalānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| vaihapphalānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ  pañ‍ca kappasatāni āyuppamāṇaṃ| tattha puthuj‍janau yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā nirayampi gacchati tiracchānayaunimpi gacchati paittivisayampi gacchati| bhagavatau pana sāvakau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā  yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā tasmiṃyaiva bhavai parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau  ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janaina, yadidaṃ gatiyā upapattiyā sati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyamaittāsuttaṃ

126. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā suddhāvāsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, upapatti asādhāraṇā puthuj‍janaihi|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau karuṇā…pai॰… muditā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā suddhāvāsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, upapatti asādhāraṇā puthuj‍janaihi| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamatathāgataacchariyasuttaṃ

127. ‘‘tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā abbhutadhammā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) pātubhavanti| katamai cattārau? yadā, bhikkhavai, baudhisattau tusitā kāyā cavitvā satau sampajānau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamati, atha sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yāpi tā laukantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārā andhakāratimisā yatthapimaisaṃ yatthimaisaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) candimasūriyānaṃ aivaṃmahiddhikānaṃ aivaṃmahānubhāvānaṃ ābhā nānubhaunti, tatthapi appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi tainaubhāsaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍jānanti – ‘añ‍ñaipi kira, bhau, santi sattā idhūpapan‍nā’ti| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā ayaṃ paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau abbhutadhammau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) pātubhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yadā baudhisattau satau sampajānau mātukucchimhā nikkhamati, atha sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yāpi tā laukantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārā andhakāratimisā yatthapimaisaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ aivaṃmahiddhikānaṃ aivaṃmahānubhāvānaṃ ābhā nānubhaunti, tatthapi appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi tainaubhāsaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍jānanti – ‘añ‍ñaipi kira, bhau, santi sattā idhūpapan‍nā’ti| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa  pātubhāvā ayaṃ dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pātubhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yadā tathāgatau anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhati, atha sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau  pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yāpi tā laukantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārā andhakāratimisā yatthapimaisaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ aivaṃmahiddhikānaṃ aivaṃmahānubhāvānaṃ ābhā nānubhaunti, tatthapi appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi tainaubhāsaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍jānanti  – ‘añ‍ñaipi kira, bhau, santi sattā idhūpapan‍nā’ti| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā ayaṃ tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pātubhavati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yadā tathāgatau anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti, atha sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yāpi tā laukantarikā aghā asaṃvutā andhakārā andhakāratimisā yatthapimaisaṃ candimasūriyānaṃ aivaṃmahiddhikānaṃ aivaṃmahānubhāvānaṃ ābhā nānubhaunti, tatthapi appamāṇau uḷārau aubhāsau pātubhavati atik‍kammaiva daivānaṃ daivānubhāvaṃ| yaipi tattha sattā upapan‍nā taipi tainaubhāsaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍jānanti – ‘añ‍ñaipi kira, bhau, santi sattā idhūpapan‍nā’ti| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā ayaṃ catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pātubhavati| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā imai cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā pātubhavantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyatathāgataacchariyasuttaṃ

128. ‘‘tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā pātubhavanti| katamai cattārau? ālayārāmā ālayarāmā (añ‍ñasuttaisu), bhikkhavai, pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā; sā tathāgataina anālayai dhammai daisiyamānai sussūsati sautaṃ audahati añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā ayaṃ paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pātubhavati|

‘‘mānārāmā, bhikkhavai, pajā mānaratā mānasammuditā| sā  tathāgataina mānavinayai dhammai daisiyamānai sussūsati sautaṃ audahati añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā ayaṃ dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pātubhavati|

‘‘anupasamārāmā, bhikkhavai, pajā anupasamaratā anupasamasammuditā| sā tathāgataina aupasamikai dhammai daisiyamānai sussūsati sautaṃ audahati añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā ayaṃ tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pātubhavati|

‘‘avij‍jāgatā, bhikkhavai, pajā aṇḍabhūtā pariyaunaddhā| sā tathāgataina avij‍jāvinayai dhammai daisiyamānai sussūsati sautaṃ audahati añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā ayaṃ catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pātubhavati| tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvā imai cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā pātubhavantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ānandaacchariyasuttaṃ

129. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, acchariyā abbhutā dhammā ānandai| katamai cattārau? sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuparisā hauti, atha ānandau tuṇhī bhavati|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniparisā ānandaṃ  dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā hauti| tattha cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniparisā hauti, atha ānandau tuṇhī bhavati|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, upāsakaparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi  sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, upāsakaparisā hauti, atha ānandau tuṇhī bhavati|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, upāsikāparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, upāsikāparisā hauti, atha ānandau tuṇhī bhavati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā ānandai’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. cak‍kavattiacchariyasuttaṃ

130. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, acchariyā abbhutā dhammā rañ‍ñai cak‍kavattimhi| katamai cattārau? sacai, bhikkhavai, khattiyaparisā rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai rājā cak‍kavattī bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, khattiyaparisā hauti, atha rājā cak‍kavattī tuṇhī bhavati|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇaparisā rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai rājā cak‍kavattī bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇaparisā hauti, atha rājā cak‍kavattī tuṇhī bhavati|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, gahapatiparisā rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā  hauti| tatra cai rājā cak‍kavattī bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, gahapatiparisā hauti, atha rājā cak‍kavattī tuṇhī bhavati|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, samaṇaparisā rājānaṃ cak‍kavattiṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai rājā cak‍kavattī bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, samaṇaparisā hauti, atha rājā cak‍kavattī tuṇhī bhavati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā rañ‍ñai cak‍kavattimhi|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau cattāraumai (ka॰) acchariyā abbhutā dhammā ānandai| katamai cattārau? sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuparisā hauti, atha ānandau tuṇhī bhavati|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniparisā…pai॰… sacai, bhikkhavai, upāsakaparisā…pai॰… sacai, bhikkhavai, upāsikāparisā ānandaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamati, dassanainapi sā attamanā hauti| tatra cai ānandau dhammaṃ bhāsati, bhāsitainapi sā attamanā hauti| atittāva, bhikkhavai, upāsikāparisā hauti, atha ānandau tuṇhī bhavati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā ānandai’’ti| dasamaṃ|

bhayavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

attānuvādaūmi ca, dvai ca nānā dvai ca haunti|

maittā dvai ca acchariyā, aparā ca tathā duvaiti||


(14) 4. puggalavaggau

1. saṃyaujanasuttaṃ

131. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni haunti, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni haunti, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni haunti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni haunti, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni haunti|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni haunti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti|

‘‘katamassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni? sakadāgāmissa| imassa khau, bhikkhavai, puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni|

‘‘katamassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni  appahīnāni? uddhaṃsautassa akaniṭṭhagāminau| imassa khau, bhikkhavai, puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni|

‘‘katamassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni? antarāparinibbāyissa| imassa khau, bhikkhavai, puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni|

‘‘katamassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni? arahatau| imassa khau, bhikkhavai, puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, upapattipaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, bhavapaṭilābhiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭibhānasuttaṃ

132. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? yuttappaṭibhānau, nau muttappaṭibhānau; muttappaṭibhānau, nau yuttappaṭibhānau; yuttappaṭibhānau ca muttappaṭibhānau ca; naiva yuttappaṭibhānau na muttappaṭibhānau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti pu॰ pa॰ 152 ādayau| dutiyaṃ|

3. ugghaṭitañ‍ñūsuttaṃ

133. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? ugghaṭitañ‍ñū, vipañ‍citañ‍ñū, naiyyau, padaparamau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti pu॰ pa॰ 152 ādayau| tatiyaṃ|

4. uṭṭhānaphalasuttaṃ

134. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? uṭṭhānaphalūpajīvī na kammaphalūpajīvī, kammaphalūpajīvī na uṭṭhānaphalūpajīvī, uṭṭhānaphalūpajīvī caiva kammaphalūpajīvī ca, naiva uṭṭhānaphalūpajīvī na kammaphalūpajīvī – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti pu॰ pa॰ 167| catutthaṃ|

5. sāvaj‍jasuttaṃ

135. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā pu॰ pa॰ 144 ādayau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? sāvaj‍jau, vaj‍jabahulau, appavaj‍jau, anavaj‍jau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau sāvaj‍jau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sāvaj‍jaina kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, sāvaj‍jaina vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, sāvaj‍jaina manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau sāvaj‍jau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau vaj‍jabahulau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sāvaj‍jaina bahulaṃ kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, appaṃ anavaj‍jaina; sāvaj‍jaina bahulaṃ vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, appaṃ anavaj‍jaina; sāvaj‍jaina bahulaṃ manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, appaṃ anavaj‍jaina| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau vaj‍jabahulau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau appavaj‍jau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau anavaj‍jaina bahulaṃ kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau  hauti, appaṃ sāvaj‍jaina; anavaj‍jaina bahulaṃ vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, appaṃ sāvaj‍jaina; anavaj‍jaina bahulaṃ manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, appaṃ sāvaj‍jaina| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau appavaj‍jau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau anavaj‍jau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau anavaj‍jaina kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, anavaj‍jaina vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, anavaj‍jaina manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau anavaj‍jau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamasīlasuttaṃ

136. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu na paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ na paripūrakārī, pañ‍ñāya na paripūrakārī|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ na paripūrakārī, pañ‍ñāya na paripūrakārī|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī, pañ‍ñāya na paripūrakārī|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī, pañ‍ñāya paripūrakārī| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyasīlasuttaṃ

137. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau na sīlagaru hauti na sīlādhipataiyyau, na samādhigaru hauti na samādhādhipataiyyau, na  pañ‍ñāgaru hauti na pañ‍ñādhipataiyyau|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlagaru hauti sīlādhipataiyyau, na samādhigaru hauti na samādhādhipataiyyau, na pañ‍ñāgaru hauti na pañ‍ñādhipataiyyau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlagaru hauti sīlādhipataiyyau, samādhigaru hauti samādhādhipataiyyau, na pañ‍ñāgaru hauti na pañ‍ñādhipataiyyau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlagaru hauti sīlādhipataiyyau, samādhigaru hauti samādhādhipataiyyau, pañ‍ñāgaru hauti pañ‍ñādhipataiyyau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. nikaṭṭhasuttaṃ

138. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? nikaṭṭhakāyau anikaṭṭhacittau, anikaṭṭhakāyau nikaṭṭhacittau, anikaṭṭhakāyau ca anikaṭṭhacittau ca, nikaṭṭhakāyau ca nikaṭṭhacittau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau nikaṭṭhakāyau hauti anikaṭṭhacittau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni arañ‍ñai vanapatthāni (sī॰ pī॰) pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati| sau tattha kāmavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti byāpādavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti vihiṃsāvitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau nikaṭṭhakāyau hauti anikaṭṭhacittau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau anikaṭṭhakāyau hauti nikaṭṭhacittau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau nahaiva khau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati| sau tattha naikkhammavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti abyāpādavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti avihiṃsāvitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau anikaṭṭhakāyau hauti  nikaṭṭhacittau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau anikaṭṭhakāyau ca hauti anikaṭṭhacittau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau nahaiva khau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni  paṭisaivati| sau  tattha kāmavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti byāpādavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti vihiṃsāvitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau anikaṭṭhakāyau ca hauti anikaṭṭhacittau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau nikaṭṭhakāyau ca hauti nikaṭṭhacittau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati| sau tattha naikkhammavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti abyāpādavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti avihiṃsāvitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau nikaṭṭhakāyau ca hauti nikaṭṭhacittau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhammakathikasuttaṃ

139. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, dhammakathikā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau dhammakathikau appañ‍ca bhāsati asahitañ‍ca; parisā cassa parisā ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pu॰ pa॰ 156 na kusalā hauti sahitāsahitassa| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, dhammakathikau aivarūpāya parisāya dhammakathikautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau dhammakathikau appañ‍ca bhāsati sahitañ‍ca; parisā cassa kusalā hauti sahitāsahitassa| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, dhammakathikau aivarūpāya parisāya dhammakathikautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau dhammakathikau bahuñ‍ca bhāsati asahitañ‍ca; parisā cassa na kusalā hauti sahitāsahitassa| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, dhammakathikau aivarūpāya parisāya dhammakathikautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau dhammakathikau bahuñ‍ca  bhāsati sahitañ‍ca; parisā cassa kusalā hauti sahitāsahitassa| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, dhammakathikau aivarūpāya parisāya dhammakathikautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau dhammakathikā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. vādīsuttaṃ

140. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, vādī| katamai cattārau? atthi, bhikkhavai, vādī atthatau pariyādānaṃ gacchati, nau byañ‍janatau; atthi, bhikkhavai, vādī byañ‍janatau  pariyādānaṃ gacchati, nau atthatau; atthi, bhikkhavai, vādī atthatau ca byañ‍janatau ca pariyādānaṃ gacchati; atthi, bhikkhavai, vādī naivatthatau nau byañ‍janatau pariyādānaṃ gacchati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau vādī| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau yaṃ catūhi paṭisambhidāhi saman‍nāgatau saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) atthatau vā byañ‍janatau vā pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

puggalavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

saṃyaujanaṃ paṭibhānau, ugghaṭitañ‍ñu uṭṭhānaṃ|

sāvaj‍jau dvai ca sīlāni, nikaṭṭha dhamma vādī cāti||


(15) 5. ābhāvaggau

1. ābhāsuttaṃ

141. ‘‘catassau  imā, bhikkhavai, ābhā| katamā catassau? candābhā, sūriyābhā, aggābhā, pañ‍ñābhā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau ābhā| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ catassan‍naṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) saddanītipadamālā passitabbā ābhānaṃ yadidaṃ pañ‍ñābhā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pabhāsuttaṃ

142. ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, pabhā| katamā catassau? candappabhā, sūriyappabhā, aggippabhā, pañ‍ñāpabhā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau pabhā| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, imāsaṃ catun‍naṃ pabhānaṃ yadidaṃ pañ‍ñāpabhā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. ālaukasuttaṃ

143. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, ālaukā| katamai cattārau? candālaukau, sūriyālaukau, aggālaukau, pañ‍ñālaukau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau ālaukā| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ ālaukānaṃ yadidaṃ pañ‍ñālaukau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. aubhāsasuttaṃ

144. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, aubhāsā| katamai cattārau? candaubhāsau, sūriyaubhāsau, aggaubhāsau, pañ‍ñaubhāsau – imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau aubhāsā| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ aubhāsānaṃ yadidaṃ pañ‍ñaubhāsau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paj‍jautasuttaṃ

145. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, paj‍jautā| katamai cattārau? candapaj‍jautau, sūriyapaj‍jautau, aggipaj‍jautau, pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau paj‍jautā| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ paj‍jautānaṃ yadidaṃ pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamakālasuttaṃ

146. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, kālā| katamai cattārau? kālaina dhammassavanaṃ, kālaina dhammasākacchā, kālaina sammasanā kālaina samathau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), kālaina vipassanā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau kālā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyakālasuttaṃ

147. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, kālā sammā bhāviyamānā sammā anuparivattiyamānā anupubbaina āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpainti| katamai cattārau? kālaina dhammassavanaṃ, kālaina dhammasākacchā, kālaina sammasanā, kālaina vipassanā  – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau kālā sammā bhāviyamānā sammā anuparivattiyamānā anupubbaina āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpainti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uparipabbatai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai taṃ udakaṃ yathānin‍naṃ pavattamānaṃ pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūraiti; pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūrā kusaubbhai paripūrainti; kusaubbhā paripūrā mahāsaubbhai paripūrainti; mahāsaubbhā paripūrā kun‍nadiyau paripūrainti; kun‍nadiyau  paripūrā mahānadiyau paripūrainti; mahānadiyau paripūrā samuddaṃ samuddaṃ sāgaraṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), samuddasāgaraṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) paripūrainti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imai cattārau kālā sammā bhāviyamānā sammā anuparivattiyamānā anupubbaina āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpaintī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. duc‍caritasuttaṃ

148. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, vacīduc‍caritāni| katamāni cattāri? musāvādau, pisuṇā vācā, pharusā vācā, samphappalāpau – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri vacīduc‍caritānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sucaritasuttaṃ

149. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, vacīsucaritāni| katamāni cattāri? sac‍cavācā, apisuṇā vācā, saṇhā vācā, mantabhāsā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri vacīsucaritānī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. sārasuttaṃ

150. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, sārā| katamai cattārau? sīlasārau, samādhisārau, pañ‍ñāsārau, vimuttisārau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau sārā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

ābhāvaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ābhā pabhā ca ālaukā, aubhāsā caiva paj‍jautā|

dvai kālā caritā dvai ca, haunti sāraina tai dasāti||

tatiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



4. catutthapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(16) 1. indriyavaggau

1. indriyasuttaṃ

151. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni| katamāni cattāri? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri indriyānī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. saddhābalasuttaṃ

152. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni cattāri? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri balānī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. pañ‍ñābalasuttaṃ

153. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni cattāri? pañ‍ñābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, anavaj‍jabalaṃ, saṅgahabalaṃ saṅgāhabalaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri balānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. satibalasuttaṃ

154. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni cattāri? satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, anavaj‍jabalaṃ, saṅgahabalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri balānī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭisaṅkhānabalasuttaṃ

155. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni cattāri? paṭisaṅkhānabalaṃ, bhāvanābalaṃ, anavaj‍jabalaṃ, saṅgahabalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri balānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kappasuttaṃ

156. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, kappassa asaṅkhyaiyyāni| katamāni cattāri? yadā, bhikkhavai, kappau saṃvaṭṭati, taṃ na sukaraṃ saṅkhātuṃ  – aittakāni vassānīti vā, aittakāni vassasatānīti vā, aittakāni vassasahassānīti vā, aittakāni vassasatasahassānīti vā|

‘‘yadā, bhikkhavai, kappau saṃvaṭṭau tiṭṭhati, taṃ na sukaraṃ saṅkhātuṃ – aittakāni vassānīti vā, aittakāni vassasatānīti vā, aittakāni vassasahassānīti vā, aittakāni vassasatasahassānīti vā|

‘‘yadā, bhikkhavai, kappau vivaṭṭati, taṃ na sukaraṃ saṅkhātuṃ – aittakāni vassānīti vā, aittakāni vassasatānīti vā, aittakāni vassasahassānīti vā, aittakāni vassasatasahassānīti vā|

‘‘yadā, bhikkhavai, kappau vivaṭṭau tiṭṭhati, taṃ na sukaraṃ saṅkhātuṃ – aittakāni vassānīti vā, aittakāni vassasatānīti vā, aittakāni vassasahassānīti vā, aittakāni vassasatasahassānīti vā| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri kappassa asaṅkhyaiyyānī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. raugasuttaṃ

157. ‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, raugā| katamai dvai? kāyikau  ca raugau caitasikau ca raugau| dissanti, bhikkhavai, sattā kāyikaina raugaina aikampi vassaṃ āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, dvaipi  vassāni āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, tīṇipi vassāni āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, cattāripi vassāni āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, pañ‍capi vassāni āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, dasapi vassāni āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, vīsatipi vassāni āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, tiṃsampi vassāni āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, cattārīsampi vassāni āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, pañ‍ñāsampi vassāni āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā, vassasatampi, bhiyyaupi āraugyaṃ paṭijānamānā| tai, bhikkhavai, sattā sudul‍labhā dul‍labhā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) laukasmiṃ yai caitasikaina raugaina muhuttampi āraugyaṃ paṭijānanti, añ‍ñatra khīṇāsavaihi|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, pabbajitassa raugā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahicchau hauti vighātavā asantuṭṭhau itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| sau mahicchau samānau vighātavā asantuṭṭhau itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina pāpikaṃ icchaṃ paṇidahati anavañ‍ñappaṭilābhāya lābhasak‍kārasilaukappaṭilābhāya| sau uṭṭhahati ghaṭati vāyamati anavañ‍ñappaṭilābhāya lābhasak‍kārasilaukappaṭilābhāya| sau saṅkhāya kulāni upasaṅkamati, saṅkhāya nisīdati, saṅkhāya dhammaṃ bhāsati, saṅkhāya uc‍cārapassāvaṃ sandhāraiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau pabbajitassa raugā|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na mahicchā bhavissāma vighātavantau asantuṭṭhā itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, na pāpikaṃ icchaṃ paṇidahissāma anavañ‍ñappaṭilābhāya lābhasak‍kārasilaukappaṭilābhāya, na uṭṭhahissāma na ghaṭaissāma na vāyamissāma anavañ‍ñappaṭilābhāya lābhasak‍kārasilaukappaṭilābhāya, khamā bhavissāma sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikā bhavissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. parihānisuttaṃ

158. tatra  khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā cattārau dhammai attani samanupassati, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| parihānamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| katamai cattārau? rāgavaipul‍lattaṃ rāgavaipul‍lataṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), dausavaipul‍lattaṃ, mauhavaipul‍lattaṃ, gambhīraisu khau panassa ṭhānāṭhānaisu pañ‍ñācakkhu  na kamati| yau hi kauci, āvusau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā imai cattārau dhammai attani samanupassati, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| parihānamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā|

‘‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā cattārau dhammai attani samanupassati, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘na parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| aparihānamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| katamai cattārau? rāgatanuttaṃ rāgatanuttanaṃ (ka॰), dausatanuttaṃ, mauhatanuttaṃ, gambhīraisu khau panassa ṭhānāṭhānaisu pañ‍ñācakkhu kamati| yau hi kauci, āvusau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā imai cattārau dhammai attani samanupassati, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘na parihāyāmi kusalaihi dhammaihi’| aparihānamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. bhikkhunīsuttaṃ

159. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī añ‍ñataraṃ purisaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, yainayyau ānandau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina ayyassa ānandassa pādai sirasā vanda – ‘itthan‍nāmā, bhantai, bhikkhunī ābādhikinī dukkhitā bāḷhagilānā| sā ayyassa ānandassa pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, ayyau ānandau yaina bhikkhunupassayau yaina sā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, ayyai’’ti khau sau purisau tassā bhikkhuniyā paṭissutvā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ  ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau purisau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘itthan‍nāmā, bhantai, bhikkhunī ābādhikinī dukkhitā bāḷhagilānā| sā āyasmatau ānandassa pādai sirasā vandati, aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau yaina bhikkhunupassayau yaina sā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| adhivāsaisi khau āyasmā ānandau tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina bhikkhunupassayau yaina sā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau sā bhikkhunī āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvā sasīsaṃ pārupitvā mañ‍cakai nipaj‍ji| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina sā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja  khau āyasmā ānandau taṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘āhārasambhūtau ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau āhāraṃ nissāya| āhārau pahātabbau| taṇhāsambhūtau ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau taṇhaṃ nissāya| taṇhā pahātabbā| mānasambhūtau ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau mānaṃ nissāya| mānau pahātabbau| maithunasambhūtau ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau| maithunai ca saitughātau vuttau bhagavatā|

‘‘‘āhārasambhūtau ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau āhāraṃ nissāya| āhārau pahātabbau’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhagini, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya| iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi| yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’ti| sau aparaina samayaina āhāraṃ nissāya āhāraṃ pajahati| ‘āhārasambhūtau ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau āhāraṃ nissāya| āhārau pahātabbau’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘taṇhāsambhūtau  ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau taṇhaṃ nissāya| taṇhā pahātabbā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca  vuttaṃ? idha, bhagini, bhikkhu suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau kira bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kudāssu nāma ahampi  āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissāmī’ti! sau aparaina samayaina taṇhaṃ nissāya taṇhaṃ pajahati| ‘taṇhāsambhūtau ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau taṇhaṃ nissāya| taṇhā pahātabbā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘mānasambhūtau  ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau mānaṃ nissāya| mānau pahātabbau’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhagini, bhikkhu suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau kira bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sau hi nāma āyasmā āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissati; kimaṅgaṃ kimaṅga (sī॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.180; cūḷava॰ 331; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1020 panāha’nti! sau aparaina samayaina mānaṃ nissāya mānaṃ pajahati| ‘mānasambhūtau ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau mānaṃ nissāya| mānau pahātabbau’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘maithunasambhūtau ayaṃ, bhagini, kāyau| maithunai ca saitughātau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti|

atha khau sā bhikkhunī mañ‍cakā vuṭṭhahitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā āyasmatau ānandassa pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā, yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yāhaṃ aivamakāsiṃ| tassā mai, bhantai, ayyau ānandau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu, āyatiṃ  saṃvarāyā’’ti| ‘‘taggha taṃ taggha tvaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), bhagini, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā, yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yā tvaṃ aivamakāsi| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, bhagini, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarausi, taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuddhi haisā, bhagini, ariyassa vinayai yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. sugatavinayasuttaṃ

160. ‘‘sugatau  vā, bhikkhavai, laukai tiṭṭhamānau sugatavinayau vā tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sugatau? idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau  purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sugatau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sugatavinayau? sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, sugatavinayau| aivaṃ sugatau vā, bhikkhavai, laukai tiṭṭhamānau sugatavinayau vā tadassa bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānanti|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū duggahitaṃ suttantaṃ pariyāpuṇanti dun‍nikkhittaihi padabyañ‍janaihi| dun‍nikkhittassa, bhikkhavai, padabyañ‍janassa  atthaupi dun‍nayau hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū dubbacā haunti dauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā akkhamā appadakkhiṇaggāhinau anusāsaniṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā, tai na sak‍kac‍caṃ suttantaṃ paraṃ vācainti| taisaṃ ac‍cayaina chin‍namūlakau suttantau hauti appaṭisaraṇau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, thairā bhikkhū bāhulikā haunti  sāthalikā, auk‍kamanai pubbaṅgamā, pavivaikai nikkhittadhurā, na vīriyaṃ ārabhanti appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| taisaṃ pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati| sāpi hauti bāhulikā sāthalikā, auk‍kamanai pubbaṅgamā, pavivaikai nikkhittadhurā, na vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau dhammau saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau dhammā saddhammassa sammausāya antaradhānāya saṃvattantī’’ti|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū suggahitaṃ suttantaṃ pariyāpuṇanti sunikkhittaihi padabyañ‍janaihi| sunikkhittassa, bhikkhavai, padabyañ‍janassa atthaupi sunayau  hauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū suvacā haunti sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatā khamā padakkhiṇaggāhinau anusāsaniṃ| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā, tai sak‍kac‍caṃ suttantaṃ paraṃ vācainti| taisaṃ ac‍cayaina nacchin‍namūlakau acchin‍namūlakau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.156 suttantau hauti sappaṭisaraṇau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, thairā bhikkhū na bāhulikā haunti na sāthalikā, auk‍kamanai nikkhittadhurā, pavivaikai pubbaṅgamā, vīriyaṃ ārabhanti appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| taisaṃ pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati| sāpi hauti na bāhulikā na sāthalikā, auk‍kamanai nikkhittadhurā, pavivaikai pubbaṅgamā, vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau dhammau saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattati| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau dhammā saddhammassa ṭhitiyā asammausāya anantaradhānāya saṃvattantī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

indriyavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

indriyāni saddhā pañ‍ñā, sati saṅkhānapañ‍camaṃ|

kappau raugau parihāni, bhikkhunī sugataina cāti||


(17) 2. paṭipadāvaggau

1. saṃkhittasuttaṃ

161. ‘‘catassau  imā, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau paṭipadā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vitthārasuttaṃ

162. ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pakatiyāpi tibbarāgajātikau hauti, abhikkhaṇaṃ rāgajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pakatiyāpi tibbadausajātikau hauti, abhikkhaṇaṃ dausajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pakatiyāpi tibbamauhajātikau hauti, abhikkhaṇaṃ mauhajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni mudūni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ muduttā dandhaṃ ānantariyaṃ pāpuṇāti āsavānaṃ khayāya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pakatiyāpi tibbarāgajātikau hauti, abhikkhaṇaṃ rāgajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pakatiyāpi tibbadausajātikau hauti, abhikkhaṇaṃ dausajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pakatiyāpi tibbamauhajātikau hauti, abhikkhaṇaṃ mauhajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| tassimāni  pañ‍cindriyāni adhimattāni  pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ adhimattattā khippaṃ ānantariyaṃ pāpuṇāti āsavānaṃ khayāya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pakatiyāpi na tibbarāgajātikau hauti, nābhikkhaṇaṃ rāgajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ  paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pakatiyāpi na tibbadausajātikau hauti, nābhikkhaṇaṃ dausajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pakatiyāpi na tibbamauhajātikau hauti, nābhikkhaṇaṃ mauhajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni mudūni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ muduttā dandhaṃ ānantariyaṃ pāpuṇāti āsavānaṃ khayāya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pakatiyāpi na tibbarāgajātikau hauti, nābhikkhaṇaṃ rāgajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pakatiyāpi na tibbadausajātikau hauti, nābhikkhaṇaṃ dausajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pakatiyāpi na tibbamauhajātikau hauti, nābhikkhaṇaṃ mauhajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni adhimattāni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ adhimattattā khippaṃ ānantariyaṃ pāpuṇāti āsavānaṃ khayāya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau paṭipadā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. asubhasuttaṃ

163. ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asubhānupassī  kāyai viharati, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī paṭik‍kūlasañ‍ñī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sabbalaukai anabhiratisañ‍ñī anabhiratasañ‍ñī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī; maraṇasañ‍ñā khau panassa ajjhattaṃ sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| sau imāni pañ‍ca saikhabalāni saikkhabalāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰) upanissāya viharati – saddhābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni mudūni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ muduttā dandhaṃ ānantariyaṃ pāpuṇāti āsavānaṃ khayāya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asubhānupassī kāyai viharati, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī, sabbalaukai anabhiratisañ‍ñī, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī; maraṇasañ‍ñā khau panassa ajjhattaṃ sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| sau imāni pañ‍ca saikhabalāni upanissāya viharati – saddhābalaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñābalaṃ| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni adhimattāni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰…  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ adhimattattā khippaṃ ānantariyaṃ pāpuṇāti āsavānaṃ khayāya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā? idha bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imāni pañ‍ca saikhabalāni upanissāya viharati – saddhābalaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñābalaṃ| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni mudūni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ muduttā dandhaṃ ānantariyaṃ pāpuṇāti āsavānaṃ khayāya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā? kathā॰ 815 ādayau idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰…  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imāni pañ‍ca saikhabalāni upanissāya viharati – saddhābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni adhimattāni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ adhimattattā khippaṃ ānantariyaṃ pāpuṇāti āsavānaṃ khayāya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau paṭipadā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamakhamasuttaṃ

164. ‘‘catassau  imā, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? akkhamā paṭipadā, khamā paṭipadā, damā paṭipadā, samā paṭipadā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, akkhamā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau aikac‍cau puggalau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) ak‍kausantaṃ pac‍cak‍kausati, rausantaṃ paṭirausati, bhaṇḍantaṃ paṭibhaṇḍati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, akkhamā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, khamā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau ak‍kausantaṃ na pac‍cak‍kausati, rausantaṃ na paṭirausati, bhaṇḍantaṃ na paṭibhaṇḍati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, khamā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, damā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī; yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya  paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ; cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina  gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī; yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati manindriyaṃ; manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, damā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, samā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti samaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti; uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ… uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti samaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samā paṭipadā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau paṭipadā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyakhamasuttaṃ

165. ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? akkhamā paṭipadā, khamā paṭipadā, damā paṭipadā, samā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, akkhamā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau akkhamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ anadhivāsakajātikau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, akkhamā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, khamā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau khamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa jighacchāya pipāsāya, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassānaṃ duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, khamā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, damā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti…pai॰… sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī; yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati manindriyaṃ; manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, damā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, samā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti samaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti, uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ… uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai nādhivāsaiti pajahati vinaudaiti samaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samā paṭipadā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau paṭipadā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ubhayasuttaṃ

166. ‘‘catassau  imā, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yāyaṃ paṭipadā dukkhā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā ubhayainaiva hīnā akkhāyati| yampāyaṃ paṭipadā dukkhā, imināpāyaṃ hīnā akkhāyati; yampāyaṃ paṭipadā dandhā, imināpāyaṃ hīnā akkhāyati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā ubhayainaiva hīnā akkhāyati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yāyaṃ paṭipadā dukkhā khippābhiñ‍ñā, ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā dukkhattā hīnā akkhāyati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yāyaṃ paṭipadā sukhā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā dandhattā hīnā akkhāyati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yāyaṃ paṭipadā sukhā khippābhiñ‍ñā, ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā ubhayainaiva paṇītā akkhāyati| yampāyaṃ paṭipadā sukhā, imināpāyaṃ paṇītā akkhāyati; yampāyaṃ paṭipadā khippā, imināpāyaṃ paṇītā akkhāyati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā ubhayainaiva paṇītā akkhāyati| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau paṭipadā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mahāmauggal‍lānasuttaṃ

167. atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahāmauggal‍lānaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘catassau imā, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā| imā khau, āvusau, catassau paṭipadā| imāsaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ catassan‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) katamaṃ tai paṭipadaṃ āgamma anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’’nti?

‘‘catassau  imā, āvusau sāriputta, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā| imā khau, āvusau, catassau paṭipadā| imāsaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ yāyaṃ paṭipadā dukkhā khippābhiñ‍ñā, imaṃ mai paṭipadaṃ āgamma anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. sāriputtasuttaṃ

168. atha  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘catassau imā, āvusau sāriputta, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā| imā khau, āvusau, catassau paṭipadā| imāsaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ  katamaṃ tai paṭipadaṃ āgamma anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’’nti?

‘‘catassau imā, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, paṭipadā| katamā catassau? dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā| imā khau, āvusau, catassau paṭipadā| imāsaṃ, āvusau, catun‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ yāyaṃ paṭipadā sukhā khippābhiñ‍ñā, imaṃ mai paṭipadaṃ āgamma anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sasaṅkhārasuttaṃ

169. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau kāyassa bhaidā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau kāyassa bhaidā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asubhānupassī kāyai viharati, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī, sabbalaukai anabhiratisañ‍ñī, sabbasaṅkhāraisu  anic‍cānupassī| maraṇasañ‍ñā  khau panassa ajjhattaṃ  sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| sau imāni pañ‍ca saikhabalāni upanissāya viharati – saddhābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni adhimattāni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ adhimattattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau kāyassa bhaidā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu asubhānupassī kāyai viharati, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī, sabbalaukai anabhiratisañ‍ñī, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassī| maraṇasañ‍ñā khau panassa ajjhattaṃ sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| sau imāni pañ‍ca saikhabalāni upanissāya viharati – saddhābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni mudūni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ muduttā kāyassa bhaidā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau kāyassa bhaidā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imāni pañ‍ca saikhabalāni upanissāya viharati – saddhābalaṃ…pai॰…  pañ‍ñābalaṃ| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni adhimattāni pātubhavanti – saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ adhimattattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau diṭṭhaiva dhammai asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau kāyassa bhaidā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imāni pañ‍ca saikhabalāni upanissāya viharati – saddhābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ| tassimāni pañ‍cindriyāni…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| sau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ muduttā kāyassa bhaidā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau kāyassa bhaidā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. yuganaddhasuttaṃ

170. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| tatra khau āyasmā ānandau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā  ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘yau  hi kauci, āvusau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā mama santikai arahattappattiṃ arahattappattaṃ (ka॰) paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.1 paṭisambhidāmaggaipi byākarauti, sabbau sau catūhi maggaihi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, āvusau, bhikkhu samathapubbaṅgamaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa samathapubbaṅgamaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvayatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau saṃyaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ samathaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ samathaṃ bhāvayatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau saṃyaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaiti| tassa samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvayatau maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau bahulīkarautau saṃyaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunau dhammuddhac‍caviggahitaṃ mānasaṃ hauti| hauti sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ ajjhattamaiva santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| tassa maggau sañ‍jāyati| sau taṃ maggaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| tassa taṃ maggaṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau  bahulīkarautau saṃyaujanāni pahīyanti, anusayā byantīhaunti|

‘‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, bhikkhu vā bhikkhunī vā mama santikai arahattappattiṃ  byākarauti, sabbau sau imaihi catūhi maggaihi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñatarainā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

paṭipadāvaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

saṃkhittaṃ vitthārāsubhaṃ, dvai khamā ubhayaina ca|

mauggal‍lānau sāriputtau, sasaṅkhāraṃ yuganaddhaina cāti||


(18) 3. sañ‍caitaniyavaggau

1. caitanāsuttaṃ

171. kathā॰ 539 ‘‘kāyai  vā, bhikkhavai, sati kāyasañ‍caitanāhaitu uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ  sukhadukkhaṃ| vācāya vā, bhikkhavai, sati vacīsañ‍caitanāhaitu uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| manai vā, bhikkhavai, sati manausañ‍caitanāhaitu uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayāva|

‘‘sāmaṃ vā taṃ, bhikkhavai, kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| parai vāssa parai vā tassa (ka॰) taṃ, bhikkhavai, kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharaunti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| sampajānau vā taṃ, bhikkhavai, kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| asampajānau vā taṃ, bhikkhavai, kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ|

‘‘sāmaṃ vā taṃ, bhikkhavai, vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ; parai vāssa taṃ, bhikkhavai, vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharaunti; yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ; sampajānau vā taṃ, bhikkhavai, vacīsaṅkhāraṃ  abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ; asampajānau vā taṃ, bhikkhavai, vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ|

‘‘sāmaṃ vā taṃ, bhikkhavai, manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ; parai vāssa taṃ, bhikkhavai, manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharaunti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ; sampajānau vā taṃ, bhikkhavai, manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ; asampajānau vā taṃ, bhikkhavai, manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ|

‘‘imaisu, bhikkhavai, dhammaisu avij‍jā anupatitā, avij‍jāyatvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā sau kāyau na hauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ, sā vācā na hauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ, sau manau na hauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ, khaittaṃ taṃ vatthuṃ taṃ (sabbattha) na hauti…pai॰… vatthuṃ taṃ  na hauti…pai॰… āyatanaṃ taṃ na hauti…pai॰… adhikaraṇaṃ taṃ na hauti yaṃpac‍cayāssa taṃ uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkha’’nti|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, attabhāvapaṭilābhā| katamai cattārau? atthi, bhikkhavai, attabhāvapaṭilābhau, yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai attasañ‍caitanā kamati, nau parasañ‍caitanā| atthi, bhikkhavai, attabhāvapaṭilābhau, yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai parasañ‍caitanā kamati, nau attasañ‍caitanā| atthi, bhikkhavai, attabhāvapaṭilābhau, yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai attasañ‍caitanā ca kamati parasañ‍caitanā ca| atthi, bhikkhavai, attabhāvapaṭilābhau, yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai naivattasañ‍caitanā kamati, nau parasañ‍caitanā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau attabhāvapaṭilābhā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā sāriputtau  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imassa khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi – ‘tatra, bhantai, yāyaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhau yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai attasañ‍caitanā kamati nau parasañ‍caitanā, attasañ‍caitanāhaitu taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā kāyā cuti hauti| tatra, bhantai, yāyaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhau  yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai parasañ‍caitanā kamati nau attasañ‍caitanā, parasañ‍caitanāhaitu taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā kāyā cuti hauti| tatra, bhantai, yāyaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhau yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai attasañ‍caitanā ca kamati parasañ‍caitanā ca, attasañ‍caitanā ca parasañ‍caitanā ca haitu taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā kāyā cuti hauti| tatra, bhantai, yāyaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhau yasmiṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhai naiva attasañ‍caitanā kamati nau parasañ‍caitanā, katamai taina daivā daṭṭhabbā’’’ti? ‘‘naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagā, sāriputta, daivā taina daṭṭhabbā’’ti|

‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā tamhā kāyā cutā āgāminau  haunti āgantārau itthattaṃ? kau pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai  sattā tamhā kāyā cutā anāgāminau haunti anāgantārau itthatta’’nti? ‘‘idha, sāriputta, aikac‍cassa puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni appahīnāni haunti, sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tadassādaiti, taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati; tattha ṭhitau tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| sau tatau cutau āgāmī hauti āgantā itthattaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, sāriputta, aikac‍cassa puggalassa aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti, sau diṭṭhaiva dhammai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tadassādaiti, taṃ nikāmaiti, taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati; tattha ṭhitau tadadhimuttau  tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| sau tatau cutau anāgāmī hauti anāgantā itthattaṃ|

‘‘ayaṃ khau, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā tamhā kāyā cutā āgāminau haunti āgantārau itthattaṃ| ayaṃ pana, sāriputta, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā tamhā kāyā cutā anāgāminau haunti anāgantārau itthatta’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vibhattisuttaṃ

172. tatra  khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘addhamāsūpasampan‍naina mai, āvusau, atthapaṭisambhidā sacchikatā audhisau byañ‍janasau| tamahaṃ anaikapariyāyaina ācikkhāmi daisaimi pañ‍ñāpaimi paṭṭhapaimi vivarāmi vibhajāmi uttānīkaraumi| yassa khau panassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, sau  maṃ pañhaina| ahaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina sammukhībhūtau nau satthā yau nau dhammānaṃ sukusalau|

‘‘addhamāsūpasampan‍naina  mai, āvusau, dhammapaṭisambhidā sacchikatā audhisau byañ‍janasau| tamahaṃ anaikapariyāyaina ācikkhāmi daisaimi pañ‍ñāpaimi paṭṭhapaimi vivarāmi vibhajāmi uttānīkaraumi| yassa khau panassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, sau maṃ pañhaina| ahaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina sammukhībhūtau nau satthā yau nau dhammānaṃ sukusalau|

‘‘addhamāsūpasampan‍naina mai, āvusau, niruttipaṭisambhidā sacchikatā audhisau byañ‍janasau| tamahaṃ anaikapariyāyaina ācikkhāmi daisaimi pañ‍ñāpaimi paṭṭhapaimi vivarāmi vibhajāmi uttānīkaraumi| yassa khau panassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, sau maṃ pañhaina| ahaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina sammukhībhūtau nau satthā yau nau dhammānaṃ sukusalau|

‘‘addhamāsūpasampan‍naina mai, āvusau, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā sacchikatā audhisau byañ‍janasau| tamahaṃ anaikapariyāyaina ācikkhāmi daisaimi pañ‍ñāpaimi paṭṭhapaimi vivarāmi vibhajāmi uttānīkaraumi| yassa khau panassa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā, sau maṃ pañhaina| ahaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina sammukhībhūtau nau satthā yau nau dhammānaṃ sukusalau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. mahākauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

173. atha  khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau  khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’’ti?

‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’’ti?

‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthi ca natthi ca añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’’ti?

‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā naivatthi nau natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’’ti?

‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthi ca natthi ca añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā  naivatthi nau natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| yathā kathaṃ pana, āvusau, imassa bhāsitassa atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

‘‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti vadaṃ appapañ‍caṃ papañ‍caiti| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti vadaṃ appapañ‍caṃ papañ‍caiti| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthi ca natthi ca añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti vadaṃ appapañ‍caṃ papañ‍caiti| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā naivatthi nau natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti vadaṃ appapañ‍caṃ papañ‍caiti| yāvatā, āvusau, chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ gati tāvatā papañ‍cassa gati; yāvatā papañ‍cassa gati  tāvatā chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ gati| chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā papañ‍caniraudhau papañ‍cavūpasamau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. ānandasuttaṃ

174. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaināyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahākauṭṭhikaina saddhiṃ  sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’’ti?

‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’’ti?

‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthi ca natthi ca añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’’ti?

‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā naivatthi nau natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’’ti?

‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthi ca natthi ca añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā naivatthi nau natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| yathā kathaṃ panāvusau, imassa bhāsitassa atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

‘‘‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti vadaṃ appapañ‍caṃ papañ‍caiti| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti vadaṃ appapañ‍caṃ papañ‍caiti| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā atthi ca natthi ca añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti vadaṃ appapañ‍caṃ papañ‍caiti| ‘chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā naivatthi nau natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍cī’ti, iti vadaṃ appapañ‍caṃ papañ‍caiti| yāvatā, āvusau, chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ gati tāvatā papañ‍cassa gati| yāvatā papañ‍cassa gati tāvatā chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ  gati| chan‍naṃ, āvusau, phassāyatanānaṃ asaisavirāganiraudhā papañ‍caniraudhau papañ‍cavūpasamau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. upavāṇasuttaṃ

175. atha  khau āyasmā upavāṇau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upavāṇau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, vij‍jāyantakarau hautī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, caraṇainantakarau hautī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau  sāriputta, vij‍jācaraṇainantakarau hautī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, añ‍ñatra vij‍jācaraṇainantakarau hautī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, vij‍jāyantakarau hautī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, caraṇainantakarau hautī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, vij‍jācaraṇainantakarau hautī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, añ‍ñatra vij‍jācaraṇainantakarau hautī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau – ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| yathā kathaṃ panāvusau, antakarau hautī’’ti?

‘‘vij‍jāya  cai, āvusau, antakarau abhavissa, saupādānauva samānau antakarau abhavissa| caraṇaina cai, āvusau, antakarau abhavissa, saupādānauva samānau antakarau abhavissa| vij‍jācaraṇaina cai, āvusau, antakarau abhavissa, saupādānauva samānau antakarau abhavissa| añ‍ñatra vij‍jācaraṇaina cai, āvusau, antakarau abhavissa, puthuj‍janau antakarau abhavissa| puthuj‍janau hi, āvusau, añ‍ñatra vij‍jācaraṇaina| caraṇavipan‍nau khau, āvusau, yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati| caraṇasampan‍nau yathābhūtaṃ  jānāti passati| yathābhūtaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ antakarau hautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. āyācanasuttaṃ

176. ‘‘saddhau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ sammā āyācamānau āyācaiyya – ‘tādisau haumi yādisā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’ti a॰ ni॰ 2.131 idaṃ suttaṃ āgataṃ| aisā, bhikkhavai, tulā aitaṃ pamāṇaṃ mama sāvakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, yadidaṃ sāriputtamauggal‍lānā|

‘‘saddhā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī aivaṃ sammā āyācamānā āyācaiyya – ‘tādisā haumi yādisā khaimā ca bhikkhunī uppalavaṇṇā cā’ti| aisā, bhikkhavai, tulā aitaṃ pamāṇaṃ mama sāvikānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ, yadidaṃ khaimā ca bhikkhunī uppalavaṇṇā ca|

‘‘saddhau, bhikkhavai, upāsakau aivaṃ sammā āyācamānau āyācaiyya – ‘tādisau haumi yādisau cittau ca gahapati hatthakau ca āḷavakau’ti| aisā, bhikkhavai, tulā aitaṃ pamāṇaṃ mama sāvakānaṃ upāsakānaṃ, yadidaṃ cittau ca gahapati hatthakau ca āḷavakau|

‘‘saddhā, bhikkhavai, upāsikā aivaṃ sammā āyācamānā āyācaiyya – ‘tādisā haumi yādisā khuj‍juttarā ca upāsikā vaiḷukaṇḍakiyā ca nandamātā’ti| aisā, bhikkhavai, tulā aitaṃ pamāṇaṃ mama sāvikānaṃ upāsikānaṃ, yadidaṃ khuj‍juttarā ca upāsikā vaiḷukaṇḍakiyā ca nandamātā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. rāhulasuttaṃ

177. atha  khau āyasmā rāhulau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rāhulaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yā ca, rāhula, ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu yā ca bāhirā pathavīdhātu, pathavīdhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā pathavīdhātuyā nibbindati, pathavīdhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

‘‘yā ca, rāhula, ajjhattikā āpaudhātu yā ca bāhirā āpaudhātu, āpaudhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā āpaudhātuyā nibbindati, āpaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

‘‘yā  ca, rāhula, ajjhattikā taijaudhātu yā ca bāhirā taijaudhātu, taijaudhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā taijaudhātuyā nibbindati, taijaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

‘‘yā ca, rāhula, ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu yā ca bāhirā vāyaudhātu, vāyaudhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā vāyaudhātuyā nibbindati, vāyaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

‘‘yatau khau, rāhula, bhikkhu imāsu catūsu dhātūsu naivattānaṃ na attaniyaṃ samanupassati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rāhula, bhikkhu acchaicchi taṇhaṃ, vivattayi saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. jambālīsuttaṃ

178. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ santaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ manasi karauti| tassa sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ manasi karautau sak‍kāyaniraudhai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati nādhimuc‍cati| tassa khau aivaṃ tassa kau aitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), aivaṃ khau tassa (?) ‘‘aivaṃ hi tassā bhikkhavai jambāliyā’’ti pāṭhau viya, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau na sak‍kāyaniraudhau  pāṭikaṅkhau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau laipagataina lasagataina (sī॰ pī॰) hatthaina sākhaṃ gaṇhaiyya, tassa sau hatthau saj‍jaiyyapi gaṇhaiyyapi gayhaiyyapi (?) bajjhaiyyapi khaj‍jaiyyapi (sī॰); aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ santaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ manasi karauti| tassa sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ manasi karautau sak‍kāyaniraudhai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati nādhimuc‍cati| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau na sak‍kāyaniraudhau pāṭikaṅkhau|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ santaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ manasi karauti| tassa sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ manasi karautau sak‍kāyaniraudhai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sak‍kāyaniraudhau pāṭikaṅkhau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau suddhaina hatthaina sākhaṃ gaṇhaiyya, tassa sau hatthau naiva saj‍jaiyya na gaṇhaiyya na bajjhaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ santaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ manasi karauti| tassa sak‍kāyaniraudhaṃ manasi karautau sak‍kāyaniraudhai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sak‍kāyaniraudhau pāṭikaṅkhau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ santaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau avij‍jāppabhaidaṃ manasi karauti| tassa avij‍jāppabhaidaṃ manasi karautau avij‍jāppabhaidai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati nādhimuc‍cati| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau na avij‍jāppabhaidau pāṭikaṅkhau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, jambālī  anaikavassagaṇikā| tassā purisau yāni caiva āyamukhāni tāni pidahaiyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni vivaraiyya, daivau ca na sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| aivañhi tassā, bhikkhavai, jambāliyā na āḷippabhaidau  pāṭikaṅkhau| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ santaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau avij‍jāppabhaidaṃ manasi karauti| tassa avij‍jāppabhaidaṃ manasi karautau avij‍jāppabhaidai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati nādhimuc‍cati| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau na avij‍jāppabhaidau pāṭikaṅkhau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ santaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau avij‍jāppabhaidaṃ manasi karauti| tassa avij‍jāppabhaidaṃ manasi karautau avij‍jāppabhaidai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau avij‍jāppabhaidau pāṭikaṅkhau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, jambālī anaikavassagaṇikā| tassā purisau yāni caiva āyamukhāni tāni vivaraiyya, yāni ca apāyamukhāni tāni pidahaiyya, daivau ca sammā dhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| aivañhi tassā, bhikkhavai, jambāliyā āḷippabhaidau pāṭikaṅkhau| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ  santaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau avij‍jāppabhaidaṃ manasi karauti| tassa avij‍jāppabhaidaṃ  manasi karautau avij‍jāppabhaidai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati| tassa khau aivaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau avij‍jāppabhaidau pāṭikaṅkhau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nibbānasuttaṃ

179. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai na parinibbāyantī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau ānanda, sattā imā hānabhāgiyā sañ‍ñāti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, imā ṭhitibhāgiyā sañ‍ñāti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, imā visaisabhāgiyā sañ‍ñāti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti,  imā nibbaidhabhāgiyā sañ‍ñāti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti| ayaṃ khau, āvusau ānanda, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai na parinibbāyantī’’ti|

‘‘kau panāvusau sāriputta, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau ānanda, sattā imā hānabhāgiyā sañ‍ñāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, imā ṭhitibhāgiyā sañ‍ñāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, imā visaisabhāgiyā sañ‍ñāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, imā nibbaidhabhāgiyā sañ‍ñāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti| ayaṃ khau, āvusau ānanda, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. mahāpadaisasuttaṃ

180. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā bhauganagarai viharati ānandacaitiyai ānandai caitiyai (dī॰ ni॰ 2.186) mahāva॰ 303 pana añ‍ñathā āgataṃ| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū  bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, mahāpadaisai daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamai, bhikkhavai, cattārau mahāpadaisā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai autāraitabbāni ausāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai autāriyamānāni ausāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni na caiva suttai autaranti ausaranti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.188) na vinayai sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ na caiva tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; imassa ca bhikkhunau duggahita’nti| iti haitaṃ iti hidaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, chaḍḍaiyyātha|
