aittakau pāṭhau dīghanikāyai na dissati, paiyyālamukhaina dassitau bhavaiyya ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘sammukhā maitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau  bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai autāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni aittakau pāṭhau dīghanikāyai na dissati, paiyyālamukhaina dassitau bhavaiyya| tāni cai suttai autāriyamānāni vinayai  sandassiyamānāni suttai caiva autaranti vinayai ca sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; imassa ca bhikkhunau suggahita’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ mahāpadaisaṃ dhāraiyyātha|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asukasmiṃ nāma āvāsai saṅghau viharati sathairau sapāmaukkhau| tassa mai saṅghassa sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā  appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai autāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai autāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni na caiva suttai autaranti na vinayai sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ na caiva tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; tassa ca saṅghassa duggahita’nti| iti haitaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaḍḍaiyyātha|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asukasmiṃ nāma āvāsai saṅghau viharati sathairau sapāmaukkhau| tassa mai saṅghassa sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai autāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai autāriyamānāni, vinayai sandassiyamānāni suttai caiva autaranti vinayai ca  sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; tassa ca saṅghassa suggahita’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ mahāpadaisaṃ dhāraiyyātha|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asukasmiṃ nāma āvāsai sambahulā thairā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā| taisaṃ mai thairānaṃ sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai autāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai autāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni na caiva suttai autaranti na vinayai sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ na caiva tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; taisañ‍ca thairānaṃ duggahita’nti| iti haitaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaḍḍaiyyātha|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asukasmiṃ nāma āvāsai sambahulā thairā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā| taisaṃ mai thairānaṃ sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai autāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai autāriyamānāni vinayai  sandassiyamānāni suttai caiva autaranti vinayai ca sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; taisañ‍ca thairānaṃ suggahita’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ mahāpadaisaṃ dhāraiyyātha|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asukasmiṃ nāma āvāsai aikau thairau bhikkhu viharati bahussutau  āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau| tassa mai thairassa sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai autāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai autāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni na caiva suttai autaranti na vinayai sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ na caiva tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; tassa ca thairassa duggahita’nti| iti haitaṃ, bhikkhavai, chaḍḍaiyyātha|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘asukasmiṃ nāma āvāsai aikau thairau bhikkhu  viharati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau| tassa mai thairassa sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsana’nti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinanditabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā tāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ uggahaitvā suttai autāraitabbāni, vinayai sandassaitabbāni| tāni cai suttai autāriyamānāni vinayai sandassiyamānāni suttai caiva autaranti vinayai  ca sandissanti, niṭṭhamaittha gantabbaṃ – ‘addhā, idaṃ tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; tassa ca thairassa suggahita’nti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthaṃ mahāpadaisaṃ dhāraiyyātha| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau mahāpadaisā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sañ‍caitaniyavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

caitanā  vibhatti kauṭṭhikau, ānandau upavāṇapañ‍camaṃ|

āyācana-rāhula-jambālī, nibbānaṃ mahāpadaisaināti||


(19) 4. brāhmaṇavaggau

1. yaudhājīvasuttaṃ

181. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau yaudhājīvau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau ṭhānakusalau ca hauti, dūraipātī ca, akkhaṇavaidhī ca, mahatau ca kāyassa padālaitā| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau yaudhājīvau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ṭhānakusalau ca hauti, dūraipātī ca, akkhaṇavaidhī ca, mahatau ca kāyassa padālaitā|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ṭhānakusalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ṭhānakusalau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dūraipātī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dūraipātī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhaṇavaidhī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhaṇavaidhī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahatau kāyassa padālaitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahantaṃ avij‍jākkhandhaṃ padālaitā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahatau kāyassa padālaitā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pāṭibhaugasuttaṃ

182. kathā॰ 624 ‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ natthi kauci pāṭibhaugau – samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘katamaisaṃ catun‍naṃ? ‘jarādhammaṃ mā jīrī’ti natthi kauci pāṭibhaugau – samaṇau vā  brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ; ‘byādhidhammaṃ mā byādhiyī’ti natthi kauci pāṭibhaugau – samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ; ‘maraṇadhammaṃ mā mīyī’ti natthi kauci pāṭibhaugau – samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ; ‘yāni khau pana tāni pubbai attanā katāni pāpakāni kammāni saṃkilaisikāni paunaubhavikāni sadarāni dukkhavipākāni āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇikāni, taisaṃ vipākau mā nibbattī’ti natthi kauci pāṭibhaugau – samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ natthi kauci pāṭibhaugau – samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā daivau vā mārau vā brahmā vā kauci vā laukasmi’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sutasuttaṃ

183. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘ahañhi, bhau gautama, aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi aivaṃdiṭṭhī (sabbattha) – ‘yau kauci diṭṭhaṃ bhāsati – aivaṃ mai diṭṭhanti, natthi tatau dausau; yau kauci sutaṃ bhāsati – aivaṃ mai sutanti, natthi tatau dausau; yau kauci mutaṃ bhāsati – aivaṃ mai mutanti, natthi tatau  dausau; yau kauci viñ‍ñātaṃ bhāsati – aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñātanti, natthi tatau dausau’’’ti|

‘‘nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ diṭṭhaṃ bhāsitabbanti vadāmi; na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ diṭṭhaṃ na bhāsitabbanti vadāmi; nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ sutaṃ bhāsitabbanti vadāmi; na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ sutaṃ na bhāsitabbanti  vadāmi; nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ mutaṃ bhāsitabbanti vadāmi; na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ mutaṃ na bhāsitabbanti vadāmi; nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ bhāsitabbanti vadāmi; na panāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ na bhāsitabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘yañhi, brāhmaṇa, diṭṭhaṃ bhāsatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ na bhāsitabbanti vadāmi| yañ‍ca khvassa, brāhmaṇa, diṭṭhaṃ abhāsatau kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ bhāsitabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘yañhi, brāhmaṇa, sutaṃ bhāsatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpaṃ sutaṃ na bhāsitabbanti vadāmi| yañ‍ca khvassa, brāhmaṇa, sutaṃ abhāsatau kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpaṃ sutaṃ bhāsitabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘yañhi, brāhmaṇa, mutaṃ bhāsatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpaṃ mutaṃ na bhāsitabbanti vadāmi| yañ‍ca khvassa, brāhmaṇa, mutaṃ abhāsatau kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpaṃ mutaṃ bhāsitabbanti vadāmi|

‘‘yañhi, brāhmaṇa, viñ‍ñātaṃ bhāsatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ na bhāsitabbanti vadāmi| yañ‍ca khvassa, brāhmaṇa, viñ‍ñātaṃ abhāsatau kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivarūpaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ bhāsitabbanti vadāmī’’ti|

atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmīti| tatiyaṃ|

4. abhayasuttaṃ

184. atha khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘ahañhi, bhau gautama, aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘natthi yau maraṇadhammau samānau na bhāyati, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassā’’’ti| ‘‘atthi, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau bhāyati, santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa; atthi pana, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau na bhāyati, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa|

‘‘katamau  ca, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau bhāyati, santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau  avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau gāḷhau raugātaṅkau phusati| tassa añ‍ñataraina gāḷhaina raugātaṅkaina phuṭṭhassa  aivaṃ hauti – ‘piyā vata maṃ kāmā jahissanti, piyai cāhaṃ kāmai jahissāmī’ti| sau saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau bhāyati, santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau kāyai avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau gāḷhau raugātaṅkau phusati| tassa añ‍ñataraina gāḷhaina raugātaṅkaina phuṭṭhassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘piyau vata  maṃ kāyau jahissati, piyañ‍cāhaṃ kāyaṃ jahissāmī’ti| sau saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau bhāyati, santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau akatakalyāṇau hauti akatakusalau akatabhīruttāṇau katapāpau kataluddau katakibbisau| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau gāḷhau raugātaṅkau phusati| tassa añ‍ñataraina gāḷhaina raugātaṅkaina phuṭṭhassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘akataṃ vata mai kalyāṇaṃ, akataṃ kusalaṃ, akataṃ bhīruttāṇaṃ; kataṃ pāpaṃ, kataṃ luddaṃ, kataṃ kibbisaṃ| yāvatā, bhau, akatakalyāṇānaṃ akatakusalānaṃ akatabhīruttāṇānaṃ katapāpānaṃ kataluddānaṃ katakibbisānaṃ gati taṃ gatiṃ paic‍ca gacchāmī’ti| sau saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau bhāyati, santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau kaṅkhī hauti vicikicchī aniṭṭhaṅgatau saddhammai| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau gāḷhau raugātaṅkau  phusati| tassa añ‍ñataraina gāḷhaina raugātaṅkaina phuṭṭhassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kaṅkhī vatamhi vicikicchī aniṭṭhaṅgatau saddhammai’ti| sau saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau  bhāyati, santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa| imai khau, brāhmaṇa, cattārau maraṇadhammā samānā bhāyanti, santāsaṃ āpaj‍janti maraṇassa|

‘‘katamau  ca, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau na bhāyati, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau kāmaisu vītarāgau hauti vigatacchandau vigatapaimau vigatapipāsau vigatapariḷāhau vigatataṇhau| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau gāḷhau raugātaṅkau phusati| tassa añ‍ñataraina gāḷhaina raugātaṅkaina phuṭṭhassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘piyā vata maṃ kāmā jahissanti, piyai cāhaṃ kāmai jahissāmī’ti| sau na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau na bhāyati, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau kāyai vītarāgau hauti vigatacchandau vigatapaimau vigatapipāsau vigatapariḷāhau vigatataṇhau| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau gāḷhau raugātaṅkau phusati| tassa añ‍ñataraina gāḷhaina raugātaṅkaina phuṭṭhassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘piyau vata maṃ kāyau jahissati, piyañ‍cāhaṃ kāyaṃ jahissāmī’ti| sau na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau na bhāyati, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau akatapāpau hauti akataluddau akatakibbisau katakalyāṇau katakusalau katabhīruttāṇau| tamainaṃ añ‍ñatarau gāḷhau raugātaṅkau phusati| tassa añ‍ñataraina gāḷhaina raugātaṅkaina phuṭṭhassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘akataṃ vata mai pāpaṃ, akataṃ luddaṃ, akataṃ kibbisaṃ; kataṃ kalyāṇaṃ, kataṃ kusalaṃ, kataṃ bhīruttāṇaṃ| yāvatā, bhau, akatapāpānaṃ akataluddānaṃ akatakibbisānaṃ katakalyāṇānaṃ katakusalānaṃ katabhīruttāṇānaṃ gati taṃ gatiṃ paic‍ca gacchāmī’ti| sau na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau na bhāyati, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau akaṅkhī hauti avicikicchī niṭṭhaṅgatau saddhammai| tamainaṃ  añ‍ñatarau gāḷhau raugātaṅkau phusati| tassa añ‍ñataraina gāḷhaina raugātaṅkaina phuṭṭhassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘akaṅkhī vatamhi avicikicchī niṭṭhaṅgatau saddhammai’ti| sau na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ayampi  khau, brāhmaṇa, maraṇadhammau samānau na bhāyati, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍jati maraṇassa| imai khau, brāhmaṇa, cattārau maraṇadhammā samānā na bhāyanti, na santāsaṃ āpaj‍janti maraṇassā’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. brāhmaṇasac‍casuttaṃ

185. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā paribbājakā sippinikātīrai  paribbājakārāmai paṭivasanti, saiyyathidaṃ an‍nabhārau varadharau sakuludāyī ca paribbājakau añ‍ñai ca abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā paribbājakā| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina sippinikātīrai paribbājakārāmau tainupasaṅkami|

taina khau pana samayaina taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarā kathā udapādi – ‘‘itipi brāhmaṇasac‍cāni, itipi brāhmaṇasac‍cānī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā yaina tai paribbājakā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā tai paribbājakai aitadavauca –

‘‘kāya nuttha, paribbājakā, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhau gautama, amhākaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘itipi brāhmaṇasac‍cāni, itipi brāhmaṇasac‍cānī’’’ti|

‘‘cattārimāni, paribbājakā, brāhmaṇasac‍cāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| katamāni cattāri? idha, paribbājakā, brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘sabbai pāṇā avajjhā’ti| iti vadaṃ brāhmaṇau sac‍caṃ āha, nau musā| sau taina na samaṇauti mañ‍ñati, na brāhmaṇauti mañ‍ñati, na saiyyauhamasmīti mañ‍ñati, na sadisauhamasmīti mañ‍ñati, na hīnauhamasmīti mañ‍ñati| api ca yadaiva tattha sac‍caṃ tadabhiñ‍ñāya pāṇānaṃyaiva anuddayāya tadabhiñ‍ñāya anudayāya (ka॰) anukampāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, paribbājakā, brāhmaṇau aivamāha  – ‘sabbai  kāmā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’ti| iti vadaṃ brāhmaṇau sac‍camāha, nau musā| sau taina na samaṇauti mañ‍ñati, na brāhmaṇauti mañ‍ñati, na saiyyauhamasmīti mañ‍ñati, na sadisauhamasmīti mañ‍ñati, na hīnauhamasmīti mañ‍ñati| api ca yadaiva tattha sac‍caṃ tadabhiñ‍ñāya kāmānaṃyaiva nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, paribbājakā, brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘sabbai bhavā anic‍cā…pai॰… bhavānaṃyaiva nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, paribbājakā, brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘nāhaṃ k‍vacani k‍vacana (sī॰ syā॰) kassaci kiñ‍canatasmiṃ na ca mama k‍vacani katthaci kiñ‍canatatthī’ti| iti vadaṃ brāhmaṇau sac‍caṃ āha, nau musā| sau taina na samaṇauti mañ‍ñati, na brāhmaṇauti mañ‍ñati, na saiyyauhamasmīti mañ‍ñati, na sadisauhamasmīti mañ‍ñati, na hīnauhamasmīti mañ‍ñati| api ca yadaiva tattha sac‍caṃ tadabhiñ‍ñāya ākiñ‍cañ‍ñaṃyaiva paṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti| imāni khau, paribbājakā, cattāri brāhmaṇasac‍cāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ummaggasuttaṃ

186. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kaina  nu khau, bhantai, laukau nīyati, kaina laukau parikassati, kassa ca uppan‍nassa vasaṃ gacchatī’’ti?

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! bhaddakau khau tai, bhikkhu, ummaggau ummaṅgau (syā॰ ka॰), bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī kalyāṇā (ka॰) paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pucchasi – ‘kaina nu khau, bhantai, laukau nīyati, kaina laukau parikassati, kassa ca uppan‍nassa vasaṃ gacchatī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘cittaina khau, bhikkhu, laukau nīyati, cittaina parikassati, cittassa uppan‍nassa vasaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā bhagavantaṃ uttari pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘‘bahussutau dhammadharau, bahussutau dhammadharau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bahussutau dhammadharau hautī’’ti?

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! bhaddakau khau tai, bhikkhu ummaggau, bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pucchasi – ‘bahussutau dhammadharau, bahussutau dhammadharauti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bahussutau  dhammadharau hautī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘bahū khau, bhikkhu, mayā dhammā daisitā bahu khau bhikkhu mayā dhammau daisitau (ka॰) – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthā, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| catuppadāya caipi, bhikkhu, gāthāya atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya  dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti bahussutau dhammadharauti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti|

‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā bhagavantaṃ uttari pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘‘sutavā nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau, sutavā nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, sutavā nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau hautī’’ti?

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! bhaddakau khau tai, bhikkhu, ummaggau, bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pucchasi – ‘sutavā nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau, sutavā nibbaidhikapañ‍ñauti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, sutavā nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau hautī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, bhikkhunau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti sutaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñāya cassa atthaṃ ativijjha passati; ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti sutaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñāya cassa atthaṃ  ativijjha passati; ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti sutaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñāya cassa atthaṃ ativijjha passati; ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti sutaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñāya cassa atthaṃ ativijjha passati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sutavā nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau hautī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā bhagavantaṃ uttari pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘‘paṇḍitau mahāpañ‍ñau, paṇḍitau mahāpañ‍ñau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, paṇḍitau mahāpañ‍ñau hautī’’ti?

‘‘sādhu  sādhu bhikkhu! bhaddakau khau tai, bhikkhu, ummaggau, bhaddakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ, kalyāṇī paripucchā| aivañhi tvaṃ bhikkhu pucchasi – ‘paṇḍitau mahāpañ‍ñau, paṇḍitau mahāpañ‍ñauti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, paṇḍitau mahāpañ‍ñau hautī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, paṇḍitau mahāpañ‍ñau naivattabyābādhāya caitaiti na parabyābādhāya caitaiti na  ubhayabyābādhāya caitaiti attahitaparahitaubhayahitasabbalaukahitamaiva cintayamānau cintaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, paṇḍitau mahāpañ‍ñau hautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vassakārasuttaṃ

187. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘jānaiyya nu khau, bhau gautama, asappurisau asappurisaṃ – ‘asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti? ‘‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, anavakāsau yaṃ asappurisau asappurisaṃ jānaiyya – ‘asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti| ‘‘jānaiyya pana, bhau gautama, asappurisau sappurisaṃ – ‘sappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti? ‘‘aitampi khau, brāhmaṇa, aṭṭhānaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ asappurisau sappurisaṃ jānaiyya – ‘sappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti| ‘‘jānaiyya nu khau, bhau gautama, sappurisau sappurisaṃ – ‘sappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti? ‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, vij‍jati  yaṃ sappurisau sappurisaṃ jānaiyya – ‘sappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti| ‘‘jānaiyya pana, bhau gautama, sappurisau asappurisaṃ – ‘asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti? ‘‘aitampi  khau, brāhmaṇa, ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ sappurisau asappurisaṃ jānaiyya – ‘asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yāva subhāsitaṃ  cidaṃ, bhautā gautamaina – ‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, anavakāsau yaṃ asappurisau asappurisaṃ jānaiyya – asappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti| aitampi khau, brāhmaṇa, aṭṭhānaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ asappurisau sappurisaṃ jānaiyya – sappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti| ṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, vij‍jati yaṃ sappurisau sappurisaṃ jānaiyya – sappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti| aitampi khau, brāhmaṇa, ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ sappurisau asappurisaṃ jānaiyya – asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’’’nti|

‘‘aikamidaṃ, bhau gautama, samayaṃ taudaiyyassa brāhmaṇassa parisati parūpārambhaṃ vattainti – ‘bālau ayaṃ rājā aiḷaiyyau samaṇai rāmaputtai abhippasan‍nau, samaṇai ca pana rāmaputtai aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karauti, yadidaṃ  abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikamma’nti| imaipi rañ‍ñau aiḷaiyyassa parihārakā bālā – yamakau mauggal‍lau puggalau (ka॰) uggau nāvindakī gandhabbau aggivaissau, yai samaṇai rāmaputtai abhippasan‍nā, samaṇai ca pana rāmaputtai aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaunti, yadidaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammanti| tyāssudaṃ taudaiyyau brāhmaṇau iminā nayaina naiti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñanti, bhauntau, paṇḍitau rājā aiḷaiyyau karaṇīyādhikaraṇīyaisu vacanīyādhivacanīyaisu  alamatthadasataraihi alamatthadasatarau’ti? ‘aivaṃ, bhau, paṇḍitau rājā aiḷaiyyau karaṇīyādhikaraṇīyaisu vacanīyādhivacanīyaisu alamatthadasataraihi alamatthadasatarau’’’ti|

‘‘yasmā ca khau, bhau, samaṇau rāmaputtau rañ‍ñā aiḷaiyyaina paṇḍitaina paṇḍitatarau karaṇīyādhikaraṇīyaisu vacanīyādhivacanīyaisu alamatthadasataraina alamatthadasatarau, tasmā rājā aiḷaiyyau samaṇai rāmaputtai abhippasan‍nau, samaṇai ca pana rāmaputtai aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karauti, yadidaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñanti, bhauntau, paṇḍitā rañ‍ñau aiḷaiyyassa parihārakā – yamakau mauggal‍lau  uggau  nāvindakī gandhabbau aggivaissau, karaṇīyādhikaraṇīyaisu vacanīyādhivacanīyaisu alamatthadasataraihi alamatthadasatarāti? ‘aivaṃ, bhau, paṇḍitā rañ‍ñau aiḷaiyyassa parihārakā – yamakau mauggal‍lau uggau nāvindakī gandhabbau aggivaissau, karaṇīyādhikaraṇīyaisu vacanīyādhivacanīyaisu alamatthadasataraihi alamatthadasatarā’’’ti|

‘‘yasmā ca khau, bhau, samaṇau rāmaputtau rañ‍ñau aiḷaiyyassa parihārakaihi paṇḍitaihi paṇḍitatarau karaṇīyādhikaraṇīyaisu vacanīyādhivacanīyaisu alamatthadasataraihi alamatthadasatarau, tasmā rañ‍ñau aiḷaiyyassa parihārakā samaṇai rāmaputtai abhippasan‍nā; samaṇai ca pana rāmaputtai aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karaunti, yadidaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikamma’’nti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau, gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yāva subhāsitaṃ cidaṃ bhautā gautamaina  – ‘aṭṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, anavakāsau yaṃ asappurisau  asappurisaṃ jānaiyya – asappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti| aitampi khau, brāhmaṇa, aṭṭhānaṃ anavakāsau yaṃ asappurisau sappurisaṃ jānaiyya – sappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti| ṭhānaṃ khau aitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, vij‍jati yaṃ sappurisau sappurisaṃ jānaiyya – sappurisau ayaṃ bhavanti| aitampi khau, brāhmaṇa, ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ sappurisau asappurisaṃ jānaiyya – asappurisau ayaṃ bhava’nti| handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bhau gautama, gacchāma| bahukic‍cā mayaṃ bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau vassakārau brāhmaṇau magadhamahāmattau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmīti| sattamaṃ|

8. upakasuttaṃ

188. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau upakau maṇḍikāputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau upakau maṇḍikāputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘ahañhi, bhantai, aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘yau kauci parūpārambhaṃ vattaiti, parūpārambhaṃ vattaintau  sabbau sau sabbasau (sī॰ pī॰) na upapādaiti| anupapādaintau gārayhau hauti upavaj‍jau’’’ti| ‘‘parūpārambhaṃ cai, upaka, vattaiti parūpārambhaṃ vattaintau na upapādaiti, anupapādaintau  gārayhau hauti upavaj‍jau| tvaṃ khau, upaka, parūpārambhaṃ vattaisi, parūpārambhaṃ vattaintau na upapādaisi, anupapādaintau gārayhau hausi upavaj‍jau’’ti| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, ummuj‍jamānakaṃyaiva mahatā pāsaina bandhaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, ummuj‍jamānakauyaiva bhagavatā mahatā vādapāsaina mahatā pāsaina (ka॰) baddhau’’ti|

‘‘idaṃ akusalanti khau, upaka, mayā pañ‍ñattaṃ| tattha aparimāṇā padā aparimāṇā byañ‍janā aparimāṇā tathāgatassa dhammadaisanā – itipidaṃ akusalanti| taṃ khau panidaṃ akusalaṃ pahātabbanti khau, upaka, mayā pañ‍ñattaṃ| tattha aparimāṇā padā aparimāṇā byañ‍janā aparimāṇā tathāgatassa dhammadaisanā – itipidaṃ akusalaṃ pahātabbanti|

‘‘idaṃ  kusalanti khau, upaka, mayā pañ‍ñattaṃ| tattha aparimāṇā padā aparimāṇā byañ‍janā aparimāṇā tathāgatassa dhammadaisanā – itipidaṃ kusalanti| taṃ khau panidaṃ kusalaṃ bhāvaitabbanti khau, upaka, mayā pañ‍ñattaṃ| tattha aparimāṇā padā aparimāṇā byañ‍janā aparimāṇā tathāgatassa dhammadaisanā – itipidaṃ kusalaṃ bhāvaitabba’’nti|

atha khau upakau maṇḍikāputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā yāvatakau ahausi bhagavatā saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ rañ‍ñau māgadhassa ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassa āraucaisi|

aivaṃ vuttai rājā māgadhau ajātasattu vaidaihiputtau kupitau anattamanau upakaṃ maṇḍikāputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yāva dhaṃsī vatāyaṃ lauṇakāradārakau yāva mukharau yāva pagabbau  yatra hi nāma taṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ āsādaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissati; apaihi tvaṃ, upaka, vinassa, mā taṃ addasa’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sacchikaraṇīyasuttaṃ

189. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, sacchikaraṇīyā dhammā| katamai cattārau? atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā kāyaina sacchikaraṇīyā; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā satiyā sacchikaraṇīyā; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā cakkhunā sacchikaraṇīyā; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā pañ‍ñāya sacchikaraṇīyā| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā kāyaina sacchikaraṇīyā? aṭṭha vimaukkhā, bhikkhavai, kāyaina sacchikaraṇīyā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā satiyā sacchikaraṇīyā? pubbainivāsau, bhikkhavai, satiyā sacchikaraṇīyau|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā cakkhunā sacchikaraṇīyā? sattānaṃ cutūpapātau, bhikkhavai, cakkhunā sacchikaraṇīyau|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā pañ‍ñāya sacchikaraṇīyā? āsavānaṃ khayau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāya sacchikaraṇīyau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau sacchikaraṇīyā dhammā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. upausathasuttaṃ

190. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā tuṇhībhūtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘apalāpāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā nippalāpāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā suddhā sārai patiṭṭhitā| tathārūpau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhusaṅghau, tathārūpāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā| yathārūpā parisā dul‍labhā dassanāyapi laukasmiṃ, tathārūpau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhusaṅghau, tathārūpāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā| yathārūpā parisā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa, tathārūpau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhusaṅghau, tathārūpāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā| yathārūpāya parisāya appaṃ din‍naṃ bahu hauti bahu din‍naṃ bahutaraṃ, tathārūpau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhusaṅghau, tathārūpāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā| yathārūpaṃ parisaṃ alaṃ yaujanagaṇanānipi dassanāya gantuṃ api puṭausaināpi, tathārūpau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhusaṅghau, (tathārūpāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, parisā) ( ) sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai daivappattā viharanti; santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai brahmappattā viharanti; santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai ānaiñ‍jappattā viharanti; santi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṅghai ariyappattā viharanti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu daivappattau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu daivappattau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu brahmappattau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā  dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| karuṇā… muditā… upaikkhāsahagataina  caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu brahmappattau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ānaiñ‍jappattau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ  samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ānaiñ‍jappattau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyappattau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyappattau hautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

brāhmaṇavaggau yaudhājīvavaggau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

yaudhā pāṭibhaugasutaṃ, abhayaṃ brāhmaṇasac‍caina pañ‍camaṃ|

ummaggavassakārau, upakau sacchikiriyā ca upausathauti||


(20) 5. mahāvaggau

1. sautānugatasuttaṃ

191. ‘‘sautānugatānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ, vacasā paricitānaṃ, manasānupaikkhitānaṃ, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhānaṃ cattārau ānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai  cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tassa tai dhammā sautānugatā haunti, vacasā paricitā, manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sau muṭṭhassati muṭṭhassatī (sī॰) kālaṃ kurumānau añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapaj‍jati| tassa tattha sukhinau dhammapadā p‍lavanti pilapanti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| dandhau, bhikkhavai, satuppādau; atha sau sattau khippaṃyaiva visaisagāmī hauti| sautānugatānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ, vacasā paricitānaṃ, manasānupaikkhitānaṃ, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhānaṃ ayaṃ paṭhamau ānisaṃsau pāṭikaṅkhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tassa tai dhammā sautānugatā haunti, vacasā paricitā, manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sau muṭṭhassati kālaṃ kurumānau añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapaj‍jati| tassa tattha na haiva khau sukhinau dhammapadā p‍lavanti; api  ca khau bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau daivaparisāyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ vā sau dhammavinayau, yatthāhaṃ pubbai brahmacariyaṃ acari’nti| dandhau, bhikkhavai, satuppādau; atha sau sattau khippamaiva visaisagāmī hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau kusalau bhairisaddassa| sau addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau bhairisaddaṃ suṇaiyya| tassa na haiva khau assa kaṅkhā vā vimati vā – ‘bhairisaddau nu khau, na nu khau bhairisaddau’ti! atha khau bhairisaddautvaiva niṭṭhaṃ gacchaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti  – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tassa tai dhammā sautānugatā haunti, vacasā paricitā, manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sau muṭṭhassati kālaṃ kurumānau añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapaj‍jati| tassa tattha na haiva khau sukhinau dhammapadā p‍lavanti; api ca  khau bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau daivaparisāyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ vā sau dhammavinayau, yatthāhaṃ pubbai brahmacariyaṃ acari’nti| dandhau, bhikkhavai, satuppādau; atha sau sattau khippaṃyaiva visaisagāmī hauti| sautānugatānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ, vacasā paricitānaṃ, manasānupaikkhitānaṃ, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhānaṃ ayaṃ dutiyau ānisaṃsau pāṭikaṅkhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tassa tai dhammā sautānugatā haunti, vacasā paricitā, manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sau muṭṭhassati kālaṃ kurumānau añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapaj‍jati| tassa tattha na haiva khau sukhinau dhammapadā p‍lavanti, napi bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau daivaparisāyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti; api ca khau daivaputtau daivaparisāyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ vā sau dhammavinayau, yatthāhaṃ pubbai brahmacariyaṃ acari’nti| dandhau, bhikkhavai, satuppādau; atha sau sattau khippaṃyaiva visaisagāmī hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau kusalau saṅkhasaddassa| sau addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau saṅkhasaddaṃ suṇaiyya| tassa na haiva khau assa kaṅkhā vā vimati  vā – ‘saṅkhasaddau nu khau, na nu khau saṅkhasaddau’ti! atha khau saṅkhasaddautvaiva niṭṭhaṃ gacchaiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tassa tai dhammā sautānugatā haunti, vacasā paricitā, manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sau muṭṭhassati kālaṃ kurumānau añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapaj‍jati| tassa tattha na haiva khau sukhinau dhammapadā p‍lavanti, napi bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau daivaparisāyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti; api ca khau daivaputtau daivaparisāyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ayaṃ vā sau dhammavinayau, yatthāhaṃ pubbai brahmacariyaṃ acari’nti| dandhau, bhikkhavai, satuppādau; atha sau sattau khippaṃyaiva visaisagāmī hauti| sautānugatānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ, vacasā paricitānaṃ, manasānupaikkhitānaṃ, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhānaṃ ayaṃ tatiyau ānisaṃsau pāṭikaṅkhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tassa tai dhammā sautānugatā haunti, vacasā paricitā, manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sau muṭṭhassati kālaṃ kurumānau añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapaj‍jati| tassa tattha na haiva khau sukhinau dhammapadā p‍lavanti, napi bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau daivaparisāyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti, napi daivaputtau daivaparisāyaṃ  dhammaṃ daisaiti; api ca khau aupapātikau aupapātikaṃ sāraiti – ‘sarasi tvaṃ, mārisa, sarasi tvaṃ, mārisa, yattha mayaṃ pubbai brahmacariyaṃ acarimhā’ti| sau aivamāha – ‘sarāmi, mārisa, sarāmi, mārisā’ti| dandhau, bhikkhavai, satuppādau; atha sau sattau khippaṃyaiva visaisagāmī hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dvai sahāyakā sahapaṃsukīḷikā sahapaṃsukīḷakā (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tai kadāci karahaci añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ samāgacchaiyyuṃ| añ‍ñau pana samāgacchaiyyuṃ, tamainaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sahāyakau sahāyakaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘idampi, samma, sarasi, idampi, samma, sarasī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘sarāmi, samma, sarāmi, sammā’ti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tassa tai dhammā sautānugatā haunti, vacasā paricitā, manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sau muṭṭhassati kālaṃ kurumānau añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ upapaj‍jati| tassa tattha na haiva khau sukhinau dhammapadā p‍lavanti, napi bhikkhu iddhimā caitauvasippattau daivaparisāyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti, napi daivaputtau daivaparisāyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti; api ca khau aupapātikau aupapātikaṃ sāraiti – ‘sarasi tvaṃ, mārisa, sarasi tvaṃ, mārisa, yattha mayaṃ pubbai brahmacariyaṃ acarimhā’ti| sau aivamāha – ‘sarāmi, mārisa, sarāmi, mārisā’ti| dandhau, bhikkhavai, satuppādau; atha khau sau sattau khippaṃyaiva  visaisagāmī hauti| sautānugatānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ, vacasā paricitānaṃ, manasānupaikkhitānaṃ, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhānaṃ ayaṃ catutthau ānisaṃsau pāṭikaṅkhau| sautānugatānaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ, vacasā paricitānaṃ, manasānupaikkhitānaṃ diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhānaṃ imai cattārau ānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ṭhānasuttaṃ

192. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, ṭhānāni catūhi ṭhānaihi vaiditabbāni| katamāni cattāri? saṃvāsaina, bhikkhavai, sīlaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, tañ‍ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñaina| saṃvauhāraina, bhikkhavai, saucaiyyaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, tañ‍ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñaina| āpadāsu, bhikkhavai, thāmau vaiditabbau, sau ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñaina| sākacchāya, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñā vaiditabbā, sā ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñaināti|

saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.122 ‘‘‘saṃvāsaina, bhikkhavai, sīlaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, tañ‍ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñainā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalaina saddhiṃ saṃvasamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘dīgharattaṃ khau ayamāyasmā khaṇḍakārī chiddakārī sabalakārī kammāsakārī, na santatakārī na santatavutti satatavutti (syā॰ kaṃ॰); sīlaisu dussīlau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā  sīlavā’’’ti|

‘‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalaina saddhiṃ saṃvasamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘dīgharattaṃ khau ayamāyasmā akhaṇḍakārī acchiddakārī asabalakārī akammāsakārī santatakārī  santatavutti; sīlaisu sīlavā ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā dussīlau’ti| ‘saṃvāsaina, bhikkhavai, sīlaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, tañ‍ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñainā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘saṃvauhāraina, bhikkhavai, saucaiyyaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, tañ‍ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñainā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalaina saddhiṃ saṃvauharamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘añ‍ñathā khau ayamāyasmā aikaina aikau vauharati, añ‍ñathā dvīhi, añ‍ñathā tīhi, añ‍ñathā sambahulaihi; vauk‍kamati ayamāyasmā purimavauhārā pacchimavauhāraṃ; aparisuddhavauhārau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā parisuddhavauhārau’’’ti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalaina saddhiṃ saṃvauharamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yathaiva khau ayamāyasmā aikaina aikau vauharati, tathā dvīhi, tathā tīhi, tathā sambahulaihi| nāyamāyasmā vauk‍kamati purimavauhārā pacchimavauhāraṃ; parisuddhavauhārau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā aparisuddhavauhārau’ti| ‘saṃvauhāraina, bhikkhavai, saucaiyyaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, tañ‍ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñainā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ  idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘āpadāsu, bhikkhavai, thāmau vaiditabbau, sau ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñainā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ  paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau, bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau, raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau na iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘tathābhūtau khau ayaṃ laukasan‍nivāsau tathābhūtau ayaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhau yathābhūtai laukasan‍nivāsai yathābhūtai attabhāvapaṭilābhai aṭṭha laukadhammā laukaṃ anuparivattanti laukau ca aṭṭha laukadhammai anuparivattati – lābhau ca, alābhau ca, yasau ca, ayasau ca, nindā ca, pasaṃsā ca, sukhañ‍ca, dukkhañ‍cā’ti| sau ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau saucati kilamati paridaivati, urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau raugabyasanaina  vā phuṭṭhau samānau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘tathābhūtau khau ayaṃ laukasan‍nivāsau tathābhūtau ayaṃ attabhāvapaṭilābhau yathābhūtai laukasan‍nivāsai yathābhūtai attabhāvapaṭilābhai aṭṭha laukadhammā laukaṃ anuparivattanti laukau ca aṭṭha laukadhammai anuparivattati – lābhau ca, alābhau ca, yasau ca, ayasau ca, nindā ca, pasaṃsā ca, sukhañ‍ca, dukkhañ‍cā’ti| sau ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau bhaugabyasanaina vā  phuṭṭhau samānau raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhau samānau na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| ‘āpadāsu, bhikkhavai, thāmau vaiditabbau, sau ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñainā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘sākacchāya, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñā vaiditabbā, sā ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñainā’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalaina saddhiṃ sākacchāyamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yathā khau imassa āyasmatau ummaggau yathā ca abhinīhārau yathā ca pañhāsamudāhārau, duppañ‍ñau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā pañ‍ñavā| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi ayamāyasmā na caiva gambhīraṃ atthapadaṃ udāharati santaṃ paṇītaṃ atak‍kāvacaraṃ nipuṇaṃ paṇḍitavaidanīyaṃ| yañ‍ca ayamāyasmā dhammaṃ bhāsati tassa ca nappaṭibalau saṃkhittaina vā vitthāraina  vā atthaṃ ācikkhituṃ daisaituṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ paṭṭhapaituṃ vivarituṃ vibhajituṃ uttānīkātuṃ| duppañ‍ñau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā pañ‍ñavā’’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, cakkhumā purisau udakarahadassa tīrai ṭhitau passaiyya parittaṃ macchaṃ ummuj‍jamānaṃ| tassa aivamassa – ‘yathā khau imassa macchassa ummaggau yathā ca ūmighātau yathā ca vaigāyitattaṃ, parittau ayaṃ  macchau, nāyaṃ macchau mahantau’ti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalaina saddhiṃ sākacchāyamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yathā khau imassa āyasmatau ummaggau yathā ca abhinīhārau yathā ca pañhāsamudāhārau, duppañ‍ñau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā pañ‍ñavā| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi ayamāyasmā na caiva gambhīraṃ atthapadaṃ udāharati santaṃ paṇītaṃ atak‍kāvacaraṃ nipuṇaṃ paṇḍitavaidanīyaṃ| yañ‍ca ayamāyasmā dhammaṃ bhāsati, tassa ca na paṭibalau saṃkhittaina vā vitthāraina vā atthaṃ ācikkhituṃ daisaituṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ paṭṭhapaituṃ vivarituṃ vibhajituṃ uttānīkātuṃ| duppañ‍ñau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā pañ‍ñavā’’’ti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalaina saddhiṃ sākacchāyamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yathā khau imassa āyasmatau ummaggau yathā ca abhinīhārau yathā ca pañhāsamudāhārau, pañ‍ñavā ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā duppañ‍ñau| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi ayamāyasmā gambhīrañ‍caiva atthapadaṃ udāharati santaṃ paṇītaṃ atak‍kāvacaraṃ nipuṇaṃ paṇḍitavaidanīyaṃ| yañ‍ca ayamāyasmā dhammaṃ bhāsati, tassa ca paṭibalau saṃkhittaina vā vitthāraina vā atthaṃ ācikkhituṃ daisaituṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ paṭṭhapaituṃ vivarituṃ vibhajituṃ uttānīkātuṃ| pañ‍ñavā ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā duppañ‍ñau’’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, cakkhumā purisau udakarahadassa tīrai ṭhitau passaiyya mahantaṃ macchaṃ ummuj‍jamānaṃ| tassa aivamassa  – ‘yathā khau imassa macchassa ummaggau yathā ca ūmighātau yathā ca vaigāyitattaṃ, mahantau ayaṃ macchau, nāyaṃ macchau parittau’ti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalaina saddhiṃ sākacchāyamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yathā khau imassa āyasmatau ummaggau yathā ca abhinīhārau yathā ca pañhāsamudāhārau, pañ‍ñavā ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā duppañ‍ñau| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi ayamāyasmā gambhīrañ‍caiva atthapadaṃ udāharati  santaṃ paṇītaṃ atak‍kāvacaraṃ nipuṇaṃ paṇḍitavaidanīyaṃ| yañ‍ca ayamāyasmā dhammaṃ bhāsati, tassa ca paṭibalau saṃkhittaina vā vitthāraina vā atthaṃ ācikkhituṃ daisaituṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ paṭṭhapaituṃ vivarituṃ vibhajituṃ uttānīkātuṃ| pañ‍ñavā ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā duppañ‍ñau’ti|

‘‘‘sākacchāya, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñā vaiditabbā, sā ca khau dīghaina addhunā, na ittaraṃ; manasikarautā, nau amanasikarautā; pañ‍ñavatā, nau duppañ‍ñainā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri ṭhānāni imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi vaiditabbānī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. bhaddiyasuttaṃ

193. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau bhaddiyau licchavi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau bhaddiyau licchavi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai – ‘māyāvī samaṇau gautamau āvaṭṭaniṃ māyaṃ āvaṭṭanīmāyaṃ (sī॰), āvaṭṭanimāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.60 passitabbaṃ jānāti yāya añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ sāvakai āvaṭṭaitī’ti| yai tai, bhantai, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘māyāvī samaṇau gautamau āvaṭṭaniṃ māyaṃ jānāti yāya añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ sāvakai āvaṭṭaitī’ti, kac‍ci tai, bhantai, bhagavatau vuttavādinau, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa ca anudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati, anabbhakkhātukāmā hi mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavanta’’nti?

‘‘aitha  tumhai, bhaddiya, mā anussavaina, mā paramparāya, mā itikirāya, mā piṭakasampadānaina, mā tak‍kahaitu, mā nayahaitu, mā ākāraparivitak‍kaina, mā diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā, mā bhabbarūpatāya, mā ‘samaṇau nau garū’ti| yadā tumhai, bhaddiya, attanāva jānaiyyātha – ‘imai dhammā akusalā, imai dhammā sāvaj‍jā, imai dhammā viñ‍ñugarahitā, imai dhammā samattā samādin‍nā ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattantī’ti, atha tumhai, bhaddiya, pajahaiyyātha|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaddiya, laubhau purisassa ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati  hitāya vā ahitāya vā’’ti? ‘‘ahitāya, bhantai’’| ‘‘luddhau panāyaṃ, bhaddiya, purisapuggalau laubhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau pāṇampi hanati, adin‍nampi ādiyati, paradārampi gacchati, musāpi bhaṇati, parampi tathattāya tadatthāya (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.66 samādapaiti yaṃsa hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaddiya, dausau purisassa…pai॰… mauhau purisassa…pai॰… sārambhau purisassa ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati hitāya vā ahitāya vā’’ti? ‘‘ahitāya, bhantai’’| ‘‘sāraddhau panāyaṃ, bhaddiya, purisapuggalau sārambhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau pāṇampi hanati, adin‍nampi ādiyati, paradārampi gacchati, musāpi bhaṇati, parampi tathattāya samādapaiti yaṃsa hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaddiya, imai dhammā kusalā vā akusalā vā’’ti? ‘‘akusalā, bhantai’’| ‘‘sāvaj‍jā vā anavaj‍jā vā’’ti? ‘‘sāvaj‍jā, bhantai’’| ‘‘viñ‍ñugarahitā vā viñ‍ñuppasatthā vā’’ti? ‘‘viñ‍ñugarahitā, bhantai’’| ‘‘samattā samādin‍nā ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti, nau vā? kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘samattā, bhantai, samādin‍nā ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| aivaṃ nau aittha hautī’’ti|

‘‘iti khau, bhaddiya, yaṃ taṃ tai avaucumhā – aitha tumhai, bhaddiya, mā anussavaina, mā paramparāya, mā itikirāya, mā  piṭakasampadānaina, mā tak‍kahaitu, mā nayahaitu, mā ākāraparivitak‍kaina, mā diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā, mā bhabbarūpatāya, mā ‘samaṇau nau garū’ti| yadā tumhai, bhaddiya, attanāva jānaiyyātha  – ‘imai dhammā akusalā, imai  dhammā sāvaj‍jā, imai dhammā viñ‍ñugarahitā, imai dhammā samattā samādin‍nā ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattantīti, atha tumhai, bhaddiya, pajahaiyyāthā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘aitha tumhai, bhaddiya, mā anussavaina, mā paramparāya, mā itikirāya, mā piṭakasampadānaina, mā tak‍kahaitu, mā nayahaitu, mā ākāraparivitak‍kaina, mā diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā, mā bhabbarūpatāya, mā ‘samaṇau nau garū’ti| yadā tumhai, bhaddiya, attanāva  jānaiyyātha – ‘imai dhammā kusalā, imai dhammā anavaj‍jā, imai dhammā viñ‍ñuppasatthā, imai dhammā samattā samādin‍nā hitāya sukhāya saṃvattantī’ti, atha tumhai, bhaddiya, upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyāthā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaddiya, alaubhau purisassa ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati hitāya vā ahitāya vā’’ti? ‘‘hitāya, bhantai’’| ‘‘aluddhau panāyaṃ, bhaddiya, purisapuggalau laubhaina anabhibhūtau apariyādin‍nacittau naiva pāṇaṃ hanati, na adin‍naṃ ādiyati, na paradāraṃ gacchati, na musā bhaṇati, parampi tathattāya na samādapaiti yaṃ’sa hauti dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaddiya, adausau purisassa…pai॰… amauhau purisassa…pai॰… asārambhau purisassa ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati hitāya vā ahitāya vā’’ti? ‘‘hitāya, bhantai’’| ‘‘asāraddhau panāyaṃ, bhaddiya, purisapuggalau sārambhaina anabhibhūtau apariyādin‍nacittau naiva pāṇaṃ hanati, na adin‍naṃ ādiyati, na paradāraṃ gacchati, na musā bhaṇati, parampi tathattāya na samādapaiti yaṃ’sa hauti dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhaddiya, imai dhammā kusalā vā akusalā vā’’ti? ‘‘kusalā, bhantai’’| ‘‘sāvaj‍jā vā anavaj‍jā vā’’ti? ‘‘anavaj‍jā, bhantai’’| ‘‘viñ‍ñugarahitā vā viñ‍ñuppasatthā vā’’ti? ‘‘viñ‍ñuppasatthā, bhantai’’| ‘‘samattā  samādin‍nā hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti nau vā? kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘samattā, bhantai, samādin‍nā hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti| aivaṃ nau aittha hautī’’ti|

‘‘iti  khau, bhaddiya, yaṃ taṃ tai avaucumhā – aitha tumhai, bhaddiya, mā anussavaina, mā paramparāya, mā itikirāya, mā piṭakasampadānaina, mā tak‍kahaitu, mā nayahaitu, mā ākāraparivitak‍kaina, mā diṭṭhinijjhānakkhantiyā, mā bhabbarūpatāya, mā ‘samaṇau nau garū’ti| yadā tumhai, bhaddiya, attanāva jānaiyyātha – ‘imai dhammā kusalā, imai dhammā anavaj‍jā, imai dhammā viñ‍ñuppasatthā, imai dhammā samattā samādin‍nā hitāya sukhāya saṃvattantīti, atha tumhai, bhaddiya, upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyāthā’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘yai khau tai, bhaddiya, laukai santau sappurisā tai sāvakaṃ aivaṃ samādapainti – ‘aihi tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, laubhaṃ vinaiyya vinaiyya vinaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) viharāhi| laubhaṃ vinaiyya viharantau na laubhajaṃ kammaṃ karissasi kāyaina vācāya manasā| dausaṃ vinaiyya viharāhi| dausaṃ vinaiyya viharantau na dausajaṃ kammaṃ  karissasi kāyaina vācāya manasā| mauhaṃ vinaiyya viharāhi| mauhaṃ vinaiyya viharantau na mauhajaṃ kammaṃ karissasi kāyaina vācāya manasā| sārambhaṃ vinaiyya viharāhi| sārambhaṃ vinaiyya viharantau na sārambhajaṃ kammaṃ karissasi kāyaina vācāya manasā’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai bhaddiyau licchavi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

‘‘api nu tāhaṃ, bhaddiya, aivaṃ avacaṃ – ‘aihi mai tvaṃ, bhaddiya, sāvakau hauhi; ahaṃ satthā bhavissāmī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃvādiṃ khau maṃ, bhaddiya, aivamakkhāyiṃ aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā asatā tucchā musā abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti – ‘māyāvī samaṇau gautamau āvaṭṭaniṃ māyaṃ jānāti yāya añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ sāvakai āvaṭṭaitī’’’ti| ‘‘bhaddikā, bhantai, āvaṭṭanī māyā| kalyāṇī, bhantai, āvaṭṭanī māyā| piyā mai, bhantai, ñātisālauhitā imāya āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ, piyānampi mai assa ñātisālauhitānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sabbai caipi, bhantai, khattiyā imāya āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ, sabbaisampissa khattiyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sabbai caipi, bhantai, brāhmaṇā… vaissā  … suddā imāya āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ, sabbaisampissa suddānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhaddiya, aivamaitaṃ, bhaddiya! sabbai caipi, bhaddiya, khattiyā imāya āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ akusaladhammappahānāya kusaladhammūpasampadāya, sabbaisampissa khattiyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sabbai  caipi, bhaddiya, brāhmaṇā… vaissā… suddā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ akusaladhammappahānāya kusaladhammūpasampadāya, sabbaisampissa suddānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sadaivakau caipi, bhaddiya, laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā imāya  āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ āvaṭṭaiyya (?) akusaladhammappahānāya kusaladhammūpasampadāya, sadaivakassapissa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| imai caipi, bhaddiya, mahāsālā imāya āvaṭṭaniyā āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ akusaladhammappahānāya kusaladhammūpasampadāya, imaisampissa mahāsālānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya ( ) (sacai caitaiyyuṃ) (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), (āvaṭṭaiyyuṃ) (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 8.44| kau pana vādau manussabhūtassā’’ti! tatiyaṃ|

4. sāmugiyasuttaṃ

194. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kauliyaisu viharati sāmugaṃ nāma sāpūgaṃ nāma (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kauliyānaṃ nigamau| atha khau sambahulā sāmugiyā kauliyaputtā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai sāmugiyai kauliyaputtai āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘cattārimāni, byagghapaj‍jā, pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgāni taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina  sammadakkhātāni sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ saukapariddavānaṃ (sī॰) samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya| katamāni cattāri? sīlapārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, cittapārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, diṭṭhipārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ, vimuttipārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, byagghapaj‍jā, sīlapārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ? idha, byagghapaj‍jā, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍jā, sīlapārisuddhi| iti aivarūpiṃ sīlapārisuddhiṃ aparipūraṃ vā paripūraissāmi paripūraṃ vā tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmīti, yau tattha chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, idaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍jā, sīlapārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, byagghapaj‍jā, cittapārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ? idha, byagghapaj‍jā, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰…  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍jā, cittapārisuddhi| iti aivarūpiṃ cittapārisuddhiṃ aparipūraṃ vā paripūraissāmi paripūraṃ vā tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmīti, yau tattha chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, idaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍jā, cittapārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, byagghapaj‍jā, diṭṭhipārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ? idha, byagghapaj‍jā, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍jā, diṭṭhipārisuddhi| iti aivarūpiṃ diṭṭhipārisuddhiṃ aparipūraṃ vā…pai॰… tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmīti, yau tattha chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, idaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍jā, diṭṭhipārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, byagghapaj‍jā, vimuttipārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ? sa khau sau, byagghapaj‍jā, ariyasāvakau iminā ca sīlapārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaina saman‍nāgatau iminā ca cittapārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaina  saman‍nāgatau iminā ca diṭṭhipārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaina saman‍nāgatau rajanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ virājaiti, vimaucanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ vimaucaiti| sau rajanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ virājaitvā, vimaucanīyaisu dhammaisu cittaṃ vimaucaitvā sammāvimuttiṃ phusati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍jā, vimuttipārisuddhi| iti aivarūpiṃ vimuttipārisuddhiṃ aparipūraṃ vā paripūraissāmi paripūraṃ vā tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmīti, yau tattha chandau ca vāyāmau ca ussāhau ca ussauḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, idaṃ vuc‍cati, byagghapaj‍jā, vimuttipārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgaṃ|

‘‘imāni  khau, byagghapaj‍jā, cattāri pārisuddhipadhāniyaṅgāni taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sammadakkhātāni sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya  dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. vappasuttaṃ

195. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau vappau sak‍kau nigaṇṭhasāvakau yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau vappaṃ sak‍kaṃ nigaṇṭhasāvakaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘idhassa, vappa, kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau avij‍jāvirāgā vij‍juppādā| passasi nau tvaṃ, vappa, taṃ ṭhānaṃ yataunidānaṃ purisaṃ dukkhavaidaniyā āsavā assavaiyyuṃ anvāssavaiyyuṃ (ka॰) abhisamparāya’’nti? ‘‘passāmahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ ṭhānaṃ| idhassa, bhantai, pubbai pāpakammaṃ kataṃ avipak‍kavipākaṃ| tataunidānaṃ purisaṃ dukkhavaidaniyā āsavā assavaiyyuṃ abhisamparāya’’nti| ayañ‍caiva khau pana āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa vappaina sak‍kaina nigaṇṭhasāvakaina saddhiṃ antarākathā vippakatā hauti|

atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau  yaina upaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kāya nuttha, mauggal‍lāna, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā; kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, vappaṃ sak‍kaṃ nigaṇṭhasāvakaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘idhassa, vappa, kāyaina saṃvutau vācāya saṃvutau manasā saṃvutau avij‍jāvirāgā vij‍juppādā| passasi nau tvaṃ, vappa, taṃ ṭhānaṃ yataunidānaṃ purisaṃ dukkhavaidaniyā āsavā assavaiyyuṃ abhisamparāya’nti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, vappau sak‍kau nigaṇṭhasāvakau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘passāmahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ ṭhānaṃ| idhassa, bhantai, pubbai pāpakammaṃ kataṃ avipak‍kavipākaṃ| tataunidānaṃ purisaṃ dukkhavaidaniyā āsavā assavaiyyuṃ abhisamparāya’nti| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, vappaina sak‍kaina nigaṇṭhasāvakaina saddhiṃ antarākathā vippakatā; atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā vappaṃ sak‍kaṃ nigaṇṭhasāvakaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘sacai mai tvaṃ, vappa, anuñ‍ñaiyyañ‍caiva anujānaiyyāsi, paṭik‍kausitabbañ‍ca paṭik‍kausaiyyāsi, yassa ca mai bhāsitassa atthaṃ na jānaiyyāsi mamaivaittha uttari paṭipucchaiyyāsi – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ, imassa kau atthau’ti, siyā nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti| ‘‘anuñ‍ñaiyyañ‍caivāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau anujānissāmi, paṭik‍kausitabbañ‍ca paṭik‍kausissāmi, yassa cāhaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitassa atthaṃ na jānissāmi bhagavantaṃyaivaittha uttari paṭipucchissāmi – ‘idaṃ bhantai, kathaṃ, imassa kau atthau’ti? hautu nau aittha kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, vappa, yai kāyasamārambhapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, kāyasamārambhā paṭiviratassa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| sau navañ‍ca kammaṃ na karauti, purāṇañ‍ca kammaṃ phussa phussa  byantīkarauti, sandiṭṭhikā nij‍jarā akālikā aihipassikā aupanaiyyikā pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbā viñ‍ñūhi viñ‍ñūhīti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.364 passitabbaṃ| passasi nau tvaṃ, vappa, taṃ ṭhānaṃ yataunidānaṃ purisaṃ dukkhavaidaniyā āsavā assavaiyyuṃ abhisamparāya’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, vappa, yai vacīsamārambhapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, vacīsamārambhā paṭiviratassa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| sau navañ‍ca kammaṃ na karauti, purāṇañ‍ca kammaṃ phussa phussa byantīkarauti| sandiṭṭhikā nij‍jarā akālikā aihipassikā aupanaiyyikā pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbā viñ‍ñūhi| passasi nau tvaṃ, vappa, taṃ ṭhānaṃ yataunidānaṃ purisaṃ dukkhavaidaniyā āsavā assavaiyyuṃ  abhisamparāya’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, vappa, yai manausamārambhapac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, manausamārambhā paṭiviratassa aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| sau navañ‍ca kammaṃ na karauti, purāṇañ‍ca kammaṃ phussa phussa byantīkarauti| sandiṭṭhikā nij‍jarā akālikā  aihipassikā aupanaiyyikā  pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbā viñ‍ñūhi| passasi nau tvaṃ, vappa, taṃ ṭhānaṃ yataunidānaṃ purisaṃ dukkhavaidaniyā āsavā assavaiyyuṃ abhisamparāya’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, vappa, yai avij‍jāpac‍cayā uppaj‍janti āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, avij‍jāvirāgā vij‍juppādā aivaṃsa tai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| sau navañ‍ca kammaṃ na karauti, purāṇañ‍ca kammaṃ phussa phussa byantīkarauti| sandiṭṭhikā nij‍jarā akālikā aihipassikā aupanaiyyikā pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbā viñ‍ñūhi| passasi nau tvaṃ, vappa, taṃ ṭhānaṃ yataunidānaṃ purisaṃ dukkhavaidaniyā āsavā assavaiyyuṃ abhisamparāya’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivaṃ sammā vimuttacittassa khau, vappa, bhikkhunau cha satatavihārā adhigatā haunti| sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau; upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰.. jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau; upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| sau kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ  vaidanaṃ vaidiyāmī’ti pajānāti; jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyāmī’ti pajānāti; ‘kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītī bhavissantī’ti pajānāti’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, vappa, thūṇaṃ paṭic‍ca chāyā pañ‍ñāyati| atha purisau  āgacchaiyya kuddālapiṭakaṃ ādāya| sau taṃ thūṇaṃ mūlai chindaiyya; mūlai chinditvā palikhaṇaiyya; palikhaṇitvā mūlāni uddharaiyya, antamasau usīranāḷimattānipi usīranāḷamattānipi (sī॰)| sau taṃ thūṇaṃ khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṃ chindaiyya| khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṃ chaitvā phālaiyya| phālaitvā sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ karaiyya| sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ katvā vātātapai visausaiyya| vātātapai visausaitvā agginā ḍahaiyya| agginā ḍahaitvā masiṃ karaiyya| masiṃ karitvā mahāvātai vā auphuṇaiyya nadiyā vā sīghasautāya pavāhaiyya| aivaṃ  hissa, vappa, yā thūṇaṃ paṭic‍ca chāyā sā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, vappa, aivaṃ sammā vimuttacittassa bhikkhunau cha satatavihārā adhigatā haunti| sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau; upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya naiva sumanau hauti na dummanau; upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau| sau kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyāmī’ti pajānāti; jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyāmī’ti  pajānāti; ‘kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītī bhavissantī’ti pajānāti’’|

aivaṃ vuttai vappau sak‍kau nigaṇṭhasāvakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, purisau udayatthikau assapaṇiyaṃ pausaiyya| sau udayañ‍caiva nādhigacchaiyya, uttariñ‍ca kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assa| aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, udayatthikau bālai nigaṇṭhai payirupāsiṃ| svāhaṃ udayañ‍caiva nādhigacchiṃ, uttariñ‍ca kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī ahausiṃ| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, aj‍jataggai yau mai bālaisu nigaṇṭhaisu pasādau taṃ mahāvātai vā auphuṇāmi nadiyā vā sīghasautāya pavāhaimi| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sāḷhasuttaṃ

196. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau sāḷhau ca licchavi abhayau ca licchavi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sāḷhau licchavi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘santi, bhantai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā dvayaina aughassa nittharaṇaṃ pañ‍ñapainti  – sīlavisuddhihaitu ca tapaujigucchāhaitu ca| idha, bhantai, bhagavā kimāhā’’ti?

‘‘sīlavisuddhiṃ  khau ahaṃ, sāḷha, añ‍ñataraṃ sāmañ‍ñaṅganti vadāmi| yai tai, sāḷha, samaṇabrāhmaṇā tapaujigucchāvādā tapaujigucchāsārā tapaujigucchāal‍līnā viharanti, abhabbā tai aughassa nittharaṇāya| yaipi tai, sāḷha, samaṇabrāhmaṇā aparisuddhakāyasamācārā aparisuddhavacīsamācārā aparisuddhamanausamācārā aparisuddhājīvā, abhabbā tai ñāṇadassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, sāḷha, purisau nadiṃ taritukāmau tiṇhaṃ kuṭhāriṃ kudhāriṃ (ka॰) ādāya vanaṃ pavisaiyya| sau tattha passaiyya mahatiṃ sālalaṭṭhiṃ ujuṃ navaṃ akuk‍kuc‍cakajātaṃ| tamainaṃ mūlai chindaiyya; mūlai chaitvā aggai chindaiyya; aggai chaitvā sākhāpalāsaṃ suvisaudhitaṃ visaudhaiyya; sākhāpalāsaṃ suvisaudhitaṃ visaudhaitvā kuṭhārīhi tacchaiyya; kuṭhārīhi tacchaitvā vāsīhi tacchaiyya; vāsīhi tacchaitvā laikhaṇiyā likhaiyya; laikhaṇiyā likhitvā pāsāṇaguḷaina dhauvaiyya dhaupaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰); pāsāṇaguḷaina dhauvaitvā nadiṃ patāraiyya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sāḷha, bhabbau nu khau sau purisau nadiṃ taritu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘asu hi, bhantai, sālalaṭṭhi bahiddhā suparikammakatā  antau avisuddhā| tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – sālalaṭṭhi saṃsīdissati, purisau anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jissatī’’ti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, sāḷha, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā tapaujigucchāvādā tapaujigucchāsārā tapaujigucchāal‍līnā viharanti, abhabbā tai aughassa nittharaṇāya| yaipi tai, sāḷha, samaṇabrāhmaṇā aparisuddhakāyasamācārā aparisuddhavacīsamācārā aparisuddhamanausamācārā  aparisuddhājīvā, abhabbā tai ñāṇadassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya|

‘‘yai  ca khau tai, sāḷha, samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapaujigucchāvādā na tapaujigucchāsārā na tapaujigucchāal‍līnā viharanti, bhabbā tai aughassa nittharaṇāya| yaipi tai, sāḷha, samaṇabrāhmaṇā parisuddhakāyasamācārā parisuddhavacīsamācārā parisuddhamanausamācārā parisuddhājīvā, bhabbā tai ñāṇadassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, sāḷha, purisau nadiṃ taritukāmau tiṇhaṃ kuṭhāriṃ ādāya vanaṃ pavisaiyya| sau tattha passaiyya mahatiṃ sālalaṭṭhiṃ ujuṃ navaṃ akuk‍kuc‍cakajātaṃ| tamainaṃ mūlai chindaiyya; mūlai chinditvā aggai chindaiyya; aggai chinditvā sākhāpalāsaṃ suvisaudhitaṃ visaudhaiyya; sākhāpalāsaṃ suvisaudhitaṃ visaudhaitvā kuṭhārīhi tacchaiyya; kuṭhārīhi tacchaitvā vāsīhi tacchaiyya; vāsīhi tacchaitvā nikhādanaṃ ādāya antau suvisaudhitaṃ visaudhaiyya; antau suvisaudhitaṃ visaudhaitvā laikhaṇiyā likhaiyya; laikhaṇiyā likhitvā pāsāṇaguḷaina dhauvaiyya; pāsāṇaguḷaina dhauvaitvā nāvaṃ karaiyya; nāvaṃ katvā phiyārittaṃ piyārittaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) bandhaiyya; phiyārittaṃ bandhitvā nadiṃ patāraiyya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sāḷha, bhabbau nu khau sau purisau nadiṃ taritu’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘asu hi, bhantai, sālalaṭṭhi bahiddhā suparikammakatā, antau suvisuddhā nāvākatā suvisuddhakatā (ka॰) phiyārittabaddhā| tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – ‘nāvā na saṃsīdissati, purisau sautthinā pāraṃ gamissatī’’’ti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, sāḷha, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapaujigucchāvādā na tapaujigucchāsārā na tapaujigucchāal‍līnā viharanti, bhabbā tai aughassa nittharaṇāya| yaipi tai, sāḷha, samaṇabrāhmaṇā parisuddhakāyasamācārā  parisuddhavacīsamācārā parisuddhamanausamācārā parisuddhājīvā, bhabbā tai ñāṇadassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya| saiyyathāpi, sāḷha, yaudhājīvau bahūni caipi kaṇḍacitrakāni jānāti; atha khau sau tīhi ṭhānaihi rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi tīhi? dūraipātī ca, akkhaṇavaidhī ca, mahatau ca kāyassa padālaitā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, sāḷha, yaudhājīvau dūraipātī; aivamaivaṃ khau, sāḷha, ariyasāvakau sammāsamādhi hauti| sammāsamādhi, sāḷha, ariyasāvakau yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ  ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai  kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, sāḷha, yaudhājīvau akkhaṇavaidhī; aivamaivaṃ khau, sāḷha, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti| sammādiṭṭhi, sāḷha, ariyasāvakau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, sāḷha, yaudhājīvau mahatau kāyassa padālaitā; aivamaivaṃ khau, sāḷha, ariyasāvakau sammāvimutti hauti| sammāvimutti, sāḷha, ariyasāvakau mahantaṃ avij‍jākkhandhaṃ padālaitī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mal‍likādaivīsuttaṃ

197. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau mal‍likā daivī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau mal‍likā daivī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘kau  nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau mātugāmau dubbaṇṇā ca hauti durūpā supāpikā syā॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu ‘‘dubbaṇau ca hauti durūpau supāpakau’’ti aivamādinā pul‍liṅgikavasaina dissati dassanāya; daliddā ca hauti appassakā appabhaugā appaisakkhā ca?

‘‘kau  pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau mātugāmau dubbaṇṇā ca hauti durūpā supāpikā dassanāya; aḍḍhā ca hauti mahaddhanā mahābhaugā mahaisakkhā ca?

‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau mātugāmau abhirūpā ca hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā; daliddā ca hauti appassakā appabhaugā appaisakkhā ca?

‘‘kau  pana, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau mātugāmau abhirūpā ca hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā, aḍḍhā ca hauti mahaddhanā mahābhaugā mahaisakkhā cā’’ti?

‘‘idha, mal‍likai, aikac‍cau mātugāmau kaudhanā hauti upāyāsabahulā| appampi vuttā samānā abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| sā sau (syā॰) na dātā hauti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| issāmanikā issāmanakau (syā॰) khau pana hauti; paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu issati upadussati issaṃ bandhati| sā cai tatau cutā itthattaṃ āgacchati, sā sau (syā॰) yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati dubbaṇṇā ca hauti durūpā supāpikā dassanāya; daliddā ca hauti appassakā appabhaugā appaisakkhā ca|

‘‘idha pana, mal‍likai, aikac‍cau mātugāmau kaudhanā hauti upāyāsabahulā| appampi vuttā samānā abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| sā dātā hauti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| anissāmanikā khau pana hauti; paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu na issati na upadussati na issaṃ bandhati| sā cai tatau cutā itthattaṃ āgacchati, sā yattha yattha  pac‍cājāyati dubbaṇṇā ca hauti durūpā supāpikā dassanāya; aḍḍhā ca hauti mahaddhanā mahābhaugā mahaisakkhā ca|

‘‘idha pana, mal‍likai, aikac‍cau mātugāmau ak‍kaudhanā hauti anupāyāsabahulā| bahumpi vuttā  samānā nābhisaj‍jati na kuppati na byāpaj‍jati na patitthīyati, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| sā na dātā hauti samaṇassa  vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| issāmanikā khau pana hauti; paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu issati upadussati issaṃ bandhati| sā cai tatau cutā itthattaṃ āgacchati, sā yattha  yattha pac‍cājāyati abhirūpā ca hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā; daliddā ca hauti appassakā appabhaugā appaisakkhā ca|

‘‘idha pana, mal‍likai, aikac‍cau mātugāmau ak‍kaudhanā hauti anupāyāsabahulā| bahumpi vuttā samānā nābhisaj‍jati na kuppati na byāpaj‍jati na patitthīyati, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| sā dātā hauti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| anissāmanikā khau pana hauti; paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu na issati na upadussati na issaṃ bandhati| sā cai tatau cutā itthattaṃ āgacchati, sā yattha yattha pac‍cājāyati abhirūpā ca hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā; aḍḍhā ca hauti mahaddhanā mahābhaugā mahaisakkhā ca|

‘‘ayaṃ khau, mal‍likai, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau mātugāmau dubbaṇṇā ca hauti durūpā supāpikā dassanāya; daliddā ca hauti appassakā appabhaugā appaisakkhā ca| ayaṃ pana, mal‍likai, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau mātugāmau dubbaṇṇā ca hauti durūpā supāpikā dassanāya; aḍḍhā ca hauti mahaddhanā mahābhaugā  mahaisakkhā ca| ayaṃ khau, mal‍likai, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau mātugāmau abhirūpā ca hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā; daliddā ca hauti appassakā appabhaugā appaisakkhā ca| ayaṃ pana, mal‍likai, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau mātugāmau abhirūpā ca hauti dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā; aḍḍhā ca hauti mahaddhanā mahābhaugā mahaisakkhā cā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai mal‍likā daivī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yā nūnāhaṃ sā nūnāhaṃ (syā॰), yaṃ nūnāhaṃ (ka॰) bhantai, añ‍ñaṃ jātiṃ añ‍ñāya jātiyā (syā॰) kaudhanā  ahausiṃ upāyāsabahulā, appampi vuttā samānā  abhisaj‍jiṃ kuppiṃ byāpaj‍jiṃ  patitthīyiṃ kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātvākāsiṃ, sāhaṃ, bhantai, aitarahi dubbaṇṇā durūpā supāpikā dassanāya|

‘‘yā nūnāhaṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñaṃ jātiṃ dātā ahausiṃ samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ, sāhaṃ, bhantai, aitarahi aḍḍhā aḍḍhā ca (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) mahaddhanā mahābhaugā|

‘‘yā nūnāhaṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñaṃ jātiṃ anissāmanikā ahausiṃ, paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu na issiṃ na upadussiṃ na issaṃ bandhiṃ, sāhaṃ, bhantai, aitarahi mahaisakkhā| santi khau pana, bhantai, imasmiṃ rājakulai khattiyakañ‍ñāpi brāhmaṇakañ‍ñāpi gahapatikañ‍ñāpi, tāsāhaṃ issarādhipac‍caṃ kāraimi| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, aj‍jataggai ak‍kaudhanā bhavissāmi anupāyāsabahulā, bahumpi vuttā  samānā nābhisaj‍jissāmi na kuppissāmi na byāpaj‍jissāmi na patitthīyissāmi, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca na pātukarissāmi; dassāmi samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā an‍naṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyaṃ| anissāmanikā bhavissāmi, paralābhasak‍kāragarukāramānanavandanapūjanāsu na ississāmi na upadussissāmi na issaṃ bandhissāmi| abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsikaṃ maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. attantapasuttaṃ

198. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pu॰ pa॰ 174; ma॰ ni॰ 2.7; dī॰ ni॰ 3.314; a॰ ni॰ 3.157-163 puggalau attantapau hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau parantapau hauti paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau attantapau ca hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau, parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau naivattantapau hauti nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau  na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| sau naiva attantapau  na parantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attantapau hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau acailakau hauti muttācārau hatthāpalaikhanau naaihibhaddantikau natiṭṭhabhaddantikau  nābhihaṭaṃ na uddissakataṃ na nimantanaṃ sādiyati| sau na kumbhimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na kaḷaupimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na aiḷakamantaraṃ na daṇḍamantaraṃ na musalamantaraṃ na dvin‍naṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ na gabbhiniyā na pāyamānāya na purisantaragatāya na saṅkittīsu na yattha sā upaṭṭhitau hauti na yattha makkhikā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinī na macchaṃ na maṃsaṃ na suraṃ na mairayaṃ na thusaudakaṃ pivati| sau aikāgārikau vā hauti aikālaupikau dvāgārikau vā hauti dvālaupikau…pai॰… sattāgārikau vā hauti sattālaupikau; aikissāpi dattiyā yāpaiti dvīhipi dattīhi yāpaiti…pai॰… sattahipi dattīhi yāpaiti; aikāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti dvāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti…pai॰… sattāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti| iti aivarūpaṃ aḍḍhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati|

‘‘sau sākabhakkhaupi hauti sāmākabhakkhaupi hauti nīvārabhakkhaupi hauti daddulabhakkhaupi hauti haṭabhakkhaupi hauti kaṇabhakkhaupi hauti ācāmabhakkhaupi hauti piñ‍ñākabhakkhaupi hauti tiṇabhakkhaupi hauti gaumayabhakkhaupi hauti; vanamūlaphalāhāraupi yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī|

‘‘sau sāṇānipi dhāraiti masāṇānipi dhāraiti chavadussānipi dhāraiti paṃsukūlānipi dhāraiti tirīṭānipi dhāraiti ajinampi dhāraiti ajinakkhipampi dhāraiti kusacīrampi dhāraiti vākacīrampi dhāraiti phalakacīrampi dhāraiti kaisakambalampi dhāraiti vāḷakambalampi dhāraiti ulūkapakkhampi dhāraiti; kaisamassulaucakaupi hauti kaisamassulaucanānuyaugamanuyuttau; ubbhaṭṭhakaupi hauti āsanappaṭikkhittau; uk‍kuṭikaupi  hauti uk‍kuṭikappadhānamanuyuttau; kaṇṭakāpassayikaupi hauti kaṇṭakāpassayai saiyyaṃ kappaiti; sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau  viharati| iti aivarūpaṃ anaikavihitaṃ kāyassa ātāpanaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau  viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau attantapau hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau parantapau hauti paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau aurabbhikau hauti sūkarikau sākuṇikau māgavikau luddau macchaghātakau caurau cauraghātakau gaughātakau bandhanāgārikau, yai vā panañ‍ñaipi kaici kurūrakammantā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau parantapau hauti paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|
