‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau attantapau ca hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau rājā vā hauti khattiyau muddhāvasittau, brāhmaṇau vā hauti mahāsālau| sau puratthimaina nagarassa navaṃ santhāgāraṃ kārāpaitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kharājinaṃ nivāsaitvā sappitailaina kāyaṃ abbhañ‍jitvā magavisāṇaina piṭṭhiṃ kaṇḍuvamānau navaṃ santhāgāraṃ pavisati, saddhiṃ mahaisiyā brāhmaṇaina ca purauhitaina| sau tattha anantarahitāya bhūmiyā haritupalittāya saiyyaṃ kappaiti| aikissāya gāviyā sarūpavacchāya yaṃ aikasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina rājā yāpaiti; yaṃ dutiyasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina mahaisī yāpaiti; yaṃ tatiyasmiṃ thanai khīraṃ hauti taina brāhmaṇau purauhitau yāpaiti; yaṃ catutthasmiṃ thanai  khīraṃ hauti taina aggiṃ juhati juhanti (sī॰ pī॰); avasaisaina vacchakau yāpaiti| sau aivamāha – ‘aittakā usabhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā vacchatarā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā vacchatariyau hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā ajā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, aittakā urabbhā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya, (aittakā assā hañ‍ñantu yañ‍ñatthāya,) ( ) natthi sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu aittakā rukkhā chij‍jantu yūpatthāya, aittakā dabbhā lūyantu barihisatthāyā’ti parikammatthāyāti (ka॰)| yaipissa  tai haunti dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā taipi daṇḍataj‍jitā bhayataj‍jitā assumukhā rudamānā parikammāni karaunti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau attantapau ca hauti attaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau parantapau ca paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattantapau hauti nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau? sau anattantapau aparantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati| idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti  ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ  vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā; nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ; yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya, mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati|

‘‘sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati| adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau  hauti din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī, athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharati| abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī hauti ārācārī viratau asaddhammā gāmadhammā| musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa| pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, na itau sutvā amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, na amutra vā sutvā imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ  bhaidāya; iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā, sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā, samaggārāmau samaggaratau  samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti; yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti kālavādī bhūtavādī attavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī; nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ|

‘‘sau bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau hauti| aikabhattikau hauti rattūparatau viratau vikālabhaujanā| nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti| mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā  paṭiviratau hauti| uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau hauti| jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| hatthigavāssavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti| dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau hauti| tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau hauti| uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā paṭiviratau hauti| chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā paṭiviratau hauti|

‘‘sau santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| saiyyathāpi nāma pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva  ḍaiti, sapattabhārauva ḍaiti; aivamaivaṃ bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati, samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā  dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ; cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati; rakkhati manindriyaṃ; manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī  hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti|

‘‘sau iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, (imāya ca ariyāya santuṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau,) ( ) natthi sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu| ma॰ ni॰ 1.296; ma॰ ni॰ 2.13 passitabbaṃ iminā ca ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau saman‍nāgatau| sau (ka॰) vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanappatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| thinamiddhaṃ pahāya  vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya…pai॰…  sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya…pai॰…  (sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai) āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, puggalau naivattantapau hauti nāttaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau na parantapau na paraparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau| sau na attantapau na parantapau diṭṭhaiva dhammai nicchātau nibbutau sītībhūtau sukhappaṭisaṃvaidī brahmabhūtaina attanā viharati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. taṇhāsuttaṃ

199. bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘taṇhaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi jāliniṃ saritaṃ visaṭaṃ visattikaṃ, yāya ayaṃ laukau uddhastau pariyaunaddhau tantākulakajātau gulāguṇṭhikajātau  kulāguṇṭhikajātau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.4.199 muñ‍japabbajabhūtau  apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ saṃsāraṃ nātivattati| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamā ca sā, bhikkhavai, taṇhā jālinī saritā visaṭā visattikā, yāya ayaṃ laukau uddhastau pariyaunaddhau tantākulakajātau gulāguṇṭhikajātau muñ‍japabbajabhūtau apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ saṃsāraṃ nātivattati? aṭṭhārasa khau panimāni, bhikkhavai, taṇhāvicaritāni ajjhattikassa upādāya, aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni bāhirassa upādāya|

‘‘katamāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni ajjhattikassa upādāya? asmīti, bhikkhavai, sati itthasmīti hauti, aivaṃsmīti aivamasmi (sī॰), aivasmi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vibha॰ 973 passitabbaṃ hauti, añ‍ñathāsmīti hauti, asasmīti hauti, satasmīti hauti, santi hauti, itthaṃ santi hauti, aivaṃ santi hauti, añ‍ñathā santi hauti, apihaṃ apiha (sī॰ pī॰), api (syā॰ kaṃ॰) santi hauti, apihaṃ api (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) itthaṃ santi hauti, apihaṃ api (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivaṃ santi hauti, apihaṃ api (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) añ‍ñathā santi hauti, bhavissanti hauti, itthaṃ bhavissanti hauti, aivaṃ bhavissanti hauti, añ‍ñathā bhavissanti hauti| imāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni ajjhattikassa upādāya|

‘‘katamāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni bāhirassa upādāya? imināsmīti, bhikkhavai, sati iminā itthasmīti hauti, iminā aivaṃsmīti hauti, iminā añ‍ñathāsmīti hauti, iminā asasmīti hauti, iminā satasmīti hauti, iminā santi hauti, iminā  itthaṃ santi hauti, iminā aivaṃ santi hauti, iminā añ‍ñathā santi hauti, iminā apihaṃ santi hauti, iminā apihaṃ itthaṃ santi hauti, iminā apihaṃ aivaṃ santi hauti, iminā apihaṃ añ‍ñathā santi hauti, iminā bhavissanti hauti, iminā itthaṃ bhavissanti hauti, iminā aivaṃ bhavissanti hauti, iminā añ‍ñathā bhavissanti hauti| imāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni bāhirassa upādāya|

‘‘iti aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni ajjhattikassa upādāya, aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni bāhirassa upādāya| imāni vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni| iti aivarūpāni atītāni chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni, anāgatāni chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni, pac‍cuppan‍nāni chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni| aivaṃ aṭṭhasataṃ taṇhāvicaritaṃ haunti|

‘‘ayaṃ  khau sā, bhikkhavai, taṇhā jālinī saritā visaṭā visattikā, yāya ayaṃ laukau uddhastau pariyaunaddhau tantākulakajātau gulāguṇṭhikajātau muñ‍japabbajabhūtau apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ saṃsāraṃ nātivattatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. paimasuttaṃ

200. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, (paimāni) ( ) natthi sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu jāyanti| katamāni cattāri? paimā paimaṃ jāyati, paimā dausau jāyati, dausā paimaṃ jāyati, dausā dausau jāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paimā paimaṃ jāyati? idha, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalassa iṭṭhau hauti kantau manāpau| taṃ parai iṭṭhaina kantaina manāpaina samudācaranti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yau  khau myāyaṃ puggalau iṭṭhau kantau manāpau, taṃ parai iṭṭhaina kantaina manāpaina samudācarantī’ti| sau taisu paimaṃ janaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, paimā paimaṃ jāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, paimā dausau jāyati? idha, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalassa iṭṭhau hauti kantau manāpau| taṃ parai aniṭṭhaina akantaina amanāpaina samudācaranti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yau khau myāyaṃ puggalau iṭṭhau kantau manāpau, taṃ parai aniṭṭhaina akantaina amanāpaina samudācarantī’ti| sau taisu dausaṃ janaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, paimā dausau jāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dausā paimaṃ jāyati? idha, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalassa aniṭṭhau hauti akantau amanāpau| taṃ parai aniṭṭhaina akantaina amanāpaina samudācaranti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yau khau myāyaṃ puggalau aniṭṭhau akantau amanāpau, taṃ parai aniṭṭhaina akantaina amanāpaina samudācarantī’ti| sau taisu paimaṃ janaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dausā paimaṃ jāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dausā dausau jāyati? idha, bhikkhavai, puggalau puggalassa aniṭṭhau hauti akantau amanāpau| taṃ parai iṭṭhaina kantaina manāpaina samudācaranti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yau khau myāyaṃ puggalau aniṭṭhau  akantau amanāpau, taṃ parai iṭṭhaina kantaina manāpaina samudācarantī’ti| sau taisu dausaṃ janaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dausā dausau jāyati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri paimāni jāyanti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, yampissa paimā paimaṃ jāyati tampissa tasmiṃ  samayai na hauti, yaupissa paimā dausau jāyati saupissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti, yampissa dausā paimaṃ jāyati tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti, yaupissa dausā dausau jāyati saupissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ  jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, yampissa paimā paimaṃ jāyati tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti, yaupissa paimā dausau jāyati saupissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti, yampissa dausā paimaṃ jāyati tampissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti, yaupissa dausā dausau jāyati saupissa tasmiṃ samayai na hauti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, yampissa paimā paimaṃ jāyati tampissa pahīnaṃ hauti ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ, yaupissa paimā dausau jāyati saupissa pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau, yampissa dausā paimaṃ jāyati tampissa pahīnaṃ hauti ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ, yaupissa dausā dausau jāyati saupissa pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu naiva ussainaiti na paṭisainaiti na paṭissainaiti (sī॰ pī॰) na dhūpāyati na paj‍jalati na  sampajjhāyati na apajjhāyati (sī॰), na pajjhāyati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ussainaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; vaidanaṃ attatau  samanupassati, vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā vaidanaṃ, vaidanāya vā attānaṃ; sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati, sañ‍ñāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā sañ‍ñaṃ, sañ‍ñāya vā attānaṃ; saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati, saṅkhāravantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā saṅkhārai, saṅkhāraisu vā attānaṃ; viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ussainaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na ussainaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; na vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati, na vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā vaidanaṃ, na vaidanāya vā attānaṃ; na sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati, na sañ‍ñāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā sañ‍ñaṃ, na sañ‍ñāya vā attānaṃ; na saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati, na saṅkhāravantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā saṅkhārai, na saṅkhāraisu vā attānaṃ; na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na ussainaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisainaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ak‍kausantaṃ pac‍cak‍kausati, rausantaṃ paṭirausati, bhaṇḍantaṃ paṭibhaṇḍati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisainaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na paṭisainaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ak‍kausantaṃ na pac‍cak‍kausati, rausantaṃ na paṭirausati, bhaṇḍantaṃ na paṭibhaṇḍati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na paṭisainaiti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhūpāyati? asmīti, bhikkhavai, sati itthasmīti hauti, aivaṃsmīti hauti, añ‍ñathāsmīti hauti, asasmīti hauti, satasmīti hauti, santi hauti, itthaṃ santi hauti, aivaṃ santi hauti, añ‍ñathā santi hauti, apihaṃ santi hauti, apihaṃ itthaṃ santi hauti, apihaṃ aivaṃ santi hauti, apihaṃ añ‍ñathā santi hauti, bhavissanti hauti, itthaṃ bhavissanti hauti, aivaṃ bhavissanti hauti, añ‍ñathā bhavissanti hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhūpāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na dhūpāyati? asmīti, bhikkhavai, asati itthasmīti na hauti, aivaṃsmīti na hauti, añ‍ñathāsmīti na hauti, asasmīti na hauti, satasmīti na hauti, santi na hauti, itthaṃ santi na hauti, aivaṃ santi na hauti, añ‍ñathā santi na hauti, apihaṃ santi na hauti, apihaṃ itthaṃ santi  na hauti, apihaṃ aivaṃ santi na hauti, apihaṃ añ‍ñathā santi na hauti, bhavissanti na hauti, itthaṃ bhavissanti na hauti, aivaṃ bhavissanti na hauti, añ‍ñathā bhavissanti na hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na dhūpāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paj‍jalati? iminā asmīti, bhikkhavai, sati iminā itthasmīti  hauti, iminā aivaṃsmīti hauti, iminā añ‍ñathāsmīti hauti, iminā asasmīti hauti, iminā satasmīti hauti, iminā santi hauti, iminā itthaṃ santi hauti, iminā aivaṃ santi hauti, iminā añ‍ñathā santi hauti, iminā apihaṃ santi hauti, iminā apihaṃ itthaṃ santi hauti, iminā apihaṃ aivaṃ santi hauti, iminā apihaṃ añ‍ñathā santi hauti, iminā bhavissanti hauti, iminā itthaṃ bhavissanti hauti, iminā aivaṃ bhavissanti hauti, iminā añ‍ñathā bhavissanti hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paj‍jalati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na paj‍jalati? iminā asmīti, bhikkhavai, asati iminā itthasmīti na hauti, iminā aivaṃsmīti na hauti, iminā añ‍ñathāsmīti na hauti, iminā asasmīti na hauti, iminā satasmīti na hauti, iminā santi na hauti, iminā itthaṃ santi na hauti, iminā aivaṃ santi na hauti, iminā añ‍ñathā santi na hauti, iminā apihaṃ santi na hauti, iminā apihaṃ itthaṃ santi na hauti, iminā apihaṃ aivaṃ santi na hauti, iminā apihaṃ añ‍ñathā santi na hauti, iminā bhavissanti na hauti, iminā itthaṃ bhavissanti na hauti, iminā aivaṃ bhavissanti na hauti, iminā añ‍ñathā bhavissanti na hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na paj‍jalati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sampajjhāyati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau asmimānau pahīnau na hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  sampajjhāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na sampajjhāyati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau asmimānau pahīnau hauti ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na sampajjhāyatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

mahāvaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sautānugataṃ ṭhānaṃ, bhaddiya sāmugiya vappa sāḷhā ca|

mal‍lika attantāpau, taṇhā paimaina ca dasā taiti||

catutthamahāpaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



5. pañ‍camapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(21) 1. sappurisavaggau

1. sikkhāpadasuttaṃ

201. ‘‘asappurisañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, asappurisaina asappurisatarañ‍ca; sappurisañ‍ca, sappurisaina sappurisatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātī hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātai samādapaiti; attanā ca adin‍nādāyī hauti, parañ‍ca adin‍nādānai samādapaiti; attanā ca kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, parañ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārai samādapaiti; attanā ca musāvādī hauti, parañ‍ca musāvādai samādapaiti; attanā ca surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī hauti, parañ‍ca surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānai samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā  paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau  hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā  ca kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca musāvādā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau’’ti pu॰ pa॰ 135| paṭhamaṃ|

2. assaddhasuttaṃ

202. ‘‘asappurisañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, asappurisaina asappurisatarañ‍ca; sappurisañ‍ca, sappurisaina sappurisatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, appassutau hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca assaddhau hauti, parañ‍ca assaddhiyai asaddhāya (ka॰) samādapaiti; attanā ca ahirikau  hauti, parañ‍ca ahirikatāya samādapaiti; attanā ca anauttappī hauti, parañ‍ca anauttappai samādapaiti; attanā ca appassutau hauti, parañ‍ca appassutai samādapaiti; attanā ca kusītau hauti, parañ‍ca kausaj‍jai samādapaiti; attanā ca muṭṭhassati hauti, parañ‍ca muṭṭhassac‍cai muṭṭhasac‍cai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samādapaiti; attanā ca duppañ‍ñau hauti, parañ‍ca duppañ‍ñatāya samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, satimā hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca saddhāsampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca saddhāsampadāya samādapaiti; attanā ca hirimā hauti, parañ‍ca hirimatāya hirisampadāya (ka॰) samādapaiti; attanā ca auttappī hauti, parañ‍ca auttappai samādapaiti; attanā ca bahussutau hauti, parañ‍ca bāhusac‍cai samādapaiti; attanā ca āraddhavīriyau hauti, parañ‍ca vīriyārambhai samādapaiti; attanā ca upaṭṭhitassati hauti, parañ‍ca satiupaṭṭhānai satipaṭṭhānai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samādapaiti; attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti, parañ‍ca pañ‍ñāsampadāya samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sattakammasuttaṃ

203. ‘‘asappurisañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, asappurisaina asappurisatarañ‍ca; sappurisañ‍ca, sappurisaina sappurisatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, pisuṇavācau hauti, pharusavācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātī hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātai samādapaiti; attanā ca adin‍nādāyī hauti, parañ‍ca adin‍nādānai samādapaiti; attanā ca kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, parañ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārai samādapaiti; attanā ca musāvādī hauti, parañ‍ca musāvādai samādapaiti; attanā ca pisuṇavācau hauti, parañ‍ca pisuṇāya vācāya samādapaiti; attanā ca pharusavācau  hauti, parañ‍ca pharusāya vācāya samādapaiti; attanā ca samphappalāpī hauti, parañ‍ca samphappalāpai samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau, hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau? idha bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau  hauti, parañ‍ca adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca musāvādā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca samphappalāpā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dasakammasuttaṃ

204. ‘‘asappurisañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, asappurisaina asappurisatarañ‍ca; sappurisañ‍ca, sappurisaina sappurisatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, pisuṇavācau hauti, pharusavācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti, abhijjhālu hauti, byāpan‍nacittau hauti, micchādiṭṭhikau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātī hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātai samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca abhijjhālu hauti, parañ‍ca abhijjhāya samādapaiti; attanā ca byāpan‍nacittau hauti, parañ‍ca byāpādai samādapaiti, attanā ca micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… anabhijjhālu hauti, abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, sammādiṭṭhikau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca anabhijjhālu hauti, parañ‍ca anabhijjhāya samādapaiti; attanā ca abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, parañ‍ca abyāpādai samādapaiti; attanā ca sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. aṭṭhaṅgikasuttaṃ

205. ‘‘asappurisañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, asappurisaina asappurisatarañ‍ca; sappurisañ‍ca, sappurisaina sappurisatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, micchāsaṅkappau hauti, micchāvācau hauti, micchākammantau hauti, micchāājīvau hauti, micchāvāyāmau hauti, micchāsati hauti, micchāsamādhi hauti| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau? idha bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā  ca micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca micchāsaṅkappau hauti, parañ‍ca micchāsaṅkappai samādapaiti; attanā ca micchāvācau hauti, parañ‍ca micchāvācāya samādapaiti; attanā ca micchākammantau hauti, parañ‍ca micchākammantai samādapaiti; attanā ca  micchāājīvau hauti, parañ‍ca micchāājīvai samādapaiti; attanā ca micchāvāyāmau hauti, parañ‍ca micchāvāyāmai samādapaiti; attanā ca micchāsati hauti, parañ‍ca micchāsatiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca micchāsamādhi hauti, parañ‍ca micchāsamādhimhi samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvācau hauti, sammākammantau hauti, sammāājīvau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca sammāsaṅkappau hauti, parañ‍ca sammāsaṅkappai samādapaiti; attanā ca sammāvācau hauti, parañ‍ca sammāvācāya samādapaiti; attanā ca sammākammantau hauti, parañ‍ca sammākammantai samādapaiti; attanā ca sammāājīvau hauti, parañ‍ca sammāājīvai samādapaiti; attanā ca sammāvāyāmau hauti, parañ‍ca sammāvāyāmai samādapaiti; attanā ca sammāsati hauti, parañ‍ca sammāsatiyā samādapaiti; attanā ca sammāsamādhi hauti, parañ‍ca sammāsamādhimhi samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dasamaggasuttaṃ

206. ‘‘asappurisañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, asappurisaina asappurisatarañ‍ca; sappurisañ‍ca, sappurisaina sappurisatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti  …pai॰… micchāñāṇī hauti, micchāvimutti hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca micchāñāṇī hauti, parañ‍ca micchāñāṇai samādapaiti; attanā ca micchāvimutti hauti, parañ‍ca micchāvimuttiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, asappurisaina asappurisatarau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… sammāñāṇī hauti, sammāvimutti hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca sammāñāṇī hauti, parañ‍ca sammāñāṇai samādapaiti; attanā ca sammāvimutti hauti, parañ‍ca sammāvimuttiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sappurisaina sappurisatarau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamapāpadhammasuttaṃ

207. ‘‘pāpañ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, pāpaina pāpatarañ‍ca; kalyāṇañ‍ca, kalyāṇaina kalyāṇatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pāpau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhikau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pāpaina pāpatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātī  hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātai samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpaina pāpatarau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhikau hauti| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇaina kalyāṇatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇaina kalyāṇatarau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyapāpadhammasuttaṃ

208. ‘‘pāpañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, pāpaina pāpatarañ‍ca; kalyāṇañ‍ca, kalyāṇaina kalyāṇatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasikarautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| aivaṃ…pai॰… aitadavauca –

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pāpau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… micchāñāṇī hauti, micchāvimutti hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pāpaina pāpatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca micchāñāṇī hauti, parañ‍ca micchāñāṇai samādapaiti; attanā ca micchāvimutti hauti, parañ‍ca micchāvimuttiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpaina pāpatarau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti  …pai॰… sammāñāṇī hauti, sammāvimutti hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇaina kalyāṇatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca sammāñāṇī hauti, parañ‍ca sammāñāṇai samādapaiti; attanā ca sammāvimutti hauti, parañ‍ca sammāvimuttiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇaina kalyāṇatarau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tatiyapāpadhammasuttaṃ

209. ‘‘pāpadhammañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, pāpadhammaina  pāpadhammatarañ‍ca; kalyāṇadhammañ‍ca, kalyāṇadhammaina kalyāṇadhammatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pāpadhammau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhikau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpadhammau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pāpadhammaina pāpadhammatarau? idha bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātī hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātai samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpadhammaina pāpadhammatarau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇadhammau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhikau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇadhammau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇadhammaina kalyāṇadhammatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā  samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇadhammaina kalyāṇadhammatarau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. catutthapāpadhammasuttaṃ

210. ‘‘pāpadhammañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, pāpadhammaina pāpadhammatarañ‍ca; kalyāṇadhammañ‍ca, kalyāṇadhammaina kalyāṇadhammatarañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰…|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, pāpadhammau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… micchāñāṇī hauti, micchāvimutti hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpadhammau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pāpadhammaina pāpadhammatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca  micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca micchāñāṇī hauti, parañ‍ca micchāñāṇai samādapaiti; attanā ca micchāvimutti hauti, parañ‍ca micchāvimuttiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāpadhammaina pāpadhammatarau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇadhammau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti…pai॰… sammāñāṇī hauti, sammāvimutti hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇadhammau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇadhammaina kalyāṇadhammatarau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā ca sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti…pai॰… attanā ca sammāñāṇī hauti, parañ‍ca sammāñāṇai samādapaiti; attanā ca sammāvimutti hauti, parañ‍ca sammāvimuttiyā samādapaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kalyāṇadhammaina  kalyāṇadhammatarau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sappurisavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sikkhāpadañ‍ca assaddhaṃ, sattakammaṃ athau ca dasakammaṃ|

aṭṭhaṅgikañ‍ca dasamaggaṃ, dvai pāpadhammā aparai dvaiti||


(22) 2. parisāvaggau

1. parisāsuttaṃ

211. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, parisadūsanā| katamai cattārau? bhikkhu, bhikkhavai idha bhikkhavai bhikkhu (pī॰ ka॰), dussīlau pāpadhammau parisadūsanau; bhikkhunī, bhikkhavai, dussīlā  pāpadhammā parisadūsanā; upāsakau, bhikkhavai, dussīlau pāpadhammau parisadūsanau; upāsikā, bhikkhavai, dussīlā pāpadhammā parisadūsanā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau parisadūsanā|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, parisasaubhanā| katamai cattārau? bhikkhu, bhikkhavai, sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau parisasaubhanau; bhikkhunī, bhikkhavai, sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā parisasaubhanā; upāsakau, bhikkhavai, sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau parisasaubhanau; upāsikā, bhikkhavai, sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā parisasaubhanā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau parisasaubhanā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. diṭṭhisuttaṃ

212. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? kāyaduc‍caritaina, vacīduc‍caritaina, manauduc‍caritaina, micchādiṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? kāyasucaritaina, vacīsucaritaina, manausucaritaina, sammādiṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. akatañ‍ñutāsuttaṃ

213. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? kāyaduc‍caritaina, vacīduc‍caritaina, manauduc‍caritaina, akatañ‍ñutā akatavaiditā akatañ‍ñutāakatavaiditāya (sī॰) – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? kāyasucaritaina, vacīsucaritaina, manausucaritaina, katañ‍ñutākatavaiditā katañ‍ñutākatavaiditāya (sī॰) – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. pāṇātipātīsuttaṃ

214. …pai॰… pāṇātipātī  hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti…pai॰… pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamamaggasuttaṃ

215. …pai॰…  micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, micchāsaṅkappau hauti, micchāvācau hauti, micchākammantau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvācau hauti, sammākammantau hauti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyamaggasuttaṃ

216. …pai॰… micchāājīvau  hauti, micchāvāyāmau  hauti, micchāsati hauti, micchāsamādhi hauti…pai॰… sammāājīvau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamavauhārapathasuttaṃ

217. …pai॰… adiṭṭhai diṭṭhavādī hauti, asutai sutavādī hauti, amutai mutavādī hauti, aviñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavādī hauti…pai॰… adiṭṭhai adiṭṭhavādī hauti, asutai asutavādī hauti, amutai amutavādī hauti, aviñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavādī hauti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyavauhārapathasuttaṃ

218. …pai॰… diṭṭhai adiṭṭhavādī hauti, sutai asutavādī hauti, mutai amutavādī hauti, viñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavādī hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhai diṭṭhavādī hauti, sutai sutavādī hauti, mutai mutavādī hauti, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavādī hauti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ahirikasuttaṃ

219. …pai॰… assaddhau hauti, dussīlau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti…pai॰… saddhau hauti, sīlavā hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti| navamaṃ|

10. dussīlasuttaṃ

220. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? assaddhau hauti, dussīlau hauti, kusītau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? saddhau  hauti, sīlavā hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| dasamaṃ|

parisāvaggau saubhanavaggau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

parisā diṭṭhi akatañ‍ñutā, pāṇātipātāpi dvai maggā|

dvai vauhārapathā vuttā, ahirikaṃ duppañ‍ñaina cāti||


(23) 3. duc‍caritavaggau

1. duc‍caritasuttaṃ

221. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, vacīduc‍caritāni| katamāni cattāri? musāvādau, pisuṇā vācā, pharusā vācā, samphappalāpau – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri vacīduc‍caritāni| cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, vacīsucaritāni| katamāni cattāri? sac‍cavācā, apisuṇā vācā, saṇhā vācā, mantavācā mantā vācā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri vacīsucaritānī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. diṭṭhisuttaṃ

222. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau  ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ; bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi catūhi? kāyaduc‍caritaina, vacīduc‍caritaina, manauduc‍caritaina, micchādiṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi catūhi? kāyasucaritaina, vacīsucaritaina, manausucaritaina, sammādiṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ; bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. akatañ‍ñutāsuttaṃ

223. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi catūhi? kāyaduc‍caritaina, vacīduc‍caritaina, manauduc‍caritaina, akatañ‍ñutā akatavaiditā – imaihi…pai॰… paṇḍitau… kāyasucaritaina, vacīsucaritaina, manausucaritaina katañ‍ñutākatavaiditā…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. pāṇātipātīsuttaṃ

224. … pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti…pai॰… pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamamaggasuttaṃ

225. … micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, micchāsaṅkappau hauti, micchāvācau hauti, micchākammantau hauti…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvācau hauti, sammākammantau hauti…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyamaggasuttaṃ

226. … micchāājīvau  hauti, micchāvāyāmau hauti, micchāsati hauti, micchāsamādhi hauti…pai॰… sammāājīvau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamavauhārapathasuttaṃ

227. …  adiṭṭhai diṭṭhavādī hauti, asutai sutavādī hauti, amutai mutavādī hauti, aviñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavādī hauti…pai॰… adiṭṭhai adiṭṭhavādī hauti, asutai asutavādī hauti, amutai amutavādī hauti, aviñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavādī hauti…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyavauhārapathasuttaṃ

228. … diṭṭhai adiṭṭhavādī hauti, sutai asutavādī hauti, mutai amutavādī hauti, viñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavādī hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhai  diṭṭhavādī hauti, sutai sutavādī hauti, mutai mutavādī hauti, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavādī hauti…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ahirikasuttaṃ

229. … assaddhau hauti, dussīlau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti…pai॰… saddhau hauti, sīlavā hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. duppañ‍ñasuttaṃ

230. …  assaddhau hauti, dussīlau hauti, kusītau hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti…pai॰… saddhau hauti, sīlavā hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. kavisuttaṃ

231. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, kavī| katamai cattārau? cintākavi, sutakavi, atthakavi, paṭibhānakavi – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau kavī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

duc‍caritavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

duc‍caritaṃ diṭṭhi akatañ‍ñū ca, pāṇātipātāpi dvai maggā|

dvai vauhārapathā vuttā, ahirikaṃ duppañ‍ñakavinā cāti||


(24) 4. kammavaggau

1. saṃkhittasuttaṃ

232. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ  kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) (dī॰ ni॰ 3.312; ma॰ ni॰ 2.81) akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditānī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vitthārasuttaṃ

233. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhaṃ sabyāpajjhaṃ (sabbattha) kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhaṃ  manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau sabyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā sabyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ sabyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ sabyābajjhā phassā phusanti| sau sabyābajjhaihi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau sabyābajjhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyati vaidayati (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.63 aikantadukkhaṃ, saiyyathāpi sattā nairayikā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, abyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, abyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, abyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, abyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā abyābajjhaṃ  laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ abyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ abyābajjhā phassā phusanti| sau abyābajjhaihi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau abyābajjhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyati aikantasukhaṃ, saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi  laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ sabyābajjhāpi abyābajjhāpi phassā phusanti| sau sabyābajjhaihipi abyābajjhaihipi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vaidanaṃ vaidiyati vaukiṇṇasukhadukkhaṃ, saiyyathāpi manussā aikac‍cai ca daivā aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati? tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ tassa pahānāya yā caitanā, yamidaṃ yampidaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ tassa pahānāya yā caitanā, yamidaṃ yampidaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ tassa pahānāya  yā caitanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditānī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sauṇakāyanasuttaṃ

234. atha  khau sikhāmauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sikhāmauggal‍lānau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘purimāni, bhau gautama, divasāni purimatarāni sauṇakāyanau māṇavau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘samaṇau gautamau sabbakammānaṃ akiriyaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti, sabbakammānaṃ khau pana akiriyaṃ pañ‍ñapaintau ucchaidaṃ āha laukassa – kammasac‍cāyaṃ kammasac‍cāyī (ka॰), bhau, laukau kammasamārambhaṭṭhāyī’’’ti|

‘‘dassanampi khau ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sauṇakāyanassa māṇavassa nābhijānāmi; kutau panaivarūpau kathāsal‍lāpau! cattārimāni, brāhmaṇa, kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ; atthi, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau sabyābajjhaṃ  kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā sabyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ sabyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ sabyābajjhā phassā phusanti| sau sabyābajjhaihi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau sabyābajjhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyati aikantadukkhaṃ, saiyyathāpi sattā nairayikā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, abyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, abyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, abyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, abyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā abyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ abyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ abyābajjhā phassā phusanti| sau abyābajjhaihi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau abyābajjhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidiyati aikantasukhaṃ, saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi laukaṃ  upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ sabyābajjhāpi abyābajjhāpi phassā phusanti| sau sabyābajjhaihipi abyābajjhaihipi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vaidanaṃ vaidiyati vaukiṇṇasukhadukkhaṃ, saiyyathāpi manussā aikac‍cai ca daivā aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati? tatra, brāhmaṇa, yamidaṃ kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ tassa pahānāya yā caitanā, yamidaṃ kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ tassa pahānāya yā caitanā, yamidaṃ kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ tassa pahānāya yā caitanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, brāhmaṇa, cattāri kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditānī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭhamasikkhāpadasuttaṃ

235. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī  hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati? tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditānī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyasikkhāpadasuttaṃ

236. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caina mātā aikac‍cau mātaraṃ (ka॰) jīvitā vauraupitā hauti, pitā pitaraṃ (ka॰) jīvitā vauraupitau vauraupitā (ka॰) hauti, arahaṃ arahantaṃ (ka॰) jīvitā vauraupitau  vauraupitā (ka॰) hauti, tathāgatassa duṭṭhaina cittaina lauhitaṃ  uppāditaṃ uppāditā (ka॰) hauti, saṅghau bhin‍nau hauti| idaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, anabhijjhālu hauti, abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, sammādiṭṭhi sammādiṭṭhikau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati? tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditānī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ariyamaggasuttaṃ

237. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ  akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhaṃ  kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati? sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditānī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. baujjhaṅgasuttaṃ

238. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, kammāni…pai॰… kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ…pai॰… idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sabyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ  abhisaṅkharauti…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau  sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati? satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri kammāni mayā sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaiditānī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sāvaj‍jasuttaṃ

239. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? sāvaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, sāvaj‍jaina vacīkammaina, sāvaj‍jaina manaukammaina, sāvaj‍jāya diṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? anavaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, anavaj‍jaina vacīkammaina, anavaj‍jaina manaukammaina, anavaj‍jāya diṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. abyābajjhasuttaṃ

240. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? sabyābajjhaina kāyakammaina, sabyābajjhaina vacīkammaina, sabyābajjhaina manaukammaina, sabyābajjhāya diṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? abyābajjhaina kāyakammaina, abyābajjhaina vacīkammaina, abyābajjhaina manaukammaina, abyābajjhāya diṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. samaṇasuttaṃ

241. ‘‘‘idhaiva, bhikkhavai, (paṭhamau) samaṇau, idha dutiyau samaṇau, idha tatiyau samaṇau, idha catutthau samaṇau; suñ‍ñā parappavādā samaṇaihi añ‍ñaihī’ti samaṇaihi añ‍ñaiti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) aittha añ‍ñaihīti sakāya paṭiñ‍ñāya sac‍cābhiñ‍ñaihīti atthau vaiditabbau| dī॰ ni॰ 2.214; ma॰ ni॰ 1.140 – aivamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā sīhanādaṃ nadatha|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau samaṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau samaṇau|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, dutiyau samaṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau samaṇau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, tatiyau samaṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau samaṇau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, catutthau samaṇau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau samaṇau|

‘‘‘idhaiva, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau samaṇau, idha dutiyau samaṇau, idha tatiyau samaṇau, idha catutthau  samaṇau; suñ‍ñā parappavādā samaṇaibhi añ‍ñaihī’ti – aivamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammā sīhanādaṃ nadathā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. sappurisānisaṃsasuttaṃ

242. ‘‘sappurisaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya cattārau ānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| katamai cattārau? ariyaina sīlaina vaḍḍhati, ariyaina samādhinā vaḍḍhati, ariyāya pañ‍ñāya vaḍḍhati, ariyāya vimuttiyā vaḍḍhati – sappurisaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya imai cattārau ānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

kammavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

saṃkhitta vitthāra sauṇakāyana,

sikkhāpadaṃ ariyamaggau baujjhaṅgaṃ|

sāvaj‍jañ‍caiva abyābajjhaṃ,

samaṇau ca sappurisānisaṃsauti||


(25) 5. āpattibhayavaggau

1. saṅghabhaidakasuttaṃ

243. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘api nu taṃ, ānanda, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasanta’’nti? ‘‘kutau taṃ, bhantai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamissati vūpasammissati (?)! āyasmatau, bhantai, anuruddhassa bāhiyau nāma saddhivihārikau kaivalakappaṃ saṅghabhaidāya ṭhitau| tatrāyasmā anuruddhau na aikavācikampi bhaṇitabbaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

‘‘kadā panānanda, anuruddhau saṅghamajjhai adhikaraṇaisu adhikaraṇaisu taisu (ka॰) vauyuñ‍jati! nanu, ānanda, yāni kānici adhikaraṇāni uppaj‍janti, sabbāni tāni tumhai caiva vūpasamaitha sāriputtamauggal‍lānā ca|

‘‘cattāraumai, ānanda, atthavasai sampassamānau pāpabhikkhu saṅghabhaidaina nandati| katamai cattārau? idhānanda, pāpabhikkhu dussīlau hauti pāpadhammau asuci saṅkassarasamācārau paṭicchan‍nakammantau assamaṇau samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñau abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau antaupūti  avassutau kasambujātau| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai khau maṃ bhikkhū jānissanti – dussīlau pāpadhammau asuci saṅkassarasamācārau paṭicchan‍nakammantau assamaṇau samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñau abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau antaupūti avassutau kasambujātauti, samaggā maṃ santā nāsaissanti; vaggā pana maṃ na nāsaissantī’ti| idaṃ, ānanda, paṭhamaṃ atthavasaṃ sampassamānau pāpabhikkhu saṅghabhaidaina nandati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, pāpabhikkhu micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai khau maṃ bhikkhū jānissanti – micchādiṭṭhikau antaggāhikāya  diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatauti, samaggā maṃ santā nāsaissanti; vaggā pana maṃ na nāsaissantī’ti| idaṃ, ānanda, dutiyaṃ atthavasaṃ sampassamānau pāpabhikkhu saṅghabhaidaina nandati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, ānanda, pāpabhikkhu micchāājīvau hauti, micchāājīvaina  jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) kappaiti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai khau maṃ bhikkhū jānissanti – micchāājīvau micchāājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaitīti, samaggā maṃ santā nāsaissanti; vaggā pana maṃ na nāsaissantī’ti| idaṃ, ānanda, tatiyaṃ atthavasaṃ sampassamānau pāpabhikkhu saṅghabhaidaina nandati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, ānanda, pāpabhikkhu lābhakāmau hauti sak‍kārakāmau anavañ‍ñattikāmau| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai khau maṃ bhikkhū jānissanti – lābhakāmau sak‍kārakāmau anavañ‍ñattikāmauti, samaggā maṃ santā na sak‍karissanti na garuṃ karissanti na mānaissanti na pūjaissanti; vaggā pana maṃ sak‍karissanti garuṃ karissanti mānaissanti pūjaissantī’ti| idaṃ, ānanda, catutthaṃ atthavasaṃ sampassamānau pāpabhikkhu saṅghabhaidaina nandati| imai khau, ānanda, cattārau atthavasai sampassamānau pāpabhikkhu saṅghabhaidaina nandatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. āpattibhayasuttaṃ

244. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, āpattibhayāni| katamāni cattāri? saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, cauraṃ āgucāriṃ gahaitvā rañ‍ñau dassaiyyuṃ – ‘ayaṃ tai, daiva, caurau āgucārī| imassa daivau daṇḍaṃ paṇaitū’ti| tamainaṃ rājā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘gacchatha, bhau, imaṃ purisaṃ daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhāmaitvā dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chindathā’ti| tamainaṃ rañ‍ñau purisā daḷhāya raj‍juyā  pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā dakkhiṇaina dvāraina nikkhāmaitvā dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chindaiyyuṃ| tatrañ‍ñatarassa thalaṭṭhassa purisassa aivamassa – ‘pāpakaṃ vata, bhau, ayaṃ purisau kammaṃ akāsi gārayhaṃ sīsacchaij‍jaṃ| yatra hi nāma rañ‍ñau purisā daḷhāya raj‍juyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanaṃ bandhitvā khuramuṇḍaṃ karitvā kharassaraina paṇavaina rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ parinaitvā dakkhiṇaina  dvāraina nikkhāmaitvā dakkhiṇatau nagarassa sīsaṃ chindissanti! sau vatassāhaṃ sau vatassāyaṃ (sī॰) aivarūpaṃ pāpakammaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) na karaiyyaṃ na karaiyya (sī॰) dī॰ ni॰ 1.183 pāḷiyā tadaṭṭhakathāya ca saṃsandaitabbaṃ gārayhaṃ sīsacchaij‍ja’nti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā aivaṃ tibbā bhayasañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti pārājikaisu dhammaisu| tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – anāpan‍nau vā pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ na āpaj‍jissati, āpan‍nau vā pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ yathādhammaṃ paṭikarissati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau kāḷavatthaṃ kāḷakaṃ vatthaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paridhāya kaisai pakiritvā musalaṃ khandhai āraupaitvā mahājanakāyaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, pāpakammaṃ akāsiṃ gārayhaṃ mausal‍laṃ, yaina mai āyasmantau attamanā haunti taṃ karaumī’ti| tatrañ‍ñatarassa thalaṭṭhassa purisassa aivamassa – ‘pāpakaṃ vata, bhau, ayaṃ purisau kammaṃ akāsi gārayhaṃ mausal‍laṃ| yatra hi nāma kāḷavatthaṃ paridhāya kaisai pakiritvā musalaṃ khandhai āraupaitvā mahājanakāyaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vakkhati – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, pāpakammaṃ akāsiṃ gārayhaṃ mausal‍laṃ, yaina mai āyasmantau  attamanā haunti taṃ karaumīti| sau  vatassāhaṃ aivarūpaṃ pāpakammaṃ na karaiyyaṃ gārayhaṃ mausal‍la’nti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā aivaṃ tibbā bhayasañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti saṅghādisaisaisu dhammaisu, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – anāpan‍nau vā saṅghādisaisaṃ dhammaṃ na āpaj‍jissati, āpan‍nau vā saṅghādisaisaṃ dhammaṃ yathādhammaṃ paṭikarissati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau kāḷavatthaṃ paridhāya kaisai pakiritvā bhasmapuṭaṃ assapuṭaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) khandhai āraupaitvā mahājanakāyaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, pāpakammaṃ akāsiṃ gārayhaṃ bhasmapuṭaṃ| yaina mai āyasmantau attamanā haunti taṃ karaumī’ti| tatrañ‍ñatarassa thalaṭṭhassa purisassa aivamassa – ‘pāpakaṃ vata, bhau, ayaṃ purisau kammaṃ akāsi gārayhaṃ bhasmapuṭaṃ| yatra hi nāma kāḷavatthaṃ paridhāya kaisai pakiritvā bhasmapuṭaṃ khandhai āraupaitvā mahājanakāyaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vakkhati – ahaṃ, bhantai, pāpakammaṃ akāsiṃ gārayhaṃ bhasmapuṭaṃ; yaina mai āyasmantau attamanā haunti taṃ karaumīti| sau vatassāhaṃ aivarūpaṃ pāpakammaṃ na karaiyyaṃ gārayhaṃ  bhasmapuṭa’nti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā aivaṃ tibbā bhayasañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti pācittiyaisu dhammaisu, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – anāpan‍nau vā pācittiyaṃ dhammaṃ na āpaj‍jissati, āpan‍nau vā pācittiyaṃ dhammaṃ yathādhammaṃ paṭikarissati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau  kāḷavatthaṃ paridhāya kaisai pakiritvā mahājanakāyaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, pāpakammaṃ akāsiṃ gārayhaṃ upavaj‍jaṃ| yaina mai āyasmantau attamanā haunti taṃ karaumī’ti| tatrañ‍ñatarassa thalaṭṭhassa purisassa aivamassa – ‘pāpakaṃ vata, bhau, ayaṃ purisau kammaṃ akāsi gārayhaṃ upavaj‍jaṃ| yatra hi nāma kāḷavatthaṃ paridhāya kaisai pakiritvā mahājanakāyaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ  vakkhati – ahaṃ, bhantai, pāpakammaṃ akāsiṃ gārayhaṃ upavaj‍jaṃ; yaina mai āyasmantau attamanā haunti taṃ karaumīti| sau vatassāhaṃ aivarūpaṃ pāpakammaṃ na karaiyyaṃ gārayhaṃ upavaj‍ja’nti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yassa kassaci bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā aivaṃ tibbā bhayasañ‍ñā pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti pāṭidaisanīyaisu dhammaisu, tassaitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ – anāpan‍nau vā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ dhammaṃ na āpaj‍jissati, āpan‍nau vā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ dhammaṃ yathādhammaṃ paṭikarissati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri āpattibhayānī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sikkhānisaṃsasuttaṃ

245. ‘‘sikkhānisaṃsamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ vussati pañ‍ñuttaraṃ vimuttisāraṃ satādhipataiyyaṃ| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sikkhānisaṃsaṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mayā sāvakānaṃ ābhisamācārikā sikkhā pañ‍ñattā appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, mayā sāvakānaṃ ābhisamācārikā sikkhā pañ‍ñattā appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya tathā tathā sau tassā sikkhāya akhaṇḍakārī hauti acchiddakārī  asabalakārī akammāsakārī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mayā sāvakānaṃ ādibrahmacariyikā sikkhā pañ‍ñattā sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, mayā sāvakānaṃ ādibrahmacariyikā sikkhā pañ‍ñattā sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya tathā tathā sau tassā sikkhāya akhaṇḍakārī hauti  acchiddakārī  asabalakārī akammāsakārī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sikkhānisaṃsaṃ hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñuttaraṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mayā sāvakānaṃ dhammā daisitā sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, mayā sāvakānaṃ dhammā daisitā sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya tathā tathāssa tai dhammā pañ‍ñāya samavaikkhitā haunti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñuttaraṃ hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vimuttisāraṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mayā sāvakānaṃ dhammā daisitā sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yathā yathā, bhikkhavai, mayā sāvakānaṃ dhammā daisitā sabbasau sammā dukkhakkhayāya tathā tathāssa tai dhammā vimuttiyā phusitā haunti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, vimuttisāraṃ hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, satādhipataiyyaṃ hauti? ‘iti aparipūraṃ vā ābhisamācārikaṃ sikkhaṃ paripūraissāmi, paripūraṃ vā ābhisamācārikaṃ sikkhaṃ tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmī’ti – ajjhattaṃyaiva sati sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| ‘iti aparipūraṃ vā ādibrahmacariyikaṃ sikkhaṃ paripūraissāmi, paripūraṃ vā ādibrahmacariyikaṃ sikkhaṃ tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmī’ti – ajjhattaṃyaiva sati sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| ‘iti asamavaikkhitaṃ vā  dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya samavaikkhissāmi, samavaikkhitaṃ vā dhammaṃ tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmī’ti – ajjhattaṃyaiva sati sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| ‘iti aphusitaṃ vā dhammaṃ vimuttiyā phusissāmi, phusitaṃ vā dhammaṃ tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmī’ti – ajjhattaṃyaiva sati sūpaṭṭhitā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, satādhipataiyyaṃ hauti| ‘sikkhānisaṃsamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ vussati pañ‍ñuttaraṃ vimuttisāraṃ satādhipataiyya’nti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. saiyyāsuttaṃ

246. ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, saiyyā| katamā catassau? paitasaiyyā, kāmabhaugisaiyyā, sīhasaiyyā, tathāgatasaiyyā| katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, paitasaiyyā? yaibhuyyaina, bhikkhavai, paitā uttānā sainti; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, paitasaiyyā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, kāmabhaugisaiyyā? yaibhuyyaina, bhikkhavai, kāmabhaugī vāmaina passaina sainti; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kāmabhaugisaiyyā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sīhasaiyyā? sīhau, bhikkhavai, migarājā dakkhiṇaina passaina saiyyaṃ kappaiti, pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya, antarasatthimhi naṅguṭṭhaṃ anupakkhipitvā| sau paṭibujjhitvā purimaṃ kāyaṃ abbhun‍nāmaitvā pacchimaṃ kāyaṃ anuvilaukaiti| sacai, bhikkhavai, sīhau migarājā kiñ‍ci passati kāyassa vikkhittaṃ vā visaṭaṃ vā, taina, bhikkhavai, sīhau migarājā anattamanau hauti| sacai pana, bhikkhavai, sīhau migarājā na kiñ‍ci passati kāyassa vikkhittaṃ vā visaṭaṃ vā, taina, bhikkhavai, sīhau migarājā attamanau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sīhasaiyyā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, tathāgatasaiyyā? idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, tathāgatasaiyyā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau saiyyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. thūpārahasuttaṃ

247. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, thūpārahā| katamai cattārau? tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau thūpārahau, pac‍caikabuddhau thūpārahau, tathāgatasāvakau thūpārahau, rājā cak‍kavattī thūpārahau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau thūpārahā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pañ‍ñāvuddhisuttaṃ

248. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā pañ‍ñāvuddhiyā saṃvattanti| katamai cattārau? sappurisasaṃsaivau, saddhammasavanaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārau, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau dhammā pañ‍ñāvuddhiyā saṃvattantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. bahukārasuttaṃ

249. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā manussabhūtassa bahukārā haunti| katamai cattārau? sappurisasaṃsaivau, saddhammasavanaṃ, yaunisaumanasikārau, dhammānudhammappaṭipatti – imai  khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau dhammā manussabhūtassa bahukārā hauntī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamavauhārasuttaṃ

250. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, anariyavauhārā| katamai cattārau? adiṭṭhai diṭṭhavāditā, asutai sutavāditā, amutai mutavāditā, aviñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavāditā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau anariyavauhārā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyavauhārasuttaṃ

251. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, ariyavauhārā| katamai cattārau? adiṭṭhai adiṭṭhavāditā, asutai asutavāditā, amutai amutavāditā, aviñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavāditā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau ariyavauhārā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tatiyavauhārasuttaṃ

252. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, anariyavauhārā| katamai cattārau? diṭṭhai adiṭṭhavāditā, sutai  asutavāditā, mutai amutavāditā, viñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavāditā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau anariyavauhārā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. catutthavauhārasuttaṃ

253. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, ariyavauhārā| katamai cattārau? diṭṭhai diṭṭhavāditā, sutai sutavāditā, mutai mutavāditā, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavāditā – imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau ariyavauhārā’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

āpattibhayavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

bhaidaāpatti  sikkhā ca, saiyyā thūpārahaina ca|

pañ‍ñāvuddhi bahukārā, vauhārā caturau ṭhitāti||

pañ‍camapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



(26) 6. abhiñ‍ñāvaggau

1. abhiñ‍ñāsuttaṃ

254. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā| katamai cattārau? atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā? pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhātissa vacanīyaṃ (ka॰) – imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā? avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca – imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā pahātabbā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā? samathau ca vipassanā ca – imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā? vij‍jā ca vimutti ca – imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, dhammā abhiñ‍ñā sacchikātabbā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau dhammā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pariyaisanāsuttaṃ

255. ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, anariyapariyaisanā| katamā catassau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā jarādhammau samānau jarādhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati; attanā byādhidhammau samānau byādhidhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati; attanā maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati; attanā  saṃkilaisadhammau samānau saṃkilaisadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau anariyapariyaisanā|

‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, ariyapariyaisanā| katamā catassau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā jarādhammau samānau jarādhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisati; attanā byādhidhammau samānau byādhidhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ  nibbānaṃ pariyaisati; attanā maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisati; attanā saṃkilaisadhammau samānau saṃkilaisadhammai ādīnavaṃ  viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisati| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau ariyapariyaisanā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṅgahavatthusuttaṃ

256. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, saṅgahavatthūni| katamāni cattāri? dānaṃ, paiyyavaj‍jaṃ piyavācā (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.32 passitabbaṃ, atthacariyā, samānattatā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri saṅgahavatthūnī’’ti|

4. mālukyaputtasuttaṃ

257. atha khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau māluṅkyaputtau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘aittha idāni, mālukyaputta, kiṃ daharai bhikkhū vakkhāma; yatra hi nāma tvaṃ jiṇṇau vuddhau mahal‍lakau tathāgatassa saṃkhittaina auvādaṃ yācasī’’ti! ‘‘daisaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ; daisaitu sugatau saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ| appaiva nāmāhaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānaiyyaṃ; appaiva nāmāhaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitassa dāyādau bhagavatau sāvakau (ka॰) assa’’nti|

‘‘cattāraumai, mālukyaputta, taṇhuppādā yattha bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati| katamai cattārau? cīvarahaitu vā, mālukyaputta, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati| piṇḍapātahaitu vā, mālukyaputta, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati| saināsanahaitu vā, mālukyaputta, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati| itibhavābhavahaitu vā, mālukyaputta, bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati| imai  khau, mālukyaputta, cattārau taṇhuppādā yattha bhikkhunau taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati| yatau  khau, mālukyaputta, bhikkhunau taṇhā pahīnā hauti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, mālukyaputta, ‘bhikkhu acchaicchi taṇhaṃ, vivattayi saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau bhagavatā iminā auvādaina auvaditau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā mālukyaputtau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā mālukyaputtau arahataṃ ahausīti| catutthaṃ|

5. kulasuttaṃ

258. ‘‘yāni kānici, bhikkhavai, kulāni bhaugaisu mahattaṃ pattāni na ciraṭṭhitikāni bhavanti, sabbāni tāni catūhi ṭhānaihi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina| katamaihi catūhi? naṭṭhaṃ na gavaisanti, jiṇṇaṃ na paṭisaṅkharaunti, aparimitapānabhaujanā haunti, dussīlaṃ itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā ādhipac‍cai ṭhapainti| yāni kānici, bhikkhavai, kulāni bhaugaisu mahattaṃ pattāni na ciraṭṭhitikāni bhavanti, sabbāni tāni imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina|

‘‘yāni kānici, bhikkhavai, kulāni bhaugaisu mahattaṃ pattāni ciraṭṭhitikāni bhavanti, sabbāni  tāni catūhi ṭhānaihi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraina| katamaihi catūhi? naṭṭhaṃ gavaisanti, jiṇṇaṃ paṭisaṅkharaunti, parimitapānabhaujanā haunti, sīlavantaṃ itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā ādhipac‍cai ṭhapainti| yāni kānici, bhikkhavai, kulāni bhaugaisu mahattaṃ pattāni ciraṭṭhitikāni bhavanti, sabbāni tāni imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñatarainā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭhamaājānīyasuttaṃ

259. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ  gacchati| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti balasampan‍nau ca javasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti balasampan‍nau ca javasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati  sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau hauti|
