‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyaājānīyasuttaṃ

260. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ  gacchati| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti, balasampan‍nau ca, javasampan‍nau ca, ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti, balasampan‍nau ca, javasampan‍nau ca, ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau  anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. balasuttaṃ

261. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni cattāri? vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri balānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. arañ‍ñasuttaṃ

262. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ arañ‍ñavanappatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivituṃ| katamaihi catūhi? kāmavitak‍kaina, byāpādavitak‍kaina, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaina, duppañ‍ñau hauti jaḷau ailamūgau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ arañ‍ñavanappatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivituṃ|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ arañ‍ñavanappatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivituṃ| katamaihi catūhi? naikkhammavitak‍kaina, abyāpādavitak‍kaina, avihiṃsāvitak‍kaina, pañ‍ñavā hauti ajaḷau anailamūgau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ arañ‍ñavanappatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivitu’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. kammasuttaṃ

263. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi catūhi? sāvaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, sāvaj‍jaina vacīkammaina, sāvaj‍jaina manaukammaina, sāvaj‍jāya diṭṭhiyā – imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi catūhi? anavaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, anavaj‍jaina  vacīkammaina, anavaj‍jaina manaukammaina, anavaj‍jāya diṭṭhiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

abhiñ‍ñāvaggau chaṭṭhau|


tassuddānaṃ –

abhiñ‍ñā  pariyaisanā, saṅgahaṃ mālukyaputtau|

kulaṃ dvai ca ājānīyā, balaṃ arañ‍ñakammunāti||


(27) 7. kammapathavaggau

1. pāṇātipātīsuttaṃ

264. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? attanā ca pāṇātipātī hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātai samādapaiti, pāṇātipātai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, pāṇātipātassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi catūhi? attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. adin‍nādāyīsuttaṃ

265. ‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ  nirayai| katamaihi catūhi? attanā ca adin‍nādāyī hauti, parañ‍ca adin‍nādānai samādapaiti, adin‍nādānai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, adin‍nādānassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau…pai॰…|

‘‘attanā  ca adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. micchācārīsuttaṃ

266. …  attanā  ca kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, parañ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārai samādapaiti, kāmaisumicchācārai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau…pai॰…|

attanā ca kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. musāvādīsuttaṃ

267. … attanā ca musāvādī hauti, parañ‍ca musāvādai samādapaiti, musāvādai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, musāvādassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau…pai॰…|

attanā  ca musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca musāvādā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, musāvādā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, musāvādā vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. pisuṇavācāsuttaṃ

268. … attanā ca pisuṇavācau hauti, parañ‍ca pisuṇāya vācāya samādapaiti, pisuṇāya vācāya ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi…pai॰…|

attanā ca pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pharusavācāsuttaṃ

269. …  attanā ca pharusavācau hauti, parañ‍ca pharusāya vācāya samādapaiti, pharusāya vācāya ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, pharusāya vācāya ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati…pai॰…|

attanā  ca pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā  ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. samphappalāpasuttaṃ

270. … attanā ca samphappalāpī hauti, parañ‍ca samphappalāpai samādapaiti, samphappalāpai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, samphappalāpassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi…pai॰…|

attanā  ca samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca samphappalāpā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, samphappalāpā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, samphappalāpā vairamaṇiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. abhijjhālusuttaṃ

271. … attanā ca abhijjhālu hauti, parañ‍ca abhijjhāya samādapaiti, abhijjhāya ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, abhijjhāya ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati…pai॰…|

‘‘attanā  ca anabhijjhālu hauti, parañ‍ca anabhijjhāya samādapaiti, anabhijjhāya ca samanuñ‍ñau  hauti, anabhijjhāya ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. byāpan‍nacittasuttaṃ

272. … attanā ca byāpan‍nacittau hauti, parañ‍ca byāpādai samādapaiti, byāpādai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, byāpādassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi…pai॰…|

attanā ca abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, parañ‍ca abyāpādai samādapaiti, abyāpādai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, abyāpādassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. micchādiṭṭhisuttaṃ

273. … attanā ca micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti, micchādiṭṭhiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, micchādiṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi…pai॰…|

attanā ca sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti, sammādiṭṭhiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti, sammādiṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggaiti| dasamaṃ|

kammapathavaggau sattamau|



(28) 8. rāgapaiyyālaṃ

1. satipaṭṭhānasuttaṃ

274. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sammappadhānasuttaṃ

275. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. iddhipādasuttaṃ

276. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti; vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ  bhāvaiti| rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4-30. pariñ‍ñādisuttāni

277-303. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñāya…pai॰… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya … vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā…pai॰…| tiṃsatimaṃ|

31-510. dausaabhiñ‍ñādisuttāni

304-783. ‘‘dausassa…pai॰… mauhassa… kaudhassa… upanāhassa… makkhassa… paḷāsassa… issāya… macchariyassa… māyāya… sāṭhaiyyassa… thambhassa… sārambhassa… mānassa… atimānassa… madassa… pamādassa abhiñ‍ñāya… pariñ‍ñāya… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya imai cattārau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dasuttarapañ‍casatimaṃ|

rāgapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


catuk‍kanipātapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
